## SH — App shell, navigation, search, toasts, dialogs, Help & Guide (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### SH-01 · P1 · ui — Toast wraps at half the viewport width on phones: tall broken pill over content

- **Devices:** iPhone (all phones); also iPad/Mac for long toasts (wraps at 410/735px)
- **Detail / repro:** Any toast longer than ~25 characters on iPhone wraps into a 201px-wide, 3-line 'pill' (radius pill on a tall box) sitting over the page content above the tab bar. Reproduced with KOS.ui.toast('You already had 44 badge tiers — they are on the Badge Wall.') at 402x874: rect x=100.5 w=201 h=78.5. Same on every width: the toast can never be wider than half the viewport.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/toast-iphone.png; eval rect [100.5,735.5,201,78.5]; css/components.css:389-405 (#toast: position fixed; inset-inline-start:50%; translate(-50%) with shrink-to-fit width)
- **Root cause:** A fixed element with left:50% gets an available width of (viewport - 50%) for shrink-to-fit sizing, so text wraps at 50% of the viewport before the max-inline-size ever applies.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the toast an explicit fit-content width independent of its left offset: e.g. inset-inline:0; margin-inline:auto; inline-size:fit-content; max-inline-size:min(var(--toast-max), calc(100% - var(--sp-32))); transform only on Y. Use a rounded-rect radius (r-16) when it wraps, or keep pill only for one line.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-02 · P1 · bug — Global Backspace = Back fires even when a page handled the key: Backspace on a Money row opens 'Delete this entry?' AND navigates to the previous page underneath

- **Devices:** MacBook; iPad with a keyboard
- **Detail / repro:** js/main.js binds Backspace (outside text fields) to KOS.back() on document and never checks e.defaultPrevented. Money's transaction list uses Delete/Backspace on a focused row to delete it (money.js:610) and calls preventDefault, but the document listener still runs: reproduced on #/money/transactions → focus a row → Backspace: the confirm dialog opens while the route has already changed to #/home behind it; cancelling leaves the user on Home, having lost their place. Any future in-page Backspace handler (ink lasso selection, lists, the editor's rows, Mind's list at mindui.js:154 which is inside a textarea so is safe) collides the same way. Backspace-as-Back is also surprising on its own: a stray Backspace with focus on any button (after clicking a card, closing a menu) leaves the page; browsers removed this shortcut for that reason.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/backspace-money-dialog-over-home.png; eval result {hash:'#/home', dialog:'Delete this entry? QA coffee, −£4.50…'}; js/main.js:20-28; js/modules/money.js:610
- **Root cause:** The shell's history shortcut ignores defaultPrevented and treats any non-text target as free to navigate.
- **Proposed fix:** In main.js return early when e.defaultPrevented, when a dialog is open, or when the target is inside [role=listbox]/[data-keys]; better, drop bare Backspace and keep Alt+←/→ (and ⌘[ / ⌘] on the Mac). Register the listener in the bubble phase after page handlers (it already is) and respect defaultPrevented.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-03 · P1 · ui — Governor ⋯ menu is cut off behind the Stage (only half of its first row shows)

- **Devices:** MacBook, iPad portrait and landscape
- **Detail / repro:** On #/governor, ⋯ in the page header opens a 280x100 (mac) / 280x136 (iPad) popover whose 'HP preview' row is cut in half and whose 'Data: Mine/Empty/Sample' row is completely hidden behind the Stage card below. Same at 1470x832, 1180x820 and 820x1180.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/gov-menu-mac.png, shots/shell/gov-menu-ipad.png, shots/shell/gov-menu-ipadl.png; popover rect mac {x:1165,y:164,w:280,h:100}; css/components.css:35 (.k-pagehead[data-state~=has-art] {position:relative; isolation:isolate}); css/views/governor.css:31 (.k-gv-stage {position:relative; isolation:isolate}) and :65 (.k-gv-tools-pop z-index var(--z-menu)); js/modules/governor-ui.js:420-439 (an inline <details> popover)
- **Root cause:** 34e made painted page headers an isolated stacking context; the z-index of the popover now only counts inside the header, and the Stage that follows (also isolated, later in DOM) paints over it. The Governor menu is a one-off <details> rather than the portalled KOS.ui.menu.
- **Proposed fix:** Use the shared KOS.ui.menu (it portals the panel to <body>, js/core/ui.js ~813) instead of a hand-made <details>; or give the painted page header z-index:var(--z-sticky) so its stacking context sits above the following content. Audit any other in-header inline popover after the 34e painted headers.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-04 · P1 · ui — iPhone bottom tab bar lost its labels: five bare kanji (灯 学 整 蒐 余) with no Home/Study/Productivity/Collection/More text

- **Devices:** iPhone (all <700px, incl. iPad Slide Over)
- **Detail / repro:** On iPhone the tab bar shows only the 28px kanji tiles; the English labels are clipped to a 1x1 rect. Design 15a and layout.css's own phone block intend visible labels ('the tree states' compact-rail rules must not clip the tab labels on a phone'). A user who does not read kanji cannot tell 整 from 蒐; the profile/More sheet is the only place the names appear. Screen readers still get the names (sr-only clip), so this is visual only.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/seed-home-iphone.png, shots/shell/more-iphone.png (bar at y=818-874); eval: label rect {w:1,h:1}, clip rect(0px,0px,0px,0px), #cols has no data-rail and data-tree=none on a phone; css/layout.css:377 (inside @media max-width:1080px: #cols:not([data-rail="full"]) .k-rail-label {position:absolute; clip:rect(0 0 0 0)} — specificity 1,2,0) beats css/layout.css:433 (.k-rail-label {position:static…} — 0,1,0) and :431 only covers data-rail=compact / tree open|closed
- **Root cause:** Regression from 4c29a2f (feat(ipad), 22 Oct): the iPad tier's ID-scoped label-hiding rule also matches at phone width and out-specifies the phone's class-level reset.
- **Proposed fix:** In the ≤700 block add the same reset for the 1080 tier's selector: '#cols:not([data-rail="full"]) .k-rail-label, #cols:not([data-rail="full"]) .k-rail-count { position: static; inline-size:auto; block-size:auto; clip:auto; overflow:visible }' (or scope the 1080 rule with min-width:701px). Add a smoke/visual assertion that a phone tab label has width > 20px.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-05 · P1 · bug — KOS.rerender() throws the page back to the top on every device: it saves/restores #main.scrollTop but #stage is the scroller

- **Devices:** MacBook, iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Every non-navigation redraw (cloud pull, routine auto-tick, achievements touch, Google/Todoist cycle, a goal or money tick, the day review, and 144 KOS.rerender() call sites in 30 files, many after an in-page action) resets the scroll position. Reproduced at 1470x832: #/home scrolled to 600 → KOS.rerender() → 0; #/reminders scrolled to 900 → KOS.rerender() → 0. Browser Back to a long page also lands at the top (reminders 900 → Home → Back → 0). This contradicts the router contract ('a background pull that repaints the page you are on leaves … your scroll position exactly as they were', router.js:25-29, invariant 65) and the standing 'lost position on redraw' rule. Same on iPhone (402x874: #/home 800 → 0, #stage is the scroller there too).
- **Evidence:** eval: {'#/home': ['stage', 600, 0]}; eval reminders {b:[900,0], afterRerender:0, after Back: 0}; scroller probe: #stage overflow-y auto (scrollHeight 2152 / client 772), #main overflow visible; js/core/ui.js:353-356 (var top = main.scrollTop … main.scrollTop = top), js/core/ui.js:1521-1523 (main.innerHTML = ''; main.scrollTop = 0); iPhone eval ['stage',800,0]
- **Root cause:** The scroll container moved from #main to #stage (layout) but ui.js still saves/restores #main.scrollTop, which is always 0.
- **Proposed fix:** Introduce one KOS.ui.scrollRoot() (the #stage on wide layouts, document.scrollingElement on the phone) and use it in rerender (save before, restore after the view drew, and again on the next frame for async content) and in show() (reset to 0 on a real navigation; restore a remembered position per history index on Back/Forward, like the router already keeps args per index). Add a smoke test.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SH-06 · P2 · bug — Phone search chips file Goals under 'Collection' and Reports/Files under a fake 'Pages' section

- **Devices:** iPhone (chips are phone-only)
- **Detail / repro:** Searching 'goal' on iPhone shows chips 'All 11 · Study 5 · Collection 6' where the six 'Collection' results are all Goals. Goals has been a Productivity page since 27a (CLAUDE.md invariant 27). Reports and Files groups have no mapping and fall back to a 'Pages' chip, although neither is a pages result (Reports lives under Home, Files under Archive).
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/search-iphone-q.png; js/modules/hub.js:3592-3593 (SEARCH_SECTION: goals: "Collection"; reports/files absent → "Pages" at :3598)
- **Root cause:** Hard-coded section table not updated when Goals moved to Productivity and when Reports/Files search domains were added.
- **Proposed fix:** goals → Productivity, planner → Collection (Wishlist), reports → Home, files → Archive; derive the section from the same table the rail uses (KOS.sectionLanding / the rail's section ids) so a new domain cannot silently fall to 'Pages'.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-07 · P2 · ux — Global search cannot find app pages or features (typing 'money', 'goals', 'help', 'settings' finds no page)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Search covers records (spec, notes, reminders, assignments, calendar, wishlist, goals, cards, media, reports, files) but no navigation entries. 'money' returns six specification leaves (Code 1.7, 5.3, CS data types…) and nothing that opens Money; the same for 'routines', 'help', 'exams', 'badges', 'connections', 'storage'. For a one-connected-OS app with ~40 pages behind 7 rail sections and the phone's 5 tabs, a page jump is the fastest route. Also missing domains: Exams hub assessments, Routines/habits, Money (payee search, if privacy allows), Help entries, achievements. A Goals result opens the Goals list (KOS.show('goals')), not the goal itself, and prints the raw status ('completed', 'active').
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/search-mac-money.png; shots/shell/search-iphone-q.png; js/core/search.js:291-307 (domain list), :194-199 (goal result opens 'goals', sub = g.status)
- **Root cause:** search.js only has record domains; the navigation map lives in the rail/mobile-shell and is not exposed to search.
- **Proposed fix:** Add a synchronous 'Pages' domain built from the rail/section tables (title + aliases like 'budget'→Money, 'badges'→Achievements, 'help'), ranked first on an exact page-name hit; add assessments and routines domains; open a goal at #/goals/<id> and print a human status.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SH-08 · P2 · bug — Phone '⋯ tools' and section-switch sheets stay open when the window grows past 700px, showing a phone sheet as a desktop modal

- **Devices:** iPad Split View/Slide Over, Mac window resize
- **Detail / repro:** Open #/governor at 402px, tap ⋯ (Status tools), then widen to 1470: the 'Status' sheet stays open centred on the desktop page, holding the moved Live/Full/Off and Mine/Empty/Sample segments, while the desktop header's own ⋯ menu is now empty of them. Happens with iPad Split View / Slide Over resizing and any desktop window resize. syncShell() closes the More and Search overlays when leaving phone width but not activeToolsOverlay or activeSectionOverlay.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/phone-sheet-left-open-at-mac.png; js/modules/mobile-shell.js:584-589 (only activeMoreOverlay/activeSearchOverlay are closed), :516-539 (openTools moves the nodes out of the page)
- **Root cause:** The phone→wide cleanup list in syncShell was not extended when the tools and section sheets were added.
- **Proposed fix:** In syncShell when !phone.matches also close activeToolsOverlay and activeSectionOverlay (their onClose already puts the nodes back).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-09 · P2 · ux — Phone ⋯ tools sheet drops the group labels: Governor's sheet shows bare 'Live Full Off' and 'Mine Empty Sample' with no idea what they control

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Only the [data-phone-more] segment nodes are moved into the sheet; their sibling labels ('HP preview', 'Data') stay behind in the hidden desktop popover. On iPhone the 'Status' sheet is two unlabelled segmented rows. These are also review/test aids (the summary's own label is 'Test controls': HP preview and an Empty/Sample account switch) exposed in the production header of a page the user visits daily.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/gov-menu-iphone.png; js/modules/governor-ui.js:423-430 (labels are spans outside the data-phone-more nodes); js/modules/mobile-shell.js:516-520
- **Root cause:** openTools moves nodes individually and has no notion of a caption.
- **Proposed fix:** Mark the label+segment pair (a wrapper) as data-phone-more, or let openTools read each node's aria-label as a visible caption. Consider moving the test controls to Archive › developer settings or hiding them behind a setting.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-10 · P2 · docs — In-app Help & Guide is missing most features released since mid-October (Money, Goals, Reports, Exams hub, Code, Japanese, Mind, Creatives, Achievements, Connections, the Assistant, the shop/Stage)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** js/modules/help.js SECTIONS (8 sections, 79 entries) was last touched 26 Oct and has NO entry at all for: (1) Money — the six views, budgets and pace, recurring bills/income/subscriptions and trials, pots, CSV, Hide amounts, the Wishlist purchase handoff (0 mentions of 'Money'); (2) Goals — the Goals page, types, repeating goals, exam goals, the Home switch (the only mention is the outdated 'Planner' entry); (3) Reports — week/month/term/year, the story, share card, Kurenai's words; (4) the Exams & Deadlines hub — assessments, scopes, readiness, the planner, Exam Season, Exam Room, Paper library, Companion, predicted papers (Help only has the old 'Exams & Papers' tracker, 'Log every exam… and a reviewed checkbox'); (5) Code (only 'Rider projects and GitHub'; nothing on the desk, lessons, runners, Daily 20, katas, capstones); (6) Japanese; (7) Mind; (8) Creatives; (9) Achievements / the Badge Wall / titles / showcase; (10) the Governor's Seat as the Stage, Edit seat, Gold Shop items and Try on, Wardrobe, Avatar & profile, Follow the system, painted pictures and 'Your own picture' unlocks; (11) Archive › Connections (Google Calendar, Google Tasks, Todoist); (12) the Kurenai Assistant itself (chat, approval cards, scheduled messages, memory, Kurenai's states/Hide); (13) closed-app notifications ('When the app is closed', Send a test), notification filters and quiet hours as settings; (14) writing by hand beyond Archive › Pen and ink (the Handwritten tab, Side by side, Clip to page, answer pads, the Working pane, palette/ruler, Mark with Kurenai, ink sync and clashes); (15) FSRS settings (target retention, exam-day target, the optimiser). The Help nav and its on-page search therefore return nothing for 'money', 'goal', 'badge', 'japanese', 'report'.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/help-mac.png, shots/shell/help-iphone.png; grep counts in js/modules/help.js: Money 0, Mind 0, Creatives 0, Achievement 0, Badge 0, Connections 0, Todoist 0, Japanese 0, Reports 0, Assistant 0; git log -1 js/modules/help.js = 26 Oct 2026
- **Root cause:** Help is a hand-kept array with no test tying it to the set of views, so each release added pages without entries.
- **Proposed fix:** Add sections: Money & Goals, Reports, Exams & Deadlines (replace 'Exams & Papers'), Study spaces (Code, Japanese, Mind, Creatives), Achievements, The Governor's Seat & shop, Assistant, Connections, Notifications (move out of Collection), Writing by hand; give each a 'Open …' relation like the others. Add a smoke check that every rail destination / KOS.views id with a page header has at least one Help entry.
- **Flags:** effort L

### SH-11 · P2 · docs — Help & Guide entries that are now wrong

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Out of date or false: 'Overview' (Home has no 'completion ring' or 'subject cards'; it is Up next/Today/Upcoming/Study hours/report/plan/routines); 'Subjects (rail)' (the rail has no subjects — sections only; subjects are pills in two groups); 'Search (topbar)' ('every spec point and flashcard across all three subjects' — it searches 11 domains and 7+ subjects); 'Back / Forward' ('The ‹ › buttons' — there are none: #nav-back/#nav-fwd do not exist in index.html, main.js:16-19 binds to null; it also omits that bare Backspace navigates back); 'Planner' ('holds Budget Planner and Goals under one secondary tab bar' — Goals is a Productivity page, the Planner is the Wishlist, the budget is Money); 'Notifications' is filed under 'The Collection Matrix' and mentions 'a Budget Planner item'; 'Autosave' ('the AUTOSAVE dot pulses' — no #save-dot exists in index.html); 'XP & level' ('The HUD in the topbar shows avatar, level, gold, HP and XP… click it' — the HUD is the rail foot / More sheet, not the topbar); 'Today's directives' (no such Home list; its link opens Routines); 'Countdowns' ('The rail merges…' — there is no countdown rail); 'Exams & Papers' (a 'reviewed checkbox' tracker; now the Exams hub); 'Sample data' (only calendar SAMPLE events, while the empty account also shows SAMPLE mock/deadline assessments, see empty-home shots). The 'Data & housekeeping' section shows a '·' where every other section has a kanji glyph. docs/GUIDE.md §5 'What is still on the way' still lists Creatives, Mind, the art pass and shop outfits, all released; GUIDE says Money 'stays on your device' although transactions replicate to kos_money.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/help-mac.png ('·' before Data & housekeeping, 'Alt ← / →' shortcut only); js/modules/help.js lines 2-5, 40, 57, 62, 78-79 and the 'Autosave' entry; index.html (no nav-back, nav-fwd, save-dot); js/main.js:15-19; docs/GUIDE.md:158 and :418-431
- **Root cause:** Docs not updated with releases; no test.
- **Proposed fix:** Rewrite those entries against the current app; give 'Data & housekeeping' a glyph; add Backspace (or remove the shortcut, see the Backspace finding) to Useful shortcuts; refresh GUIDE.md §5 and the Money sentence.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SH-12 · P2 · ux — Review chip menu never names the review on iPhone ('Open · ready' / 'Skip review'), and Skip has no undo

- **Devices:** iPhone primarily; all
- **Detail / repro:** On the phone the chip is just the 夜 glyph; tapping it opens a menu whose items are 'Open — ready' and 'Skip review — nothing else changes', with no heading, so the user is not told it is the Evening review (or that 夜 means evening). The 'ready' hint repeats the chip state instead of saying what opening does. On the Mac opening a review is two steps when the chip could open it in one. 'Skip review' finishes the slot immediately (V().finish(slot,'skipped')) with no toast/Undo.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/reviewchip-iphone.png, shots/shell/reviewchip-mac.png; js/modules/dayreview.js:705-709
- **Root cause:** Menu items assume the chip's text label is visible, which the phone header drops.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the menu a heading row ('Evening review · 3 steps') or make the first item 'Open the evening review'; make the hint describe the action; after Skip show a toast with Undo. Consider primary click = open, ▾ = Skip.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-13 · P3 · bug — Nothing redraws the open page when the day changes: header clock says Mon 2 Nov, Home still says Sunday 1 November / Good evening

- **Devices:** all; most visible on the installed iPad app
- **Detail / repro:** Left open across midnight (the user's installed iPad app is), the header clock ticks to the new date but the current view is never redrawn, so Home keeps yesterday's date line, greeting, Today list and daily goal until something else triggers a redraw. Observed at 00:00: header 'Mon 2 Nov', hero 'SUNDAY 1 NOVEMBER · Good evening', while the Week's plan card in the same page (drawn later) already read 'w/c 2 Nov'. A manual KOS.rerender() then showed 'Monday 2 November · Working late'.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/bell-mac.png (clock 00:00 Mon 2 Nov vs hero Sunday 1 November); js/main.js:86-97 (only the clock ticks every 20 s); js/main.js:199-204 (30-min interval refreshes HUD and rail counters but never the view); hub.js:623 reads new Date() only when drawn
- **Root cause:** Day-dependent views read new Date() only at draw time and the shell has no day-rollover redraw.
- **Proposed fix:** In tickClock, remember the date string; when it changes (and on visibilitychange after a day boundary) call KOS.rerender() if no dialog/ink/Focus is busy (the rerender already waits on KOS.inkUI.busy).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-14 · P3 · ui — Help on iPhone: section chips wrap into a ragged left-aligned cloud

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px the eight section chips wrap one or two per line with uneven widths and a large empty right side (e.g. 'The Behavioural Governor 7' alone on a row, 'Data & housekeeping' alone), pushing the article below the fold. Other pages use a horizontal KOS.ui.scroller for this.
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/help-iphone.png
- **Root cause:** help.css nav reflows as flex-wrap at phone width.
- **Proposed fix:** Use the shared scroller (one line, horizontally scrollable, declared overflow) or the phone title bar's section switch for the eight sections.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-15 · P3 · a11y — document.title never changes: every page, history entry and app-switcher card reads "Kurenai OS — Study Atelier"

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Only Focus writes document.title (the countdown). Navigating to Money, Goals, a topic, Help etc. leaves the same title, so the browser history menu, Back long-press list, Safari tabs, the Mac window title and screen-reader page announcements cannot tell pages apart (WCAG 2.4.2 Page Titled). The brand elsewhere is "Kurenai".
- **Evidence:** eval after navigating to #/calendar: title "Kurenai OS — Study Atelier"; grep document.title → only js/modules/focus.js:1072,1494
- **Root cause:** The router owns the URL but not the title.
- **Proposed fix:** In router.js after a successful show, set document.title = "<page h1 or section label> · Kurenai" (Focus keeps prefixing its countdown).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-16 · P3 · ux — Unknown or stale deep links fall back silently (to Home, or to a list) with no message

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** #/nonsense/xyz and #/topic/cs/9.9.9 replace to #/home with nothing said; #/goals/doesnotexist shows the Goals list with the bad id still in the URL. A link from an old notification, report or a deleted goal therefore looks like the app ignored the tap.
- **Evidence:** eval: invalid → #/home, badTopic → #/home, badGoal → stays #/goals/doesnotexist showing the list
- **Root cause:** invariant 65 replaces invalid routes with Home but nothing tells the user.
- **Proposed fix:** When the router replaces an invalid route, toast "That page is no longer here" (and for a missing record, "That goal was deleted") and normalise the URL.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-17 · P3 · a11y — Collapsed rail (Mac, iPad portrait) is kanji-only with no tooltip and drops the due-count badge

- **Devices:** MacBook (collapsed), iPad portrait
- **Detail / repro:** Collapsing the rail on the Mac (and the default iPad-portrait rail) shows 灯 学 整 蒐 守 助 蔵 with no title/tooltip on hover, so a mouse user cannot discover what 整 or 蔵 is without expanding; the Study due count (373) is clipped away with the label, so the at-a-glance signal disappears. The rail toggle itself is a 24px circle overlapping the rail edge (24x24 also on the touch iPad by design, below the 44px coarse target).
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/rail-collapsed-mac.png; shots/shell/empty-home-ipad.png; eval: rail items have no title attribute; toggle rect 24x24 at (59,76); css/layout.css:213-235, :377
- **Root cause:** The compact rail hides label and count together via the same sr-only clip.
- **Proposed fix:** Give each rail item title=<label> (or a CSS tooltip on :hover/:focus-visible when compact); show the count as a small dot/number on the tile when compact; give the toggle a 44px invisible hit area on coarse pointers (padding + negative margin) while drawing the 24px circle.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-18 · P3 · content — Search result sub-lines print raw data: 'Due 2026-10-28', goal status 'completed'/'active'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Reminder results read 'Reminder 2 · Due 2026-10-28' (ISO) while every other surface says 'Wed 28 Oct'; goal results print the stored status word in lower case. Overdue reminders are not marked as overdue in results.
- **Evidence:** eval of the search listbox for 'reminder 2' → 'Reminder 2Due 2026-10-28'; shots/shell/search-iphone-q.png (Goal 2 · completed); js/core/search.js:113 ("Due " + r.due), :196 (sub: g.status)
- **Root cause:** search.js builds its own sub-lines instead of the owners' formatters.
- **Proposed fix:** Format with the shared date helper used by Reminders ('Wed 28 Oct', 'Overdue · 28 Oct'), and map goal status to 'Reached'/'In progress'/'Missed'.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SH-19 · P3 · ui — Header controls on Graphite: the Assistant emblem is near-invisible and the four header controls are four different heights

- **Devices:** MacBook, iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On the default dark theme the Assistant trigger's emblem (whispering-bloom-emblem-production.png, a dark mark with a little red) sits on a dark 38px circle and reads as a smudge; on Dawn it is fine. Header controls are 40px (review chip), 38px (bell, Assistant), 32px (sync dot) and 34px brand at 1470px, so their centres and outlines don't line up; on iPhone the review chip is 44x40 beside 44x44 buttons (below the 44px coarse target).
- **Evidence:** shots/shell/hdr-mac-right-crop.png (dark), shots/shell/dawn-bell-mac.png (Dawn); eval: chip 203x40, bell 38x38, assistant 38x38, syncdot 32x32 (mac); phone chip 44x40; index.html assistant-trigger img
- **Root cause:** Raster emblem has no dark-theme variant; header controls size themselves from different tokens.
- **Proposed fix:** Use a light/outlined variant of the emblem (or the bloom brand mark) on dark themes, or tint via a mask with --text; size every header control from one token (--icon-btn, 44 on coarse) and give the chip min-block-size var(--hit-coarse) on touch.
- **Flags:** effort S, needs art (Gemini sheet)

## HR — Home and Reports (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### HR-01 · P1 · ux — Mind and Creatives are pushed off-screen in Home's Continue row (fixed subject order, 4 visible, no scroll affordance)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad landscape (4 across), ipad portrait (2 across)
- **Detail / repro:** Continue renders one desk per started subject in the fixed KOS.subjects order (compsci, maths, it, code, ja, mind, creatives) and then Collection, in a grid with grid-auto-columns = a quarter of the width and overflow-x:auto. With Code and Japanese started (the user's case), Mind is the 6th and Creatives the 7th column: at 1470x832 they sit at x=1766 and x=2072 inside a 1210px-wide row (scrollWidth 2434) and Collection at 2378. macOS hides the thin scrollbar, there are no arrows, no fade, no count, so the desks never appear. A second, smaller cause: KOS.mind.started()/KOS.creatives.started() only become true once a lesson page has recorded a read (state.mind.lessons / state.creatives.lessons|works); a Daily 15, Journal entries or Positions alone never put Mind on Home. Reproduced: fresh seeded account, opened Mind F1.1, Creatives A1.1, Code 1.1, Japanese H1 -> desks order compsci:236, maths:542, it:848, code:1154, ja:1460, mind:1766, creatives:2072, coll:2378.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-mac-continue.png; eval of .k-desks: clientWidth 1210, scrollWidth 2434; js/modules/hub.js:1558-1563 (filter + fixed SUBJECTS order); css/views/home.css:183 (grid-auto-columns quarter width, overflow-x:auto); js/core/mind.js:430, js/core/creatives.js:255 (started())
- **Root cause:** Fixed registry order plus a 4-column horizontal scroller with no affordance; started() reads only lesson records.
- **Proposed fix:** Order the study desks by recency (last studied time from the ledger / ui.lastRef timestamps) so what you are doing is always in the first four; make the row wrap into a second line on desktop/iPad (auto-fill minmax columns) or add visible prev/next buttons and an edge fade with a 'N more' count; let started() also count Mind's Journal/positions/cards and Creatives' works.
- **Flags:** effort M

### HR-02 · P1 · data — Weekly/monthly reports saved before the history arrives are frozen as 'A quiet week for study' forever

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** ensureReports() runs at boot and saves every backfilled week since 1 September. The skip rule for an empty rebuilt week is hasContent(), which counts f.plan and f.tasks; the shipped Pacing seed gives every fresh install plan facts, and the LATEST week is saved whatever it holds. So the first boot on an empty device (a new iPad/iPhone before sign-in finishes its first pull, or before a backup is restored with importFull, which does not touch kurenai-os-reports) saves weeks with mins:0 and the month 'October 2026 · a quiet month for study'. Reports are never recomputed (getReport hit -> skip), so after the history arrives Home's Reports card still says 'A quiet week for study · 0m studied' while Study hours on the same screen shows 9.2 h and the ledger has focus sessions on 20, 21, 22, 23 and 25 Oct. Reproduced: reset -> first boot -> seed: week:2026-10-19 and week:2026-10-12 both have facts.study.mins 0, createdAt = the reset boot, while weeks saved during the seed have real minutes.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-mac-all-desks.png (Week in review card 0m beside Study hours 9.2); eval KOS.reportStore.cached(): week:2026-10-19 {mins:0,sessions:0} created 1793576817945; ledger has type focus on 2026-10-20..25; js/core/reportstore.js:228-231 (hasContent counts plan/tasks), :275-279 (latest week always saved, a saved report is never rebuilt)
- **Root cause:** Save-once reports are created from an incomplete ledger; hasContent treats seeded Pacing rows as activity.
- **Proposed fix:** Do not let plan/tasks alone make a rebuilt week 'content'; hold the first ensureReports until the first cloud pull has completed (or until KOS.cloud says signed-out), and after importFull / first cloud link re-run the backfill with a 'replace rebuilt reports whose facts are empty or older than the newest ledger entry in their period' rule (rebuilt reports were never shown as final anyway).
- **Flags:** effort M

### HR-03 · P1 · ui — iPad portrait Home has no Daily goal, no Today's three, no Recovery list and no Start focus in the hero

- **Devices:** ipad portrait, iPad Split View, any 701-1080 window
- **Detail / repro:** css/views/home.css hides .k-home-goal entirely in the 1080px tier and only shows it again under 700px. Between 701 and 1080 (the iPad Air 11" in portrait, 820x1180, and Split View) the hero loses its whole right column: the directive list, the Morning Review's Today's three (roadmap 5.10, frame 33t) with its ▶ Start, and in Critical HP the Recovery checklist and 'Open Recovery →' (invariant 2's recovery route). Measured: .k-home-goal display:none, rect 0x0 at 820 wide; the hero shows only ring, greeting and level line.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-ipad.png (hero without Daily goal); eval at --size ipad: goal display none; css/views/home.css:221-223 (@media (max-width:1080px){.k-home-goal{display:none}}), :244 (re-shown only under 700)
- **Root cause:** A display:none for the middle tier that predates Today's three and Recovery being placed in the same slot.
- **Proposed fix:** In the 701-1080 tier put the goal block under the greeting as a full-width row (as the phone tier does with grid-column 1/-1) instead of hiding it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-04 · P1 · integration — Home does not answer 'what next' for Code, Japanese, Mind, Creatives or Money

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Home's render reads KOS.srs.dueCards (flashcards only), calendar, assignments, reminders, pacing, goals, routines and the media stats, but never KOS.code.planDay (the Daily 20), KOS.ja.due/planDay, KOS.mind.planDay (the Daily 15), KOS.creatives.planDay (the Daily Creative) or KOS.money notices (bill in two days, trial ending, budget). KOS.today.build, which invariant 149 makes the one source for the reviews and Home's three, does not read them either. So with Code started, the Daily 20 is 'Your first program' and Home says nothing; Japanese facets, Code retrieval cards and Mind cards due are not in 'Review N due cards'; a bill due tomorrow appears only in the bell. Money appears nowhere on Home (innerText has no money/bill/budget). The study-space desks in Continue are the only trace, and they are scrolled off-screen (see the Continue finding).
- **Evidence:** eval: KOS.code.planDay() -> lesson 1.1 'Your first program'; KOS.mind.planDay() and KOS.creatives.planDay() return day plans; Home innerText matches no money/bill/budget; js/modules/hub.js:1041-1730 (reads list); js/core/today.js (no ja/code/mind/creatives/money reads)
- **Root cause:** Home and the day model predate the study spaces and Money; each space built its own daily plan without registering it with the day model.
- **Proposed fix:** Give KOS.today.build a 'spaces' read (each space's planDay summary: Code Daily 20, Japanese reviews + next lesson, Mind Daily 15, Creatives Daily Creative) and a money read (KOS.money.notices(today,{hide}) and safe-to-spend), then: count all due cards in the Due card (split by space), add the spaces' daily items as Up next rungs / Today rows, and a compact Money line (next bill, left this month) on Home.
- **Flags:** effort L, needs a design prompt

### HR-05 · P1 · data — Home 'Study hours' and Reports 'Studied' measure different things (12h14m vs 2h32m for the same week)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Home's Study hours card (hoursOn / hoursThisWeek / sessionsThisWeek) sums dur of EVERY non-media ledger entry - notes, reading, quiz, exam, tracker, flashcards, and todo ticks (invariant 4: activity, never study) - while KOS.report's study() counts only type 'focus' minutes as 'Studied' (its days count uses isStudy, so 'Studied 2h 32m · 5 sessions · 7 days'). For week 28 Sep-4 Oct the ledger gives 734 min by Home's rule and 152 min by the report's. Home's card links 'Weekly reports ›' straight under its figure, so the two numbers contradict each other one click apart; the Study overview's '5 sessions · 7 days' is self-contradictory too. Invariant 26d says study statistics are derived once.
- **Evidence:** eval over state.sessions 2026-09-28..10-04: non-media dur 734 min, focus dur 152 min; js/modules/hub.js:917-921 (hoursOn), :636-649; js/core/report.js:185-200, :254; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-report-0928-study.png
- **Root cause:** Two independent minute definitions (hub.js vs report.js).
- **Proposed fix:** Define study minutes once (e.g. KOS.sessions.studyMins(range), using isStudy + parts) and have Home, Reports, Goals' study-hours type and the Daily goal all call it; exclude todo/external and media; if non-Focus activities count, count them in Reports too (or say 'Focus time' on both).
- **Flags:** effort M

### HR-06 · P2 · ux — Hero 'Daily goal' counts 5 items but shows only 3, with no way to reach the other 2

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The goal head prints autos.filter(checked).length + ' / ' + autos.length (0 / 5) but only autos.slice(0,3) are drawn and there is no '+2 more' link; the two hidden directives cannot be seen or sealed from Home. 'Start focus →' under the list is generic (opens Focus blank) while Today's three variant starts the first item; the hand-tick lets 'Clear 308 due flashcards' be sealed without doing anything.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-mac-all-desks.png (DAILY GOAL 0 / 5, three rows); js/modules/hub.js:1153-1173
- **Root cause:** slice(0,3) for display but the full length in the counter.
- **Proposed fix:** Show '+N more' linking to the full list (Routines Today / Reminders), or count only what is shown; make Start focus start the first unchecked directive like Today's three does; let auto items tick themselves from evidence rather than by hand.
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-07 · P2 · ui — Hero's season chip ('Autumn · by season') covers the Daily goal count

- **Devices:** ipad landscape (always), mac (on hover), iphone (crowds date)
- **Detail / repro:** The art chip is absolutely positioned top-right of the hero (inset 14px/16px, z-index 2) and is always visible on touch devices (hover:none) and on hover with a mouse. It lands on the goal head: at 1180x820 chip [980..1140, 94..126] over the head [878..1128, 104..122], hiding '0 / 5'; on the Mac the same overlap appears whenever the pointer is over the hero (chip [1270..1430] vs head [1168..1418]). On the iPhone the chip sits 3px above the date kicker.

### HR-08 · P2 · data — Report Focus heat map drops every block between 23:00 and 07:00 (late-night study vanishes; contradicts the Night Owl line)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.report builds the heat grid only for hours 07-22 (HEAT_FROM 7, HEAT_TO 23) and silently discards minutes outside it, while the persona counts 21:00-04:00 as 'late'. Week of 28 Sep: Focus card says '5 blocks · 2h 32m', facts.focus.hours has 55 min at 02:00 and 40 at 06:00, but the heat grid holds only two cells (33 and 24 min) - three of five blocks are invisible, under a 'Night Owl · 36% of your Focus came after 21:00' line that has nothing lit after 21:00. The user studies past midnight (Home greets 'Working late' at 00:05).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-report-week-0928.png; eval facts.focus.heat vs hours/list; js/core/report.js:33, :321, :336, :369
- **Root cause:** Fixed 07-23 grid window with no overflow bucket.
- **Proposed fix:** Make the grid span the hours that actually hold focus (min/max of the period's hours, at least 07-23), or fold the night into a labelled 23-07 column; word the persona with the range it measured ('after 21:00 or before 04:00').
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-09 · P2 · bug — Share/Export dialog stays open across navigation (Back, swipe-back, hash change) and shows the previous report over another page

- **Devices:** all, especially iPhone/iPad swipe-back
- **Detail / repro:** Open Share on Reports › Week of 28 Sep, then go Back or change the hash (#/reports, #/home): the router re-renders #main under the modal but never closes it. The share sheet for 28 Sep stays over the 19-25 Oct report and even over Home, scroll-locked. On the iPhone/iPad Home Screen app the OS back gesture triggers exactly this. Reproduced by eval: dialog open count 2 before, 2 after location.hash='#/reports', 2 after history.back().
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/reports-iphone.png (28 Sep share sheet over the 19-25 Oct report); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/home-dawn-mac.png (same sheet over Home); js/core/router.js:284-306 (onPop renders without closing dialogs); js/core/ui.js:155 openDialog
- **Root cause:** openDialog and router are not coordinated; navigation does not dismiss modals.
- **Proposed fix:** In router renderRoute/onPop, close every open KOS.ui dialog (call its close, which restores focus) before rendering; or have openDialog push a history entry so Back closes the dialog first (the iOS sheet convention).
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-10 · P2 · ui — Home's middle rows leave large empty areas inside equal-height cards (Today, Due today, Study hours, Week in review)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** The 3-column rows stretch every card to the tallest one. Measured at 1470x832 (seeded): Today with 'Nothing on today' ends ~200px above its card bottom (card 306px tall); Due today's last row ends at y=661 and its Start reviewing button sits at y=729 (68px of nothing); Study hours on a Monday morning or an empty week draws a ~140px empty plot under the goal line; the Week in review card for a quiet week draws an empty 7-bar chart plus '0m studied' (~120px). The empty account shows the same (Today and Study hours). This breaks the standing 'no dead space' rule.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-mac-all-desks.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/home-dawn-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/empty-home-mac.png; eval: .k-upnext 326px tall, last row bottom 661, button top 729
- **Root cause:** Grid rows stretch cards; empty states and empty charts keep their full height.
- **Proposed fix:** Let Today's empty state collapse and give the freed height to Upcoming/Goals (align-items:start plus masonry like Reports' pack), or fill it with the next timed item tomorrow / the next lesson day; pin Due today's button directly under its rows; hide the hours plot (keep figures) when the week has no minutes and the report chart when facts.study.chartDays is false.
- **Flags:** effort M

### HR-11 · P2 · ux — Upcoming caps at 3 rows: two overdue assignments hide the mock exam 4 days away, with no 'N more' and a link to Assignments only

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Upcoming card draws up to two overdue assignments and then counts.slice(0, 3 - urgent) countdowns. Seeded: NEA (4 days overdue), Assignment 3 (2 overdue), Assignment 8 (in 3 days) - while 'AQA 7517 Paper 2 ... mock - 4d away' is in the hero's daily goal but absent from Upcoming. There is no '+N more' line, and the only way out is 'Tracker →', which opens Assignments (not the Exams hub or the Calendar's countdowns, where exams, start-by dates and plan milestones live). The '4'/'2'/'3' discs carry the day counts with no unit beside them (the sub-line repeats it).

- **Root cause:** Fixed 3-row budget with overdue assignments taking precedence over every other kind.

### HR-12 · P3 · a11y — Home Goals list: the ring's number (days left) has no unit and is hidden from assistive tech

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In Upcoming ▸ Goals each goal shows a ring with a bare number (39) — it is days until the period ends, while the ring's fill is percent done. Nothing says 'days', and the ring is aria-hidden so screen-reader users lose the days-left figure entirely; 'Too early to call' chips are low contrast on the card.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-goals-switch.png; js/modules/hub.js:1413-1415
- **Root cause:** Number-only badge reused from the Upcoming countdown style.
- **Proposed fix:** Print '39d' (or 'days' under the figure) and include it in the row's accessible text (e.g. '39 days left').
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-13 · P3 · content — Focus overview headline and persona contradict ('most of them before noon' beside 'Night Owl'), and blocks outside 07-23 are drawn at the 07 edge

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** reportwords.js picks ', most of them before noon' when share(4,12) >= 0.5 while report.js picks Night Owl first when share(21..4) >= 0.35; week 28 Sep satisfies both (64% 04-12, 36% 21-04), so the page says '5 Focus blocks, most of them before noon.' next to 'Night Owl - 36% of your Focus came after 21:00'. The 'Every Focus block' ribbon clamps a 02:28 Sunday block and a 06:20 Friday block to the 07 gridline ('Longest session Fri 06:20' in Study is drawn at 07:00).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-report-0928-focus.png; js/core/reportwords.js:192; js/core/report.js:366-371
- **Root cause:** Two independent time-of-day classifiers with different windows.
- **Proposed fix:** Derive the headline clause from the persona (or order the tests the same way) and widen the ribbon axis to the period's real first/last hour.
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-14 · P3 · a11y — Story player top controls: speed and close are below the 32px hit size and not the same shape as Pause

- **Devices:** mac, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** On the Mac the story header's Speed '1×' button is 34x25 (a flat pill), Pause and Close 34x34; on fine pointers this is under the 32px minimum height for Speed and the three adjacent controls read as three sizes. The large 週 outline is drawn under the body text, crossing 'Collection, from Monday to Sunday.' (Mac and iPhone), and the chips '5 sessions' and '5 Focus blocks' repeat one number.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/story-1.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/story-iphone.png; eval button rects Speed 34x25, Pause 34x34, Close 34x34
- **Root cause:** Per-control sizing in reportstory CSS.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the three header controls one size (36-44 circles), keep the decorative glyph behind a scrim or lower in the frame below the text block, and drop one of the duplicate chips.
- **Flags:** effort S

### HR-15 · P3 · ui — Week's plan card header wraps 'Pacing →' onto its own line and truncates every row title

- **Devices:** mac, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** In the 415px column the header holds title, '23 behind', '0 of 13 · w/c 2 Nov' and an 84px segment meter, so the Pacing link drops to a second line at the left (1055,858) unlike every other card whose link is right-aligned on the title row. Each row loses ~45% of its width to the subject tag and the 'N weeks behind' pill: 'Programming constructs: s…', 'Arithmetic, relational & Boo…'; on the iPhone 'Programming constr…'. The footer then repeats three class lessons in a long run-on line.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-mac-continue-scrolled.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/home-reports/seed-home-iphone.png; eval .k-plan-head children rects
- **Root cause:** Header packs four facts plus a link into one flex row with wrap.
- **Proposed fix:** Move the count/meter under the title (sub-line) and keep the link on the title row; put 'N weeks behind' as a single header fact when all rows share it, freeing the row width; let titles wrap to two lines.
- **Flags:** effort S

## AG — Achievements / Badge Wall and the Governor's Seat (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### AG-01 · P1 · bug — The Seat's ⋯ menu is cut off by the stage: its Data row (Mine / Empty / Sample) is hidden and unclickable

- **Devices:** mac, ipad (landscape and portrait)
- **Detail / repro:** Opening ⋯ on #/governor (Status) shows a 280 x 100 popover at y=164-264; the stage starts at y=202 and paints over everything below it. Only the top of the 'HP preview' row shows; the 'Data' row (Mine / Empty / Sample) is entirely behind the hero. elementFromPoint across the popover: rows y>=204 hit .k-gv-stage (7/7 samples), rows y<=192 hit the popover. The controls cannot be reached by pointer.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/gov_menu_crop.png (2x: HP preview row sliced by the stage edge, Data row missing), gov_menu_dpr2.png; eval occlusion rows 204-252 '7/7 outside'; css/components.css:35 (.k-pagehead[data-state~=has-art] {position:relative; isolation:isolate}), css/views/governor.css:31 (.k-gv-stage isolation:isolate), :65 (.k-gv-tools-pop z-index var(--z-menu) trapped inside the header's stacking context)
- **Root cause:** The header's isolation:isolate creates a stacking context with z-index auto, so the popover's z-index only competes inside the header; the later, isolated stage paints on top
- **Proposed fix:** Give the art page header a z-index above the page body (e.g. .k-pagehead[data-state~=has-art] { z-index: var(--z-raised) }) or, better, build the ⋯ menu with KOS.ui.menu so the popover is portalled to the top layer like the bell. Also raise the 32px ⋯ button to 44px on coarse pointers (iPad).
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-02 · P1 · ui — iPhone Seat: showcase medals draw at 80px over each other and the XP bar, and the empty slots are 17 x 44 ovals

- **Devices:** iphone (402), anything under 700
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px the arc becomes a flex row of 44px slots. (1) Filled slots carry the medal with an inline --m:80px (governor-ui.js passes px:80), which beats the phone rule `.k-gv-arc-slot .k-ach-medal { --m: var(--ach-medal-sm) }`, so three picked medals render 80 x 80 at x=32/80/128, overlapping one another and rising over the XP bar. (2) The empty .k-gv-arc-note keeps flex-basis:100% while the row does not wrap (the wrap rule only applies when the note has content), so it takes 131px and the empty slots shrink to 17 x 44 (ovals); before the list() call returns, all six are ovals.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/iphone_gov.png (six ovals), iphone_gov_ovals_crop.png (2x: 80px medals overlapping each other and the XP bar, two oval empties); eval: medals [32,580,80,80,'80px'], [80,580,80,80], [128,580,80,80]; slots [44,44]x3 + [17,44]x3; note width 131 while empty; js/modules/governor-ui.js:363-366, css/views/governor.css:518-523
- **Root cause:** Inline custom property beats the phone stylesheet; empty flex-basis:100% note squeezes the shrinkable slots
- **Proposed fix:** Set the medal size through a class/attribute (data-size) or `--m` on the slot and read it with var() fallback, or override with `.k-gv-arc-slot .k-ach-medal { --m: var(--ach-medal-sm) !important }` at <=700; give slots flex: 0 0 auto (no shrink) and hide the empty note (`.k-gv-arc-note:empty { display:none }`).
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-03 · P2 · bug — Wall tabs and set deep links are not in the URL: #/achievements/<tab> and #/achievements/set/<id> open Sets

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** router.js has no ENCODE/DECODE entry for the achievements view, so KOS.show("achievements","titles") leaves the hash at #/achievements and a typed/bookmarked #/achievements/recent, #/achievements/showcase, #/achievements/titles or #/achievements/set/focus decodes to no arg and draws Sets. Reloading on Titles returns to Sets; a badge link shared or opened after reload loses its set dialog. CLAUDE.md documents these routes (#/achievements[/sets|recent|showcase|titles|set/<id>]).
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/empty_achievements_recent.png, empty_achievements_titles.png (both show Sets selected); eval: KOS.show("achievements","titles") -> location.hash "#/achievements", data-page "titles"; js/core/router.js:45-104 (ENCODE) and 117-150 (DECODE) have no achievements entry
- **Root cause:** Route table in js/core/router.js omits the achievements view
- **Proposed fix:** Add achievements: ENCODE a=>typeof a==="string"&&a&&a!=="sets"?a.split("/"):null and DECODE p=>p.length?p.join("/"):null, matching the view's own split in js/modules/achievements.js:112.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-04 · P2 · ui — Progress ping overflows its card when the badge name is long

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** A ping for a long badge name (e.g. the unit badge 'Fundamentals of functional programming', 1 / 7 topics) draws the name, the count and the bar past the right edge of the 340px card: the card ends at x=568 while the text runs to ~656. The name never ellipsises although its CSS asks for it.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_governor.png and ping_long_name.png (card 228-568, '1 / 7 topics' and the bar outside it); css/views/achievements.css:275-277
- **Root cause:** .k-ach-ping-txt is display:grid with an implicit auto column sized to the nowrap row's min-content, so the flex row cannot shrink
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-ach-ping-txt grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) (a grid's auto track takes the nowrap row's max-content width, so the b's min-inline-size:0/ellipsis never applies).
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-05 · P2 · bug — A ping shows on top of the unlock overlay (and above any dialog) when a tier and a ping arrive in the same sweep

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Invariant 163 says the ping never shows while a dialog is open. In boot()'s onChange, celebrate(r.earned) only queues the overlay (drain() waits for the async A().list), then r.pings.forEach(ping) runs immediately; quiet() sees no dialog yet, so the ping appears. It sits at z-index var(--z-toast) (130) above the overlay scrim (120), at the top-left over the dimmed page while the unlock overlay is up.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_governor.png (ping at top left over the 'Regular III' unlock overlay); eval: .k-ach-pings z-index 130 vs .k-ach-unlock-overlay 120; js/modules/achievements.js:760-765, 684-689
- **Root cause:** Overlay opening is async while ping is sync; quiet() only checks dialogs already in the DOM
- **Proposed fix:** In onChange, skip pings when r.earned.length (the overlay is about to open), or have ping() also check a module-level `showing`/queue.length; drainPing should re-check quiet() before each ping.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-06 · P2 · ui — The current-badge ring in an opened set's strip is an oval, off-centre on its medal

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In a set dialog (e.g. Focus) the strip of badge chips marks the badge on show with a box-shadow ring on the button. The button measures 36 x 42.3 px, so the ring is a tall oval and the 32px medal sits in its top part, not its centre.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_set_focus.png, set_chip_oval_crop.png (2x zoom); eval: .k-ach-dlg-chip 36 x 42.3, inner .k-ach-medal 32 x 32; css/views/achievements.css:229-230
- **Root cause:** The chip button wraps an inline span medal, so its height includes line-height/descender space; border-radius:50% on a non-square box gives an ellipse
- **Proposed fix:** Make .k-ach-dlg-chip display:inline-grid; place-items:center; aspect-ratio:1; line-height:0 (the inline <span> medal inside the button adds a line box's descent). Raise it to 44px on coarse pointers (invariant 69) and draw the ring as a circle round the medal.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-07 · P2 · data — 'Owned' means two different things: the Wall header and the Seat say 8 badges while the set panels and the Showcase show 21

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.achievements.counts().owned counts only badges with EVERY tier earned (8 on the seeded account), and the Wall toolbar ('Owned 8 / 254'), the Seat's glass panel ('THE WALL 8 / 254 badges') and the Governor card print it. The set panels count a badge as owned at its first tier (Time with KurenaiOS 4/6, Focus 3/6, Streaks 1/3, Study 2/11, Goals and Money 2/6, The Collection 7/11, Kurenai and the Governor 2/7 = 21), the Showcase says 'Earned 21 badges'. The same page contradicts itself; a user who earned In the zone I sees it counted in Focus 3/6 but not in Owned.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_ach.png ('Owned 8 / 254' over panels 4/6, 3/6, 1/3), seed_showcase2.png ('Earned 21 badges'); eval counts() {owned:8,tiers:44}, views with tier>0: 21, panel aria-labels sum 21; js/core/achievements.js:343-351 vs js/modules/achievements.js:220
- **Root cause:** counts().owned = fully complete badges; panel() owned = tier > 0; no shared definition
- **Proposed fix:** Pick one meaning. Either print 'Earned 21 / 254 · 8 complete' everywhere (counts() returning earned and complete), or make the panels count complete badges; and label the Seat 'Tiers earned 44 / 417' consistently beside it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-08 · P2 · perf — Every Wall tab (and the Seat's showcase arc) waits 1.6-6 s on 'Reading your records…' because list() re-walks the whole vault each time

- **Devices:** all (worse on iPad/iPhone)
- **Detail / repro:** Each Wall draw (Sets, Recent, Showcase, Titles, every tab switch since each is a KOS.show) calls KOS.achievements.list(), which makes a fresh context and re-measures all 254 badges; the Collection badges open an unbounded IndexedDB cursor over every vault entry (1,880 rows on the seeded account) and tally them. Timed on the seeded account: list() 1.6-4.7 s; per set sequentially: collection 6.5 s, exams 1.8 s, study 1.4 s, mind 1.3 s. Showcase still read 'Reading your records…' after 1.5 s. It also conflicts with invariant 8 (never read an unbounded collection).
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_showcase.png (blank 'Reading your records…' after 1.5 s); eval list() 4744 ms then 1623 ms; per-set timings above; js/core/achcatalogue.js:70-110 (openCursor over entries), js/core/achievements.js:329-341 (fresh makeCtx per list)
- **Root cause:** Achievements are measured on read with no memo across calls; the Collection reader is a full-table cursor
- **Proposed fix:** Cache the last evaluation's views (evaluate() already measures everything at boot and every 30 min and on owner touches) and draw the Wall from that cache immediately, refreshing in the background; for the vault, keep running tallies keyed on the vault's change notes or read counts through indexes (status/module) instead of a full cursor.
- **Flags:** effort M

### AG-09 · P2 · a11y — Edit seat's showcase picker: 21 badge buttons with no name, visible or accessible

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In #/governor/edit > Showcase, the 'Earned' grid draws each earned badge as a bare medal button (showcasePicker with small:true drops the name span and the medal is aria-hidden). None of the 21 buttons has an accessible name, a title or visible text: a screen reader hears 'toggle button, not pressed' 21 times, and a sighted user must recognise badges by their art (several share a palette) with no tooltip.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_edit.png; eval: 21 [data-ui~=ach.pick-item] buttons, 0 with aria-label/text/title; js/modules/achievements.js:397-401
- **Root cause:** The small variant removes the only text and the medal is decorative
- **Proposed fix:** Always set aria-label=badgeName(v) (and title) on .k-ach-pick; in the small variant add a tooltip or a name under the medal.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-10 · P2 · bug — Edit seat's '＋ Upload an image' uploads a new banner without the 'Your own picture' unlock

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Invariant 2 / roadmap 5.14: uploading a picture of your own is a gold unlock (KOS.art.canUpload). The Avatar tab honours it (bannerCropper allowUpload:false hides upload), but #/governor/edit > Background always shows '＋ Upload an image'; it calls KOS.governor.setCustomBanner(file), which opens the same cropper with the NEW file already as its source, and 'Save image' stores it. Reproduced with canUpload('banner') === false and owns('upload-all') === false: governor.bannerImg went from 19 to 52,142 chars, banner 'custom'. The cropper even says 'Uploading a new picture is an unlock in the Gold Shop' while saving the upload.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/edit_Background.png (upload tile, no lock), upload_bypass_cropper.png (cropper with the new picture, Save image enabled); eval before/after above; js/modules/governor-ui.js:745-758, js/core/governor.js:917-935
- **Root cause:** The unlock is enforced only by hiding the cropper's own upload button; the seat editor's file input feeds the cropper a new source directly
- **Proposed fix:** In paintBg, when !KOS.art.canUpload('banner') draw the tile locked ('Your own picture · Gold Shop →', linking to the shop item) and make setCustomBanner refuse (done(new Error(...))) unless canUpload; keep picking an existing bannerImg allowed.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-11 · P2 · a11y — Study cadence heatmap labels shrink to ~6px on iPhone (10px on the Mac)

- **Devices:** iphone, mac (slightly under)
- **Detail / repro:** The Seat's Study cadence chart is a 545-unit-wide SVG (KOS.charts.heatmap, cell 15, gap 4) with 11-unit month and weekday labels, scaled to its box. On the iPhone it renders at 298px (scale 0.55), so 'May', 'Mon', 'Wed', 'Fri' are ~6px; on the Mac 509px (10.3px); the cells shrink to ~8px. Invariant 63 sets an 11px label floor for charts.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/iphone_gov_y700.png (cadence labels unreadable); eval effective label size: iphone 6.0px, mac 10.3px, ipad 12.5px; js/modules/governor-ui.js:609-611, js/core/charts.js:339
- **Root cause:** Fixed 26-week viewBox scaled to fit the container instead of choosing the week count from the width
- **Proposed fix:** On narrow widths show fewer weeks (e.g. 13 at <700, so the chart is drawn at ~1:1) or let the heatmap scroll horizontally inside a declared KOS.ui.scroller, keeping text at true 11px+.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-12 · P3 · ui — A four-tier badge's tiers wrap 3 + 1 in the set dialog, leaving the fourth alone on a second row

- **Devices:** mac, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** The set dialog lays tiers with flex-wrap; for the 14 four-tier badges (e.g. Focus > In the zone: 10/100/500/1,000 sessions) the four items need 613px but the stage is 600px, so tier IV drops alone under the other three, with empty space either side and a 655px-tall dialog.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_set_focus.png; eval .k-ach-dlg-tiers 600 x 379, items at y=228,228,228,422; css/views/achievements.css:218
- **Root cause:** flex-wrap with content-sized tier captions instead of a column count chosen from data-n
- **Proposed fix:** Use a grid: repeat(min(n,4)) at desktop when it fits, else 2 x 2 for n=4 (the phone rule at :330 already does 2 columns); key off data-n which the markup already sets.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-13 · P3 · content — HP preview's 'Off' button actually previews Critical (0 HP)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The ⋯ menu's HP preview segment reads Live · Full · Off. 'Off' sets the preview to critical (0 / 100, 'Critical · nothing locks'); it does not turn the preview off (Live does). The label reads as the opposite of what it does.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/gov_hp_off.png (after pressing Off: 'HP preview 0 / 100 Critical'); js/modules/governor-ui.js:425 hpPreviewButton("critical", "Off")
- **Root cause:** Label text out of step with the value
- **Proposed fix:** Label the three 'Live · Full · Critical' (the HP state names used everywhere else).
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-14 · P3 · data — 'First day' on the Seat can be later than the history it sits beside (138 days journeyed, first day 1 Nov 2026)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The record reads 'Days journeyed 138' and 'First day 1 Nov 2026' side by side. First day is store.state.created, the install/reset moment, while days journeyed is the ledger's months rollup. An account whose history predates `created` (an imported backup, a sample account, a ledger older than the device's first boot) shows a first day after months of activity.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/seed_gov_s1b.png (138 days / 1 Nov 2026); js/modules/governor-ui.js:377 (since = +store.state.created), :391
- **Root cause:** First day reads the state document's creation stamp rather than the evidence
- **Proposed fix:** Show the earliest of state.created, the first session ledger entry and the earliest achievements month row.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-15 · P3 · a11y — Seat showcase medals: the focus ring is an oval (button 80 x 86.3)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad with keyboard
- **Detail / repro:** Each showcase medal on the Seat's arc is a .k-gv-arc-btn wrapping an inline 80px medal span; the button is 80 x 86.3 px, so its border-radius:50% focus outline is a tall ellipse hanging below the medal. Same root cause as the set dialog's oval chip.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/arc_focus_crop.png (2x, keyboard focus on the first showcase medal); eval .k-gv-arc-btn 80.0x86.3; css/views/governor.css:88-89
- **Root cause:** Inline medal span inside a button adds line-box height
- **Proposed fix:** display:grid; place-items:center; line-height:0 (or aspect-ratio:1) on .k-gv-arc-btn; ideally one shared 'medal button' rule in achievements.css used by the arc, the set strip and the picker.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AG-16 · P3 · ux — A partial showcase fills the Seat's arc from the bottom-left, leaving the top and right of the arc empty

- **Devices:** mac, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** Slots are filled left to right along AX/AY (bottom-left first, then rising). With three badges picked, the leading badge sits at the lowest-left point and the crown and the whole right half stay empty rings, so the arc looks lopsided; the code comment says 'highest in the middle'. The first badge also 'leads on the share card' but is the least prominent on the Seat.
- **Evidence:** shots/achievements-governor/after_save2.png (3 picked: bottom-left, left, upper-left filled; 3 empty rings to the right); js/modules/governor-ui.js:348-366
- **Root cause:** Showcase index used directly as the arc slot index
- **Proposed fix:** Map the pick order to slots centre-out (2,3,1,4,0,5) so the first picks take the crown and the arc stays symmetric.
- **Flags:** effort S

## SC — Gold Shop and every cosmetic, themes (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### SC-01 · P1 · ui — Shop avatar previews: the painted avatar escapes its disc (drawn top-left, overflowing the card) and the disc sits empty, offset

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Every one of the 12 avatar cards in the Gold Shop (and the preview sheet) draws the picture at the preview's top-left corner, overflowing the card's top edge (the fox's ears are clipped), while the dark backing disc sits centred and empty behind/below it, so the avatar reads off-centre and the disc looks like a stray shadow. Measured at mac 1470x832: preview box 235x235 at (458,3078); disc 179x179 at (486,3107); img 193x193 at (458,3078) - the image's top-left equals the preview's, not the disc's.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/ef-1.png and audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/ef-2.png (Avatars rows); eval getBoundingClientRect on .k-gv-ware[data-kind=avatar]; js/modules/governor-ui.js:1227-1228 (img(it.art) uses the default class k-gv-apv-img); css/views/governor.css:589 (.k-gv-apv-img position:absolute; inset:0) vs :602-603 (.k-gv-apv-disc has no position:relative)
- **Root cause:** Generic absolute-positioned image class reused inside a non-positioned disc
- **Proposed fix:** Give the avatar img its own class (e.g. img(it.art,'k-gv-apv-disc-img')) instead of k-gv-apv-img, or make .k-gv-apv-disc position:relative and the img inset:9%; centre with object-fit:contain. Re-check the art itself: several avatar PNGs carry a baked dark disc (koi, lamp) so a second CSS disc doubles it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-02 · P1 · ui — Try on panel's 'Buy · ◈ 4,500' button is illegible (dark ink on dark glass)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Trying any item on shows the Trying on panel on the Stage with its primary action 'Buy · ◈ n' - the purchase call to action of the whole try-on flow. It renders with color oklch(0.2 0.03 70) (gold-ink, meant for a gold fill) on background oklch(0.14 0.01 265 / .72) (the stage's glass), i.e. near-black on near-black; the button looks disabled although it is enabled.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-tryon-outfit.png (button left of 'Stop'); eval: getComputedStyle([data-ui~=shop.buy-tried]) color oklch(0.2 0.03 70), bg oklch(0.14 0.01 265/0.72); css/views/governor.css:57 '.k-gv-stage .k-btn { background: var(--gv-glass) }' (specificity 0,2,0) overrides css/views/governor.css:567 '.k-gv-buy { background: var(--gold) }' (0,1,0)
- **Root cause:** CSS specificity: stage glass-button rule beats the buy colour rule
- **Proposed fix:** Raise the buy rule's specificity (.k-gv-stage .k-gv-buy, or :where() on the stage rule) so a gold buy button keeps its gold fill on the Stage.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-03 · P1 · ui — A painted frame on the Seat spills over 'Level 53', the title chip and past the stage edge

- **Devices:** mac, ipad (same stage)
- **Detail / repro:** With a painted frame worn (Koi ring, bought in the shop), the Governor's Seat Status stage draws the frame picture 296x296 round a 180px avatar, outside the avatar's box with no space reserved: it overlaps the 'Level 53' heading by 24px (the L is under a koi's tail), hides the start of 'Pick a title' (reads 'k a title'), and is cut off by the stage's overflow:hidden on the left (frame x=228, stage x=236).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-status-mac.png; eval: frame art [228,190,296,296], .k-gv-name [500,274,329,58], 'Pick a title' [500,340], stage [236,202] overflow hidden; css/layout.css:254 (.k-avatar-frame-art 152% + 2*--fo, absolutely positioned); js/core/governor.js avatarNode (frame drawn 'outside the box ... never changes layout')
- **Root cause:** Painted frames are absolutely positioned overflow with no layout space reserved
- **Proposed fix:** Reserve the frame's bleed where a large framed avatar sits (padding/margin on the Seat's face column equal to (frame - avatar)/2, or a CSS var the host exposes, e.g. --frame-bleed), and scale painted frames so their inner hole meets the HP ring instead of a fixed 152%.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SC-04 · P1 · bug — Seals are sold as 'the topbar mark' but the topbar has no mark any more; the painted stamp art is never shown in use

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every seal says 'variant of the OS mark' and the Wardrobe row is labelled 'Seal · The topbar mark'. applyCosmetics() writes the glyph into '#topbar [data-ui~=shell.brand] [data-ui~=shell.brand-mark]', but the topbar brand is now <img class=k-brand-logo src=kurenai-bloom.png> + 'Kurenai' with no brand-mark element, so querySelector returns null and nothing changes. The only place a worn seal appears is the small glyph disc on the Seat's/profile ring (k-gv-ring-seal, text only). The six 400-gold seals' stamp pictures (app.seal.yuki...hikari) are drawn only in the shop card and the Wardrobe chip, never where the seal is worn.
- **Evidence:** eval: document.querySelector('[data-ui~=shell.brand-mark']) -> null; brand outerHTML = img + 'Kurenai'; js/core/governor.js:782-783; js/modules/governor-ui.js:235-244 (ring seal glyph only); wardrobe label in governor-ui.js WARD ['seal','Seal','The topbar mark']; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-assistant-gala.png (topbar shows the bloom logo with seal-hikari worn)
- **Root cause:** Topbar markup changed (brand image) without updating applyCosmetics/the seal feature
- **Proposed fix:** Decide where a seal shows and draw its stamp there: e.g. replace the topbar bloom with the worn seal's stamp (keep the bloom for 紅), and use the stamp art (not a crimson text disc) on the Seat ring and the Share card; fix the Wardrobe caption to match.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### SC-05 · P1 · bug — All 10 painted shelf skins and Shrine card styles never show: their picture URL resolves to /css/views/assets/... (404)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The five new shelf skins (Paulownia, Steel and glass, Library oak, Bamboo, Sakura wood, ◈800 each) and five Shrine card styles (Moon, Moth, Camellia, Lacquer, Frost, ◈900 each), 8,500 gold in all, can be bought and worn but draw nothing. books.js and shrine.js set an inline custom property --skin-art: url(assets/app/img/<id>.webp); collection.css consumes it with background: var(--skin-art). A relative url() inside a custom property is resolved against the stylesheet that uses it, so the browser requests /css/views/assets/app/img/app.shelf.sakura.webp (404), and the same for app.shrinecard.*. Worse, the painted-skin rule also strips the default shelf's board, border and shadow (border:0; box-shadow:none), so a bought painted skin makes the Books shelf look emptier than the free default (covers floating on the page background), while the cheap CSS skins (Walnut) work.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/shelf-compare.png (top: default, middle: Walnut CSS skin, bottom: Sakura wood painted skin = nothing); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-books-shelf-sakura.png; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-shrine-gold-frost.png; eval: getComputedStyle(.k-bk-shelf).backgroundImage = url(http://127.0.0.1:9104/css/views/assets/app/img/app.shelf.sakura.webp); Shrine hero cover ::after = .../css/views/assets/app/img/app.shrinecard.frost.webp; curl 404 vs /assets/app/img/... 200; js/modules/books.js:1285; js/modules/shrine.js:954; css/views/collection.css:532-534
- **Root cause:** Relative url() inside an inline CSS custom property resolves against the consuming stylesheet's URL
- **Proposed fix:** Set the variable to an absolute URL, as dayreview.js:142 already does: 'url("' + new URL('assets/app/img/' + art + '.webp', location.href).href + '")'. Then re-check each skin's look (keep a board under the painted back panel). Add a smoke check that every --skin-art resolves.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-06 · P1 · ui — Shop frame previews: the avatar is a small disc lost inside a big painted ring (55px avatar in a 179px frame)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every painted frame card (Sakura, Ice, Ember, Lacquer, Wisteria, Koi; ◈800 each) previews the frame at 179x179 with the avatar at 55x55 in its middle, i.e. the avatar fills ~42% of the ring's inner hole (the art's measured inner-hole radius is 0.67-0.74 of the frame), leaving a wide empty dark gap between avatar and ring. Worn, the same art is sized at 152% of the avatar, so the shop shows a different (and much worse) fit than the real thing. The preview sheet does the same at 120px.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-shop-frames.png; eval: Sakura ring frame-art [486,4375,179,179], avatar [548,4433,55,55]; image analysis: inner hole radius/half-width app.frame.sakura 0.73, ice 0.74, koi 0.67; js/modules/governor-ui.js:1229-1230 (avatarNode(big ? 120 : 64) inside .k-gv-apv-frame 76%); css/views/governor.css:604-607 (avatar * 0.86)
- **Root cause:** Preview avatar uses a fixed pixel size unrelated to the frame's box
- **Proposed fix:** Size the preview avatar as a fraction of the frame box (e.g. inline-size: 66% of .k-gv-apv-frame, matching the worn 1/1.52 rule) instead of a fixed 64/120px; better, store each frame's measured hole ratio in the catalogue and use it both worn and in the shop so the ring hugs the HP ring.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-07 · P1 · bug — With a painted frame worn, every CSS frame card in the shop previews the WORN frame instead of its own

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Wearing Koi ring, the Gilt ring, Kurenai bloom, Jade band and Amethyst orbit cards all show the Koi ring. shopPreview() builds the CSS-frame preview from KOS.governor.avatarNode(64), which already contains the worn painted frame's <img class=k-avatar-frame-art>, then only swaps data-frame to the previewed id; the rule '.k-avatar-host:has(> .k-avatar-frame-art)::before,::after {display:none}' then hides the CSS frame being sold, leaving the worn picture. The Avatar tab's frameChip removes that img (oldArt) but the shop does not.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-shop-frames.png (top row: four different CSS frames all drawn as the Koi ring); js/modules/governor-ui.js:1258-1265 (case 'frame' in shopPreview); css/layout.css:255
- **Root cause:** Preview reuses the live avatar node including the worn frame layer
- **Proposed fix:** Strip .k-avatar-frame-art from the node (as frameChip does) before setting data-frame, or give avatarNode an option {frame: id|null} so previews never inherit the worn frame; the same applies to the banner preview's avatarNode.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-08 · P1 · ui — A painted frame on Home's hero and on the phone overlaps the greeting and is cut off by the hero's edge

- **Devices:** mac, iphone, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** Same root cause as the Seat: the worn Koi ring is drawn at 152% of the avatar outside its box. On Home (mac) the frame [235,102,163,163] runs 6px under the headline 'Working late…' (h1 x=392) and the date kicker, and is clipped 1px by the hero (x=236, overflow hidden). On iPhone (402x874) the frame on Home is cropped by the hero's top-left rounded corner and sits on 'MONDAY 2 NOVEMBER'; on the phone Seat (#/governor) the frame's top is cut off by the stage's top edge and the koi cover the 'BEHAVIOURAL GOVERNOR' kicker.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-home-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-home-iphone.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-status-iphone.png; eval Home: frame [235,102,163,163], avatar [269,137,94,94], hero [236,80,1210,207] overflow hidden, h1 [392,161]; css/layout.css:254
- **Root cause:** Painted frame overflow with no reserved space
- **Proposed fix:** Reserve the frame bleed in every host that shows a large framed avatar (Home ring, Seat face, Avatar stage, phone stage): pad the ring's container by (frame-avatar)/2 or shrink the painted frame to the HP ring (using each frame's hole ratio) so nothing spills.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SC-09 · P2 · bug — Pen set 'Study set' (◈500) changes nothing: it maps the pen to exactly the default roles

- **Devices:** all

The Study set's description 'Ink, mark and note' and its shop preview (text, crimson, teal) are the default palette, so the user pays 500 gold for no visible change anywhere.

- **Root cause:** Catalogue item added without its CSS mapping
- **Proposed fix:** Give the Study set its own mapping (e.g. ink=text, accent=amber 'mark', teal=green 'note') in ink.css and the preview, or drop the item and refund owners.

### SC-10 · P2 · ui — Repainted art for the original 16 cosmetics is imported but never used (frames, shelf skins, Shrine cards, seals)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** docs/app-art.md promises 'the four owned ones are repainted free' (frames), 'the three owned ones repainted free' (shelf skins, Shrine card styles) and stamp art 'for all twelve' seals. The pictures exist in assets/app/img (app.frame.gilt/bloom/jade/amethyst, app.shelf.walnut/lacquer/vermilion, app.shrinecard.gilded/ink/neon, app.seal.homura/tsuki/ryuu/sakura/rai/hoshi) but no code references them: the 16 older catalogue rows have no art field, so they still draw as the old CSS rings/skins and the old seals preview as a 'Kurenai 焔' pill. The shop therefore mixes two visual generations in one grid (old CSS frames with a black strip preview ~110px tall next to tall painted square previews; seal row 2 has two short cards stretched to the painted cards' height with ~200px of empty card).
- **Evidence:** grep 'frame.gilt|shelf.walnut|shrinecard.gilded|app.seal.homura' js css -> no hits; js/core/governor.js:414-432 (no art); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/ef-2.png (Frames rows), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/ef-3.png (Seals rows, Rai/Hoshi cards half empty)

- **Proposed fix:** Add art: 'app.frame.gilt' etc. to the 16 legacy rows (frame-gold->app.frame.gilt, frame-crimson->app.frame.bloom, ...) with painted:true so avatarNode/the Books shelf/the Shrine/the seal preview take the painted path; keep ids so ownership is unchanged.
