### SC-15 · P2 · ui — Share-card finishes: the card looks nothing like the shop preview (Washi turns muddy brown), the finish reaches only the Reports card, and the shop preview's text is illegible on Washi

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** reportshare.js draws the finish picture at 55% and then the dark lacquer again at 50% on top, so Washi's cream kozo paper becomes a dirty grey-brown and Foil (a red paper with gold flakes, sold as 'a gold-foil edge') becomes a dark red wash with no gold edge. The shop card preview instead shows the raw picture under white 'THIS WEEK 20.3 h' text, which on Washi is white-on-cream and unreadable. The Wardrobe promises 'Your shared cards', but only the Reports share card reads the finish: the Shrine's 'Create share card' (shrine.js STYLES) neither uses the finish nor offers the five new painted Shrine card styles. The share dialog itself does not say which finish is on or let you pick one.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-share-washi.png (Reports share dialog with finish-washi worn); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/ef-1.png (shop finish previews: Washi text invisible); js/modules/reportshare.js:92-104 (0.55 alpha picture then 0.5 lacquer); js/modules/shrine.js:127-135 (styles list: only gold/crimson/ink + 3 legacy); js/modules/governor-ui.js artPreview case 'finish'
- **Root cause:** Finish implemented as a dimmed underlay; preview drawn separately from the real card
- **Proposed fix:** Make each finish a real treatment designed for the card (paper as the ground with dark ink text for Washi; a foil rim gradient for Foil; grain overlay for Film), render the shop preview from the same card() function scaled down, add a finish row to the share dialog, and apply it to the Shrine card too.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### SC-16 · P2 · bug — Ink toolbar's Paper button says 'Lined' on every bought paper (Kraft, Slate, Iso, Manuscript, Staff, 2 mm graph)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With '2 mm graph' bought and worn, a new notebook correctly starts on graph paper (the picker highlights '2 mm graph' and .k-ink-page[data-paper=graph]), but the toolbar's Paper menu button reads 'Lined'. paintPaper() looks the label up in I.PAPERS (the six built-in papers only) and falls back to PAPERS[1] = Lined for any shop paper, so the toolbar always lies once a bought paper is in use.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-ink-graph-sketch.png (toolbar right end 'Lined' while the page is graph); eval: .k-ink-paper-btn text 'Lined▾', .k-ink-page data-paper 'graph'; js/core/inkui.js:1020; js/core/ink.js:42-56 (PAPERS vs SHOP_PAPERS / papers())
- **Root cause:** Label lookup uses the built-in list only
- **Proposed fix:** Look the label up in I.papers() (built-in + owned shop papers), or in PAPERS.concat(SHOP_PAPERS).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SC-17 · P2 · ux — 'Try on' for Focus scenes, Shrine halls and share-card finishes shows nothing: the try-on ends the moment you leave the shop

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Scenes, halls and finishes get a Try on button (card and preview sheet). Pressing it only adds a line to the Stage's 'Trying on' list: the Stage keeps drawing the profile banner (stageBg -> seatBgSrc reads the banner only) and nothing on the shop page renders the tried scene/hall/finish. The places that would show it (Focus stage, Shrine hero, Reports share card) are on other pages, and KOS.show() calls stopTrying() on any navigation away from #/governor/shop (js/core/ui.js:1494-1497), so the try-on can never be seen. Only outfits (the mannequin) and themes (the whole app) are visibly tried on. Conversely avatars, frames, banners, seals, shelf skins and Shrine cards - things that could be shown on the Stage - have no Try on at all.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-tryon-scene-hall.png (Trying on: The night train, Moon hall, Washi; stage images = app.banner.snow + the outfit only); js/modules/governor-ui.js:1039 (canTry list), 1092 (sheet Try on kinds), 271-294 (stageBg ignores tried kinds); js/core/ui.js:1494-1497
- **Root cause:** Try on wired to state only; no renderer for most kinds on the shop page
- **Proposed fix:** Make the Stage the fitting room: while a scene/hall/banner is tried, draw it as the Stage ground (with the 25:00 clock or the 01 rank over it); a finish as a mini share card; avatar/frame/seal on a live identity chip. Or remove Try on from kinds that cannot be shown.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### SC-18 · P2 · ux — Preview sheet of an owned item is a dead end: no Use / Take off, no 'Owned' or 'In use' state, and half the dialog is empty

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Opening an owned ware (e.g. Sakura ring) shows the picture, the kicker, the name and one line of description, and nothing else: shopSheet() only builds actions for the buy/try states, so the footer is omitted entirely - no way to wear it from the sheet, no sign that it is owned or in use, only the ✕. The right column holds three short lines in a ~1060x615 dialog (the text column is empty below y=290). The avatar inside the frame preview is again tiny (103px in a ~380px ring).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-sheet-owned-frame.png; js/modules/governor-ui.js:1090-1098 (actions only when st is buy/try), 1104 (footer omitted when no actions)
- **Root cause:** Sheet actions written for the purchase states only
- **Proposed fix:** Add the card's own actions to the sheet for owned states (Use / Take off, Unlocked · where it shows), a status chip, and a 'Where it shows' line per kind (the Wardrobe's captions); size the dialog to its content.

### SC-19 · P2 · ux — Two sources of truth for the Focus scene / Shrine hall / banner: the shop's 'Use' says 'In use' while the picture drawer's rule silently wins

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The shop and Wardrobe wear a scene with g.scene (KOS.governor.wear), while the picture drawer (KOS.artSlot) stores state.artSlots.focus = {mode, pick, ...}. Once the drawer has ever been used, KOS.art.pick('focus') returns the drawer's rule and ignores g.scene. Reproduced: drawer 'One picture' -> The library, then shop/Wardrobe 'Use' The night train: g.scene = scene-train and the shop card reads '✓ In use', but KOS.art.pick('focus') = app.focus.library and explain = 'Now: The library, always.' Nothing in the shop or Wardrobe says a rule is overriding the choice. Also, with no drawer rule yet, the drawer opens on 'One picture' with no thumbnail selected and says 'Now: First snow, by the season' although nothing rotates (explain() falls through to the season wording).
- **Evidence:** eval after clicking the library thumb in KOS.artSlot.open('focus') then KOS.governor.wear('scene','scene-train'): {cfg:{mode:'one',pick:'app.focus.library'}, worn:'scene-train', pick:'app.focus.library'}; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/shop-cosmetics/seed-drawer-focus.png; js/core/art.js:118-126 (rotated: cfg wins), 168 (fallback to G.scene only without cfg), 143-152 (explain); js/modules/governor-ui.js wareActive/wareUse
- **Root cause:** Rotation rule added beside the existing worn-item field instead of replacing it
- **Proposed fix:** Make one record: 'Use' in the shop/Wardrobe should write the drawer rule ({mode:'one', pick: art}) or clear it, and the drawer's One picture should mark/write g.scene; the drawer's initial state should select the worn item and explain should say 'always' for the implicit one-picture state.
- **Flags:** effort S


---

## ST — Study desks (CS/Maths/IT), topic page, Review, Assignments (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** The study core works, but six problems would trip the user up on their own devices. On iPad and iPhone, tapping an assignment does nothing you can see: the detail opens at the bottom of the page. On the Mac its Focus and Complete buttons sit about 1,000px below the panel's content. On the iPhone, the Working and Edit buttons squeeze a topic's tab strip so far that the selected tab is often hidden. 'Quiz best' stores a count of correct answers, but the inspector and the subject tile print it as a percentage, so 6 of 11 reads '6%'. Flashcard batches are logged only when the whole batch is finished, so 100 cards rated out of a 308-card queue give no streak, no pay and 'Reviewed 0'. The IT desk's boundary chart is crushed on iPad portrait. The root causes are mostly master-detail and side-column layouts with no step between 1240px and 700px (the assignment panel, the inspector, the IT grids, the tool row beside the tabs), two readers that use different units for one field (quiz best), and surfaces that read the session list instead of the per-rating review log.

**Coverage.** Seeded and empty accounts. Measured topic pages (all 7 tabs) at mac/ipad/iphone; Review, Due today, Card stats, Assignments at mac/ipad/iphone (seeded) and mac/iphone (empty). Exercised the flashcard engine (click and keyboard ratings), the quiz engine to completion, notes paging (Next page, page select), the quick note (filed on leaving), the study editor (mac/iphone), the spine drawer (iphone/ipad), the new-assignment form (empty-save validation, save), the assignment detail (mac/ipadl/ipad/iphone), Atelier Dawn on flashcards. An earlier auditor's 7 findings were kept (desk heroes compared across all seven study spaces, iPad touch sizes, IT desk at iPad portrait, unit-card zeros, pill clipping, assignment row rule, phone next-card). Not examined: the Simulate tab beyond its locked state, the Files tab's preview with a real PDF, the Handwritten tab when drawing with a pen (no Pencil in the harness), a shop theme, landscape iPhone, the Maths/IT topic pages beyond a measurement pass.

### ST-01 · P1 · ui — IT desk on iPad portrait: the qualification-boundary chart is crushed to 52px and its numbers overlap; the units table truncates every unit name

- **Devices:** iPad portrait (and Split View)
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px (the user's iPad in portrait) the IT 'So far' card keeps its three-column desktop grid (220px | 1fr | 340px with 32px gaps) inside a 716px card, so the middle column is ~52px: the P M D D* ruler is 90px wide, its four boundary numbers print on top of each other ('120180240270' as one smear) and the caption 'Qualification boundaries · out of 300 across the 5 units' wraps one word per line over eight lines. The IT units table below keeps its six fixed columns (44 + 180 + 120px + gaps), so unit names read 'F200 Funda…', 'F201 Big dat…', status wraps to three lines ('Sitting · date not set') and the header 'AGAINST THE BOUNDARIES' wraps to three lines. Landscape (1180) is fine (middle column 252px).
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/s-it-ipad.png (full page), zoom shots/study-core/s-it-agg-ipad-crop.png; eval --size ipad: it.agg 716 wide, it.target at x442 w340; k-it-name ellipsised at 95px. css/views/study.css:996 (.k-it-agg three columns), :1031 (.k-it-tr six columns), the single-column/stacked rules only at study.css:1123-1125 (the <=700 tier); tokens.css:657-661 fixed widths; js/modules/itdesk.js:109 caption.
- **Root cause:** desktop grid with fixed side columns has no tier between 1080 and 700
- **Proposed fix:** Add an 860px tier (invariant 47 breakpoints): .k-it-agg two columns (left+ruler stacked, target/need on the right) or one column; .k-it-tr switch to the stacked grid-template-areas layout already written for <=700. Give the ruler numbers a min width or drop them below the bar when narrow.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-02 · P1 · ui — iPhone topic page: the Working and Edit buttons squeeze the tab strip to ~181px, hiding the selected tab

- **Devices:** iPhone (and iPad Slide Over)
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px, on every topic tab that shows Working and Edit (Specification, Notes, Flashcards, Quiz, Exam questions), the tab row holds the tab scroller plus those two pill buttons in one flex row (flex: none on the buttons). The scroller shrinks to 181px: only 'Specification' and 'Notes' fit, the Notes count reads '1' instead of '13', and on Quiz the selected tab is not visible at all (strip shows 'Specification, Notes 8 ›'). On Files/Handwritten the strip is 328px, so its width jumps as you change tab. The phone user sees about two of seven tabs.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-topic-notes-iphone.png (Notes), shots/study-core/q-quiz-result-iphone.png (Quiz selected but invisible); eval --size iphone on #/ref/compsci/4.1.1.1 Notes: .k-topic-tabs clientWidth 181, scrollWidth 621. css/views/study.css:208-213 (.k-topic-tabrow one flex row; :not(.k-scroller) flex:none); js/modules/hub.js:2746 (scroller, moreSlot, workBtn, editBtn in one row).
- **Root cause:** tools share the tab row with no narrow-width tier
- **Proposed fix:** Under 700px wrap the tools onto their own line (.k-topic-tabrow {flex-wrap: wrap} with the scroller at flex-basis 100%, or move Working/Edit into the reader row beside the page picker / the ⋯ fold), so the tab strip always gets the full width.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-03 · P1 · bug — 'Quiz best' shows a raw count as a percentage: 6 of 11 right reads 'Quiz best 6%'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The quiz engine stores a topic's best as the number of correct answers (st.best = correct), but the topic inspector and the subject desk's 'Quiz best' tile print it through pctText as a percent. Reproduced on a fresh topic (#/ref/compsci/4.2.2.1): 6/11 (lastPct 55) is stored best 6 and the inspector shows 'Quiz best 6%'. The subject tile then takes the max of counts across topics of different lengths. The seeded account has percentages in best (57), so the quiz header reads 'best 57/10'. Either way one surface is wrong for every learner.
- **Evidence:** eval: state.study.quiz['compsci:4.2.2.1'] = {attempts:1, best:6, lastPct:55}; inspector text 'Quiz best\n6%'; quiz head 'score 0 · best 6/11'. js/engines/quiz.js:159 (best = correct count), :77 (prints best + '/' + items.length); js/modules/hub.js:3224 and :2200 pctText(best); hub.js:126 max over topics.
- **Root cause:** two readers disagree on the unit of study.quiz[k].best
- **Proposed fix:** Store best as a percent (bestPct) in the quiz engine, migrate existing records once at the store gate (best/len where the deck length is known, else keep), and print '/n' only from a stored count; or keep the count and make hub.js divide by the topic's quiz length. Add a smoke check that the inspector and the quiz header agree.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-04 · P1 · bug — Cards reviewed in an unfinished flashcard batch are never logged: no streak, no pay, 'Today so far · Reviewed 0'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The flashcard engine logs its session (and so the Governor's XP/gold/HP, the study streak and every ledger reader) only when the WHOLE batch is finished. Due today opens 'Start review · all 308'; rating 7 cards and leaving (or rating 100 of 308) writes FSRS schedules and review-log rows but adds nothing to the ledger: Review's 'Today so far' still says Reviewed 0, Card stats' Reviews per day and Rating mix stay at zero, the day does not count for the streak (invariant 4) and nothing is paid. With a real backlog the user is punished for doing most of the work.
- **Evidence:** eval: on #/due rated 5 cards Good, navigated to #/review: 0 new sessions, 'Today so far Reviewed 0 Accuracy —', dueCount 308 -> 303. js/engines/flashcards.js:325-353 (sessions.log only in finish()); js/modules/due.js:222-230 and js/modules/cardstats.js:218-228 read only those session entries. shots/study-core/q-cardstats-mac.png (Total reviews 5,810 beside an empty Reviews per day and an all-zero Rating mix).
- **Root cause:** session logged only in finish(); review surfaces read session metrics rather than the review log
- **Proposed fix:** Log the batch when the view is left or hidden (the page-hide / KOS.show teardown the engine already notes at :373), paying for the cards rated so far, once (a session id held by the engine and updated, never logged twice); or cap a due batch at a size people finish (e.g. 20-50 with 'Next 50'). Read 'Reviewed today' and Reviews per day from KOS.reviewLog (one row per rating) instead of session metrics.
- **Flags:** effort M

### ST-05 · P1 · ux — Assignment detail: 'Focus on this' and the Complete/Submitted/Blocked buttons sit ~1,000px below the panel's content, off-screen

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** On the Mac, opening an assignment shows its facts, Subtasks and Attachments, then nothing: the panel is stretched to the full list height (min-block-size: 100%, 1569px on the seeded account) and its footer has margin-block-start: auto, so '◉ Focus on this', '✓ Complete', '⇪ Submitted', 'Blocked' and 'Reopen' land at y≈1577, about 1,000px under the last block. The panel is position: sticky but taller than the viewport, so it never sticks. The primary actions of the page are invisible unless you scroll to the bottom of the whole list.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-asg-open-mac.png (panel ends at ATTACHMENTS, no actions visible); eval: .k-asg-foot y 1577 (margin-top 984px), previous block bottom 577, .k-asg-panel height 1569 sticky. css/views/study.css:864 (min-block-size: 100%), :886 (.k-asg-foot margin-block-start: auto); js/modules/assignments.js:305-321.
- **Root cause:** stretched sticky panel + auto margin footer
- **Proposed fix:** Size the panel to the viewport: max-block-size: calc(100dvh - header) with overflow-y: auto and no min-block-size: 100% (align-self: start), so the sticky panel sticks and the foot sits at its bottom edge; or drop margin-block-start: auto and put the actions right under the stats.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-06 · P1 · ux — On iPad and iPhone, tapping an assignment opens its detail at the bottom of the page, out of sight

- **Devices:** iPad portrait/landscape, iPhone, Mac windows under 1240
- **Detail / repro:** At 1240px and below the assignments layout becomes one column and the detail panel is appended after the whole list (position: static). Tapping a row only highlights it; the detail, with Edit, subtasks, attachments, Focus on this and Complete, renders 1,300-2,700px further down and nothing scrolls to it or opens it as a sheet. On the iPad (both orientations) and iPhone the page appears to do nothing.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-asg-tap-ipadl.png (row highlighted, no detail); eval after tapping row 2: panel top y=2109 (ipadl, viewport 820), 2629 (ipad, 1180), 2729 (iphone, 874), not in a dialog. css/views/study.css:1057-1059 (@media max-width 1240: one column, panel static); js/modules/assignments.js has no scrollIntoView/openDialog for the detail.
- **Root cause:** master-detail layout collapses to a stacked list with no navigation to the detail
- **Proposed fix:** Below 1240 open the detail in a KOS.ui.openDialog sheet (bottom sheet on the phone, side sheet on the iPad) or expand it inline under the tapped row; at minimum scrollIntoView the panel and move focus to its heading.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-07 · P2 · a11y — Topic inspector and Assignments controls stay 30-32px tall on iPad touch (below the 44px minimum)

- **Devices:** iPad portrait and landscape
- **Detail / repro:** On the iPad (820x1180 and 1180x820, pointer: coarse) the zero-specificity :where() touch rule in base.css loses to view-level class rules. On a topic page: the status select (k-ts-select) is 560x32, the page picker (k-topic-page-select) 304x30, the four 'Ask Kurenai / Make flashcards / Make a quiz / Test me' buttons 350x32, the progress-check rows 33px tall. On #/assignments the four filter selects are 30px tall. The phone layout (<700) is fine; only the iPad, the user's touch device, misses the target. Also on iPhone the four assignment filter selects measure 30px (148x30 etc.), Card stats retention selects 67x30, and the Handwritten tab paper chips 32px with the finger toggle 34x20 on touch.
- **Evidence:** eval on #/ref/compsci/4.2.1.1 --size ipad: asst.ctx-btn minHeight 32px, ui.status-select 32px, topic.reader-page-select min-height 0 -> 30px, label.k-ts-check 33px; #/assignments ui.status-select 148x30. css/views/study.css:414 '.k-insp-asst button {min-block-size: var(--hit)}' (0,1,1) beats css/views/assistant.css:517 coarse rule (0,1,0); css/views/study.css:231 '.k-topic-page-select {min-block-size: 0}'; study.css:428 '.k-ts-select {min-block-size: var(--rag-pick)}' (32px) override css/base.css:115-119.
- **Root cause:** base.css touch rule is :where() zero specificity, so any class that sets min-block-size (even 0) wins
- **Proposed fix:** Add these to the study.css coarse block at :1184 (.k-insp-asst button, .k-ts-select, .k-topic-page-select, .k-ts-check, the assignment filter selects -> min-block-size: var(--hit-coarse)); or make the base touch rule a layered override that view rules can't silently undercut (e.g. a tools/smoke check that every control under pointer:coarse measures >=44).
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-08 · P2 · ui — Study-space heroes are seven different heights on one shared component

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** All seven desks use the same .k-subj-hero (same width, radius, padding, 132px ring, title size), but the height varies with what each space bolts on. Mac 1470: CS/Maths/IT 189px, Code 292, Mind 296, Japanese 339, Creatives 341. iPad portrait: CS 347, Maths/IT 297, Code 459, Mind 482, Japanese 507, Creatives 509. iPhone: CS 255, Maths/IT 205, Creatives 309, Code 379, Mind 402, Japanese 438. Switching subject with the pills makes the whole page below jump by up to 150px. The four path strips are also styled four slightly different ways (Code: 'Stage 1 Foundations' labels over boxed units; Japanese: kanji labels and ellipsised stage names 'Intermedi…', 'Upper int…'; Mind: a single row of unit chips; Creatives: unlabelled-stage section names centred over boxes), and the exam subjects have no path strip at all. The side column then stretches to the hero, so Code's 'Start here' (290px) and Creatives' 'Start here' (340px) cards carry ~120px of content over ~200px of empty card.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/heroes-compare-mac.png (CS, Code, Japanese, Mind, Creatives cropped one above the other), shots/study-core/ipad-cs-vs-maths.png; eval scratchpad/qa/sc-hero.js at mac/ipad/iphone (numbers above). Duplicated per-space rules: css/views/code.css:162-163, css/views/japanese.css:82,172, css/views/mind.css:103-104, css/views/creatives.css:26-27 (identical grid-template-rows/path padding/border written four times); css/views/study.css:54-55 makes the side column follow the hero.
- **Root cause:** each study space extends the shared hero with its own path/bars markup and duplicated CSS; height is content-driven
- **Proposed fix:** Promote the path strip to one shared part of the hero (.k-subj-hero-path in study.css with a fixed row height, one label style, shared ellipsis rule) and give the bar column a fixed number of rows (pad to 4 or set a fixed hero min-block-size per breakpoint) so every desk's hero is one height; give CS/Maths/IT a path strip too (their units, as Course units already lists) or a matching fixed height. Let the side 'Start here' cards size to content and fill the rest with the next card (Due/Deadlines) instead of stretching.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### ST-09 · P2 · ui — Subject pills clip 'Study spa…' inside a nested scroller on iPad portrait

- **Devices:** iPad portrait (820)
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px the Study section nav fits, but the CS / Maths / IT / Study spaces pill group (.k-seg, overflow-x:auto) is flex-shrunk from 330 to 288px inside .k-subnav, so 'Study spaces' is cut to 'Study spa' with no arrow or fade; the outer subnav scroller (which has the arrows) never overflows, so nothing tells the user the pill can scroll.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/ipad-cs-vs-maths.png (top nav row); eval --size ipad: .k-seg x88 w288 scrollWidth 330 overflow auto, parent .k-subnav w716 sw716. css/components.css:52 (.k-seg overflow-x:auto, max-inline-size:100%), css/components.css:80 (.k-subnav flex).
- **Root cause:** nested shrinkable scroller inside the declared scroller
- **Proposed fix:** .k-subnav > .k-seg { flex: none; } so the declared outer k-subnav-scroller owns overflow (it already has arrows), as invariant 50 requires.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-10 · P2 · ui — Assignment list rows draw a rule through the middle: .k-asg-sub is two different things

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every row on #/assignments has a hairline between its title and its 'CS · Coursework' line, and that line is 13.5px with 7px padding instead of the intended 12px muted caption. The class k-asg-sub names both the row subtitle and a subtask row in the detail panel; the subtask rule (display:flex, padding-block, border-block-start, font-size 13.5) lands on every list row too.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/s-assign-mac.png, zoom shots/study-core/s-assign-row-crop.png; elementsFromPoint under the title hits .k-asg-sub. css/views/study.css:856 (.k-asg-sub as caption) vs css/views/study.css:879 (.k-asg-sub as subtask row with border-block-start).
- **Root cause:** class-name collision between two components in one stylesheet
- **Proposed fix:** Rename the subtask row (e.g. .k-asg-subtask, and its -t/-x parts) in js/modules/assignments.js and study.css:879-883; add the pair to smoke55's collision guard like the Review/Wishlist ones.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-11 · P2 · ui — Weakest/Continue card ellipsises a word mid-title on the phone ('4.5.4.8 Norm… of floating point form')

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Under 700px the side cards wrap (white-space: normal) but keep overflow:hidden + text-overflow:ellipsis, so a long first word that does not fit beside the ref is cut to 'Norm…' and the rest wraps below. The title reads 'Norm… of floating point form'. The ref and the title also have no space between them in the text (the reader hears '4.5.4.8Normalisation').
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/s-compsci-iphone-next-crop.png (dpr2); .k-subj-next-t measured 110x65 on iphone. css/views/study.css:60 (nowrap/overflow/ellipsis), :1171 (white-space: normal only).
- **Root cause:** ellipsis rules kept when wrapping is enabled
- **Proposed fix:** At <=700 set overflow: visible; text-overflow: clip; overflow-wrap: anywhere (or a -webkit-line-clamp: 3 box) on .k-subj-next-t; put the ref on its own line in the phone layout; add a space or aria-label between ref and title.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-12 · P2 · ux — A topic's tab strip forgets its scroll on every redraw, leaving the selected tab off-screen on the phone

- **Devices:** iPhone, iPad Slide Over
- **Detail / repro:** On iPhone, scroll the topic tab strip to Handwritten and select it, then any redraw (KOS.rerender: a cloud pull, a rotation crossing a breakpoint, a store change) rebuilds the strip at scrollLeft 0: the selected tab sits at x=498 of a 402px screen and no visible tab is marked as current, while the panel below shows Handwritten (also seen with Files in shots/study-core/q-topic-spec-iphone.png: Files content, strip showing Specification…Quiz). The topic tab strip is created without the scroller's keep/reveal options that the other redrawn strips use since kos-polish-3.
- **Evidence:** eval --size iphone #/ref/compsci/4.1.1.4: after selecting Handwritten the tab is at x 223-330 (scrollLeft 275); after KOS.rerender() it is at x 498-605, scrollLeft 0. shots/study-core/q-topic-spec-iphone.png. js/modules/hub.js:2746 KOS.ui.scroller(tabBar, {keepRole, prevLabel, nextLabel}) with no keep/reveal.
- **Root cause:** scroller created without keep/reveal
- **Proposed fix:** Pass keep: 'topic-tabs' and reveal: the selected tab (KOS.ui.scroller({keep, reveal})) as the term ribbon and edge-marked tabs do, and reveal the selected tab on a tab change too.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-13 · P2 · ui — Due today's progress meter draws one pip per card: a 308-card queue is a 108px wall of 308 dashes

- **Devices:** all (worst on Mac and iPhone)
- **Detail / repro:** The flashcard session meter appends a span per card (flex-wrap), so a real backlog (308 due on the seeded account) renders 13 rows of tiny dashes above the card, 560x108px, pushing the card below the fold on the Mac (card top at y=460 of 832) and carrying no readable information. It is rebuilt (308 nodes) on every card.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-due-mac.png; eval on #/due: [data-ui~=fc.pip] count 308, fc.meter 560x108. js/engines/flashcards.js:271-273, css/views/study.css:453-455.
- **Root cause:** meter scales linearly with deck size
- **Proposed fix:** Above ~30 cards draw one continuous bar (done/total, with the current position) instead of pips, e.g. KOS.ui progress bar with a 'n of N' label; keep pips for small topic decks.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-14 · P2 · bug — Card stats' RAG column is always empty: the dot is a 0x0 inline span

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Per-topic breakdown table has a RAG header but every cell looks blank. Each cell holds <span class=k-spine-dot data-band=a>, whose size rules (inline-size/block-size) do not apply to an inline element outside the spine's flex row, so it measures 0x0. The 'charts →' links sit in an unlabelled last column, which reads as if they belonged to RAG.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-cardstats-mac.png (RAG column blank); eval: table td .k-spine-dot getBoundingClientRect 0x0, display inline, data-band 'a'. js/modules/cardstats.js:357, css/views/study.css:162.
- **Root cause:** component rule assumes a flex parent
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-spine-dot display:inline-block (or render KOS.rag's chip with its label, 'Amber'), and label the last column ('Charts') or make the whole row the link.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-15 · P2 · ux — The spec spine drawer (iPad portrait, iPhone) opens at the top of the subject, not at the topic you are on, and does not take focus

- **Devices:** iPad portrait, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On a topic page at 820px or 402px, ☰ 'Show the spec spine' opens the overlay drawer scrolled to 4.1. The current topic (marked aria-current) is ~1,600px further down the drawer (y≈2420 in an 822px drawer on iPhone, 2424 on iPad portrait), so finding the neighbouring topics means scrolling the whole tree every time. Focus stays on <body>, so a keyboard or VoiceOver user is not taken into the drawer (no dialog semantics, no focus move).
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-spine-iphone.png (drawer at 4.1 while on 4.4.1.3 Kinds of abstraction); eval --size iphone and --size ipad after clicking [data-ui~=study.spine-open]: current entry y 2416/2424, drawer scrollTop 0, activeElement BODY. js/modules/hub.js:368-375 setTreeClosed only toggles drawerOpen/applyTreeCollapsed; no scrollIntoView or focus.
- **Root cause:** drawer open path does no reveal/focus management
- **Proposed fix:** When the drawer opens, scroll the aria-current entry into view (block: 'center') and focus it (or the filter); trap/restore focus as an overlay (or use KOS.ui.openDialog's focus handling) and return focus to ☰ on close.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-16 · P2 · ui — Card stats' Due forecast draws the coming days' bars in --s3, darker than the card they sit on (near invisible)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In Graphite the 13 future-day bars of Due forecast are filled var(--s3) = oklch(0.14 …), below the card surface (~0.17-0.19), so they read as black holes rather than bars; only the red overdue bar and the numbers above show. The chart is the main reason to open the card.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-cardstats-mac.png and shots/study-core/q-cardstats-dawn-ipadl.png (Due forecast); eval: rect fill oklch(0.14 0.008 265), body oklch(0.165 0.008 265). js/modules/cardstats.js:268 bars(forecast, {color: 'var(--s3)'}).
- **Root cause:** surface token used as a data colour
- **Proposed fix:** Use the scope hue (HUE[sid] / --teal, as Reviews per day does) or --muted for future days, keeping --alarm for overdue; check the result in Dawn and a shop theme.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-17 · P2 · ux — On iPad portrait the topic inspector is stacked under the whole tab, ~1,700-2,600px down, behind a right-pointing collapse chevron

- **Devices:** iPad portrait
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px the inspector (topic status, the four checks, RAG, recall record, quick note, Ask Kurenai / Make flashcards / Test me) is laid out after the tab content at full width: the status select sits at y≈1765 on Specification and the Ask Kurenai buttons at y≈2591, below the end of a long notes page, so the state surface invariant 52 makes 'the sole state surface' is out of reach while studying. Its '›' toggle still points right as if the panel were a side column. On iPad landscape and the Mac it is a side column.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/empty-topic-ipad.png (Inspector heading under the notes, '›' at right); measurement --size ipad on #/ref/compsci/4.1.1.1: ui.status-select y 1765, asst.ctx-btn y 2591 (Spec), 1227/2053 (Notes).
- **Root cause:** no tier for the inspector between the side column and the phone layout
- **Proposed fix:** Between 700 and 1080 open the inspector as an overlay side sheet from a header button (as the spine drawer does), or as a compact strip of status + checks above the tabs with the rest in a sheet; give the stacked state a down/up chevron.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### ST-18 · P3 · content — Unit cards print 'completed · 0 started' (a supporting zero, invariant 77)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every Course units card on CS/Maths/IT desks reads '0 / 17 completed · 0 started' on a fresh account and 'n completed · 0 started' whenever nothing is in progress. The started count is a supporting fact and should be suppressed at zero.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/e-compsci-mac.png (Course units row); js/modules/hub.js:2065 always appends ' completed · ' + started + ' started'.
- **Root cause:** hub.js:2065 unconditional string
- **Proposed fix:** Append ' · n started' only when started > 0.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-19 · P3 · ux — Inspector's Quiz best and attempts do not update when a quiz finishes

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** After finishing a quiz on a topic the inspector still shows the old 'Quiz best' (0% on a first attempt) until something redraws the page; the session has been logged and paid. The result card and the inspector disagree on screen.
- **Evidence:** eval on #/ref/compsci/4.2.2.1: after the last 'See your score', record attempts 1 / best 6, inspector still 'Quiz best 0%'; after KOS.rerender() it reads 6%. js/engines/quiz.js:150-165 finish() saves and logs but does not refresh the inspector.
- **Root cause:** engine writes state without notifying the inspector
- **Proposed fix:** Have finish() trigger the topic page's inspector refresh (the same hook the flashcard engine uses after a rating), or KOS.rerender with keep.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-20 · P3 · bug — Streak milestone note repeats '36-day streak' once per milestone, and missing milestone records pay every past milestone at once

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** A quiz scored 40% paid 56 gold: 2 for the quiz plus 3+7+14+30 for streak milestones the run had already passed, and the award notes read '36-day streak' four times. The note text uses the current streak (stk) instead of the milestone (ms). The retro-payment happens whenever g.milestones lacks the run's dates (seen on the seeded account; also possible after the ledger's 2,000-entry cap shortens the computed streak, which moves the run's start date and re-pays every milestone).
- **Evidence:** eval KOS.governor.lastAward(): {gold:56, notes:['36-day streak' x4]}; state.governor.milestones afterwards streak3..30 dated 09-30..10-27. js/core/governor.js:330-339.
- **Root cause:** note uses stk; milestone key derived from a recomputed streak length
- **Proposed fix:** Push ms + '-day streak' (and collapse to one note), pay only the highest newly reached milestone when several are pending, and key the run by its first ledger date that is not subject to the cap (or store the run start once).
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-21 · P3 · ui — Quiz "Next question" shows a keyboard Enter hint (↵) on touch devices

- **Devices:** iPhone, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** On iPhone the Next question button carries a ↵ key badge; there is no keyboard. The flashcard Keys panel likewise lists Space/1-4 on touch. The flashcard front also says "press to flip" on touch.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-quiz-result-iphone.png
- **Root cause:** kbd hint rendered regardless of input type
- **Proposed fix:** Hide key hints under (hover: none) and (pointer: coarse), as other kbd hints should be.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-22 · P3 · content — Study editor card headers print raw ISO dates and wrap "Card 1" onto two lines on the phone

- **Devices:** all; wrap on iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Each row in the flashcard editor reads e.g. "10 reviews · due 2026-10-29" (an ISO date, and a date already past with no "overdue"). On iPhone the three 44px row buttons leave the header so little width that "Card" and "1" break onto separate lines and the due date splits as "due 2026- / 10-29".
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-editor-mac.png, shots/study-core/q-editor-iphone.png
- **Root cause:** raw card.due string in js/modules/editor.js row header
- **Proposed fix:** Format with the app date helper ("due 29 Oct", "4 days overdue"), keep the label nowrap, and put the meta on its own line under the phone tier.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-23 · P3 · ux — Empty Review, Due today and Assignments are dead ends with zero rows and placeholder panels

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On a new account: Review's 'Today so far' prints Reviewed 0 / Accuracy — / Time 0 min (supporting zeros, invariant 77) and 'Next 7 days · nothing scheduled' keeps a 100px empty chart with seven grey ticks. Due today is one line ('Queue clear…') under a lone '← Queue' button on an otherwise empty 1210px page, with no way forward (e.g. 'Study a new topic' / Continue from the plan). Assignments shows four filter selects with nothing to filter and a right column whose only content is 'Select an assignment to see its brief…' although there are none.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-empty-review-mac.png, q-empty-due-mac.png, q-empty-assignments-mac.png; js/modules/due.js:222-236 (Today so far always drawn).
- **Root cause:** empty states render the populated layout's scaffolding
- **Proposed fix:** Hide 'Today so far' until something was reviewed today and collapse the empty forecast to its sub-line; give Due today's empty state the next action from KOS.homeFocusPlan / Continue; hide the assignment filters and the detail column while the list is empty and let the empty state with its 'New assignment' button take the width.
- **Flags:** effort S

### ST-24 · P3 · a11y — New assignment: a missing title is reported only in a toast; the field is not marked or focused, and a subject-less assignment can never be focused on

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Pressing Save with an empty title keeps the dialog open and shows the toast 'An assignment needs a title.', but the Title input gets no aria-invalid, no inline message and no focus (on the iPhone sheet the toast can sit under the keyboard). The form also allows 'No subject' (the default), and the detail panel then omits '◉ Focus on this' (it requires a.subject), so that assignment can never receive Focus time. The sheet's inputs are 39-42px tall on touch.
- **Evidence:** shots/study-core/q-asg-new-iphone.png; eval --size iphone: after Save, title aria-invalid null, activeElement not the title, #toast text 'An assignment needs a title.'; field heights 39-42. js/modules/assignments.js:316 (Focus button only when a.subject).
- **Root cause:** validation via toast only; Focus link gated on subject
- **Proposed fix:** Mark the field (aria-invalid + aria-describedby to an inline error) and focus it; either require a subject or let Focus run on a subject-less assignment (Focus's own subject picker); raise sheet controls to --hit-coarse.
- **Flags:** effort S

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Reveal the selected tab whenever a scroller's selection changes (topic tabs, flashcard modes).
- Say 'tap to flip' on touch devices and hide keyboard badges (↵, Space, 1-4) there.
- Print relative dates in the study editor and assignment rows ('due 29 Oct', '4 days overdue').
- Give the 'charts →' column in Card stats a header, or make the whole row the link.

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- Read every review count (Today so far, Reviews per day, Rating mix, Reports) from KOS.reviewLog instead of session metrics.
- An iPad-portrait design for the topic page: the inspector and the spine as overlay sheets, the tools in the header.
- A shared study-space hero with a path strip of one fixed height for CS/Maths/IT as well as Code, Japanese, Mind and Creatives.


---

## SY — Background redraws / cloud sync disrupting work (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** The iPad resets the user reported are real and systemic. Every applied cloud state (clean pull, three-way merge, and the push that loses its compare-and-set race because another device pushed first) ends in cloudsync.rerenderCurrent() -> KOS.rerender(), which fully remounts #main with no check of what changed or whether the user is mid-task; the only guard is the ink pen (1.5 s). Pulls fire on Realtime notes from any other device, on every return to the app (visibility/focus) and after the iPad's own ratings, so redraws are frequent. A remount loses everything held in render closures: the CS/Maths/IT note page (the study spaces store theirs and survive), the flashcard queue and session counts (the batch is logged only at finish, so pay/streak for ratings before the redraw is lost), quiz and exam progress and typed answers, Japanese session results, Code listing edits and retrieval tries/hints/clock, open menus, focus/keyboard, and scroll: rerender saves #main.scrollTop but #stage is the real scroller, so every redraw jumps to the top on all three devices, and Side by side panes reset too. The same blanket KOS.rerender is used as a change broadcast by the Daily Review close, routine auto-ticks and boot loaders, while Google/Todoist pulls refresh nothing. Fix design: a redraw policy (background changes carry the changed keys; views declare what they read; redraws are deferred while a dialog/menu/editable focus/active study engine/recent interaction exists, with a quiet Refresh chip), per-view transient state kept per device in state.ui as the Code/Japanese/Mind/Creatives lesson pages already do, partial review batches banked on unmount, and rerender preserving the real scroller, pane scrolls and focus.

**Coverage.** Read the redraw paths in js/core/ui.js (KOS.rerender, KOS.show), cloudsync.js (every rerenderCurrent caller, Realtime, visibility/focus/interval, the CAS conflict path), routines.js, dayreview.js, ext.js, main.js, achievements, focus, mobile-shell, the ink listeners, and the engines (flashcards, quiz, jarun, Code retrieval, the codetopic/jatopic/crtopic/mindtopic page memos). Reproduced with KOS.rerender() (exactly what cloudsync calls) on the seeded account at ipad, ipadl, mac and iphone: notes page, flashcards (topic and Due today), quiz, exam typed answer, Code 1.1 listings, quick-note focus, bell menu, assignment dialog focus, Daily Review close, Side by side ink panes, scroll on topic/Home/Anime; toast geometry on iphone and ipad. Screenshots: shots/sync-disruption/review-close-notes-reset.png, shots/sync-disruption/toast-over-rate-iphone.png. NOT reproduced end to end: a real two-device Supabase pull (no cloud sign-in in the harness; the chain is code-read), a routine auto-tick redraw (a hand-made ledger entry did not tick a habit), a Japanese session with due items (none due in the sample), the Creatives Writing Desk (autosaves at 600 ms), the Focus stage and exam paper tally (overlays outside #main, judged safe from code). The empty account was not exercised separately: with no cloud link it has no pull-triggered redraws and the remount behaviour is identical. Themes are not relevant to this area and were not checked.

### SY-01 · P1 · bug — KOS.rerender() never keeps the scroll position: it saves #main.scrollTop but #stage is the scroller, so every cloud pull jumps the page to the top

- **Devices:** all (mac, ipad, iphone)
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.rerender (the cloud-pull/background redraw path) reads and restores document.getElementById('main').scrollTop, but the element that actually scrolls is #stage (#main has overflow visible; on the iPad #main.scrollHeight 2406 vs #stage 2406/1120 scrolling). KOS.show then empties #main, the height collapses and the browser clamps #stage.scrollTop to 0. Repro (seeded account): open compsci 4.5.2.1, set #stage.scrollTop=900, KOS.rerender() -> 0. Same on Home (700 -> 0) and the Anime vault (1500 -> 0) at mac, ipad and iphone sizes. So any pull, merge, auto-ticked habit, Daily Review close etc. throws the reader back to the top of the page.
- **Evidence:** harness eval sd3.js: ipad {st1:900,st2:0,h1:700,h2:0,a1:1500,a2:0}; mac {st1:530,st2:0,...}; iphone same. Code: js/core/ui.js:353-356 (var main=getElementById('main'); var top=main.scrollTop; ... main.scrollTop=top); KOS.show resets main.scrollTop=0 at js/core/ui.js:1526
- **Root cause:** rerender measured the wrong element after the shell moved scrolling to #stage
- **Proposed fix:** Save/restore the real scroller (#stage, or a KOS.shell.scroller() helper that returns the element whose overflow scrolls, including the phone shell) and restore AFTER the view has rendered (and once more after async content lands, e.g. on next rAF / after vault lazy batch). Better: part of the redraw policy below — background redraws should not happen while the user is reading.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-02 · P1 · bug — CS/Maths/IT notes reset to page 1 on every background redraw (the reported 'notes page 4 jumps back to page 1')

- **Devices:** all; most visible on iPad
- **Detail / repro:** In a subject topic the current note page lives in a closure variable (`var notePage = 0` inside the topic view). KOS.rerender re-runs the view, so the variable is recreated at 0. hub.js keeps only the open TAB across a redraw (openTabMemo), not the page. Repro on iPad: open compsci 4.5.2.1, Next page x3 -> 'Page 4 of 7', KOS.rerender() -> 'Page 1 of 7'. Code, Japanese, Mind and Creatives lessons do NOT have this bug because they store the page in state.ui.<space>.page[ref].
- **Evidence:** harness eval: {before:'Page 4 of 7', after:'Page 1 of 7'} at --size ipad. js/modules/hub.js:2901 (var notePage = 0), :2983 (cur = o.keep ? notePage : 0), :2590 (only the tab survives KOS.ui.redrawing). Contrast js/modules/codetopic.js:342/377, jatopic.js:206/229, crtopic.js:154/171, mindtopic.js:261/282 (ui.page[ref])
- **Root cause:** transient reader state held in a view-render closure
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the note page in a module-level memo keyed by sid:ref (like openTabMemo) or per device in state.ui.notePage[sid:ref], and read it when KOS.ui.redrawing (and arguably on return to the topic). Same pattern the study spaces already use.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-03 · P1 · bug — A background redraw mid-flashcards restarts the set at card 1 and silently drops the cards already rated from the session ledger (no XP, no streak credit)

- **Devices:** all; reported on iPad
- **Detail / repro:** The flashcard engine keeps queue, graduated and counts in its mount closure, and only logs the review session in finish(). A KOS.rerender mid-set remounts it: on a topic's Flashcards tab the queue is rebuilt from ALL the topic's cards, so the learner is put back on card 1 and re-meets cards they just rated (re-rating them applies FSRS's short-term rule again, skewing their schedule); on Due today the counts reset ('This session: 0 again · 0 hard · 3 good' -> ''), and because a session is only logged at finish(), the ratings made before the redraw never produce a session entry — their XP/gold and the study-streak credit are lost (the SRS schedule itself was saved). Repro iPad: compsci 4.5.2.1 Flashcards, rate 3 Good ('15 left of 18'), KOS.rerender() -> '18 left of 18', first card again. Due today: rate 3 ('304 left of 307', life line 3 good) -> after rerender '304 left of 304', life line empty.
- **Evidence:** harness eval (ipad): topic log ['18 left of 18 What is a number base?', ..., '15 left of 18'] -> afterFront '18 left of 18 What is a number base?'; due {d3life:'This session: 0 again · 0 hard · 3 good · 0 easy', dAfterLife:''}. js/engines/flashcards.js:295-322 (rate keeps counts in closure, store.save only), :325-355 (finish is the only KOS.sessions.log), :356 restart
- **Root cause:** session progress and the pay-at-finish rule live in DOM-bound closures
- **Proposed fix:** (1) Never redraw under an active study session (redraw policy below). (2) Keep an in-progress review session in a per-device session store (state.ui.fcSession[deckKey] = {queue keys, graduated, counts, startedAt}) and resume it on remount. (3) Log/bank the partial batch on unmount/pagehide (like Focus banks its clock, invariant 4c) so pay never depends on the DOM surviving.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SY-04 · P1 · bug — Quiz progress and a typed exam answer are wiped by any background redraw

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Topic Quiz (MCQ) and Exam tabs keep the question index, chosen answers and typed text in engine closures. Repro iPad: compsci 4.5.2.1 Quiz, answer two -> 'Question 3 of 11'; KOS.rerender() -> 'Question 1 of 11' (tab kept, progress lost). Exam tab: go to 'Question 3 of 14', type into the answer textarea, KOS.rerender() -> 'Question 1 of 14' and the textarea is empty: the typed answer is lost with no recovery.
- **Evidence:** harness eval sd1.js (ipad): {quizBefore:'Question 3 of 11', quizAfter:'Question 1 of 11', examBefore:'Question 3 of 14', examTA:true, examAfter:'Question 1 of 14', examTAAfter:''}. js/engines/quiz.js:62-121 (MCQ state in closure), :225-232 (exam 'at' index in closure); js/modules/hub.js:3044-3084 remounts them
- **Root cause:** engine state in closures + unconditional rerender
- **Proposed fix:** Persist the quiz/exam position and drafts per device (state.ui.quizRun[sid:ref] = {at, picks, drafts}) like Code drafts, and restore on remount; plus the redraw policy (no background redraw while a study engine is mounted and touched).
- **Flags:** effort M

### SY-05 · P1 · arch — Every cloud pull/merge redraws the active view unconditionally — even when nothing the view shows changed, and even while the user is mid-task

- **Devices:** iPad primarily (app switching + Pencil study), all
- **Detail / repro:** cloudsync.rerenderCurrent() calls KOS.rerender() after every applied remote state: the clean path (applyRemoteState, cloudsync.js:775), the merge path (mergeRemoteIn, :495, also used by a push that lost its compare-and-set race, :533-560) and first link (:1279). There is no check of whether the merged document differs in anything the current view reads, no check that the user is interacting, a dialog/menu is open, an input has focus, or a study engine is running. The only guard in KOS.rerender is the ink pen (ui.js:347). Triggers that reach it: another device's push -> Realtime 'changed' (1.5 s debounce, :1483); returning to the app (visibilitychange/focus, :1548-1552, at most once a minute) — on the iPad every app switch; the 15-min interval; and the iPad's OWN ratings: each store.save -> noteChange -> push after 4 s -> if the Mac/iPhone pushed meanwhile the CAS fails -> conflict() -> mergeRemoteIn -> rerender. An idle open Mac pushes on its own (achievement sweep every 30 min, goals/notify ticks, routine auto-ticks, Daily Review words), so the iPad is redrawn under the user regularly. This is the root of the reported iPad resets.
- **Evidence:** js/core/cloudsync.js:487-497 (mergeRemoteIn -> rerenderCurrent), :723-733 (rerenderCurrent -> KOS.rerender), :773-777, :533-560 (conflict path), :1447-1459 (noteChange push), :1480-1495 (Realtime), :1541-1553 (interval/visible/focus); js/core/ui.js:344-357 (rerender: only inkUI.busy guard). Harness: KOS.rerender() reproduces every reset in the other findings.
- **Root cause:** full remount used as the generic refresh for background state changes, with no busy/changed gating
- **Proposed fix:** Systemic redraw policy: (1) cloudsync passes what changed (top-level keys that differ between pre- and post-merge state, cheap JSON compare per key) to KOS.rerender({changed:[...], background:true}); views declare the state keys they read (KOS.views.x.reads = ['srs','edits',...]) and are skipped when untouched. (2) A background redraw is DEFERRED while the page is 'busy': a dialog or menu open, focus in an input/textarea/contenteditable, a study engine mounted and touched in the last N minutes (flashcards/quiz/exam/japanese session/code task), pointer down, or within 30 s of the last interaction — instead show a quiet 'Updated on another device · Refresh' chip, and redraw on the next navigation. (3) For views that must refresh live, give them a refresh(changed) hook that patches in place instead of a full remount.
- **Flags:** effort L

### SY-06 · P1 · ux — Closing the Daily Review sheet (Later, Esc, Close) redraws the page underneath — and on the iPad it can auto-open over a lesson when you return to the app

- **Devices:** iPad (visibility), all
- **Detail / repro:** reviewSheet.close() calls KOS.rerender() whenever it is not silent (dayreview.js:92), as does the final Close button (:661) and Skip (:709). The overlay is outside #main, so the page under it never needed a redraw; the redraw resets notes page, flashcards, quiz, scroll. Repro iPad: compsci 4.5.4.3 notes page 4 ('Page 4 of 7'), KOS.reviewSheet.open(slot), close -> 'Page 1 of 7' (screenshot). Worse, maybeOpen() runs on every visibilitychange to visible (:749); the deep-link guard uses bootHash, which is cleared 1.5 s after boot (:746), so after boot the sheet auto-opens over ANY page (a topic, a Code lesson, Due today) when the iPad app is brought back inside a new review slot; the blocked() check knows only Focus, a dialog and the Exam Room. Dismissing it then wipes the lesson state.
- **Evidence:** shots/sync-disruption/review-close-notes-reset.png (Page 1 of 7 after close; was Page 4 of 7). eval sd8.js {before:'Page 4 of 7', open:true, after:'Page 1 of 7'}. js/modules/dayreview.js:85-93, :661, :709, :722-729 (blocked), :744-749
- **Root cause:** overlay close uses KOS.rerender as a blanket refresh; auto-open guard only considers boot deep links
- **Proposed fix:** Close should repaint only what the review changed (chip, Home's cards if Home is on screen) — or call the policy's refresh(changed) — never a full remount. Auto-open on visibility should only happen on Home (or when no study page/engine is active); elsewhere it waits in the chip.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-07 · P1 · bug — Handwritten tab Side by side: both panes jump to the top on a background redraw (1.5 s after the pen lifts)

- **Devices:** iPad landscape (Side by side is >1080 only)
- **Detail / repro:** The ink guard in KOS.rerender only waits while inkUI.busy() (a stroke or text box open, or ink in the last 1.5 s). Once the pen has been lifted for 1.5 s — i.e. while the student reads the notes pane or thinks — a pull redraws the tab and both independent scroll regions reset. Repro iPad landscape (1180x820): compsci 4.5.4.3, Handwritten, Side by side; ink.side-notes scrolled to 600 and ink.side-page to 238; KOS.rerender() -> both 0. The notebook page-in-view and the notes position are lost, exactly the 'jumps back to the start' symptom on the iPad.
- **Evidence:** eval sd11.js (ipadl): {scrollers:['ink.side-notes 7882/744','ink.side-page 982/744'], before:[600,238], after:['ink.side-notes 0','ink.side-page 0']}. js/core/ui.js:347-351 (busy guard only), js/core/inkui.js:341
- **Root cause:** rerender only preserves horizontal scroller memos and the (wrong) #main scrollTop
- **Proposed fix:** Remember scroll of every [data-scroll-keep]/pane scroller (extend noteScrolls, ui.js:686, to vertical scrollTop for declared panes) and restore after render; better, defer background redraws while the Handwritten tab is open at all (ink pages and notes never change from a state pull — inksync updates pages itself).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-08 · P1 · ux — A background redraw steals focus from whatever is being typed in — the iPad/iPhone keyboard closes mid-sentence

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone (keyboard), Mac
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.rerender remounts #main; the focused field is destroyed and focus falls to <body>. The quick-note draft itself survives (stored per device), but the caret is gone, so on the iPad the on-screen keyboard dismisses while the user is typing; any keystrokes in flight are lost. The same applies to every inline input on any page (search filters in vaults, Money amount fields, Goals editor in-page fields, Code task editor, Writing Desk). Repro iPad: compsci 4.5.4.3, focus the quick note, type, caret at 4, KOS.rerender() -> document.activeElement BODY; the new textarea has the text but no focus (selection at end).
- **Evidence:** eval (ipad): {active:'BODY ', val:'half a thou', sel:11}. js/core/ui.js:344-357, :1520-1541 (main.innerHTML = '' then remount); js/core/a11y.js:97 comment says a redraw must leave the reader where they are but there is no focus restore
- **Root cause:** remount without focus/selection preservation
- **Proposed fix:** Policy: never run a background redraw while document.activeElement is an editable element inside #main (defer until blur + idle). As a fallback, record a focus signature (data-ui + index + selection) before the redraw and restore it after (mobile-shell.js:600-640 already has calendarFocusSignature to generalise).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-09 · P2 · bug — Japanese review/learn session restarts and loses its unlogged results on a background redraw

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.jaRun.run keeps `results` and the index in its closure and pays only at the end (KOS.ja.finishSession(results)). A KOS.rerender on #/japanese/session/... re-runs run(): the list is rebuilt from KOS.ja.due() (facets already graded drop out) and the results so far are discarded, so the +1 XP per passed facet for them is never logged. Mind's Daily 15 does it right: its d.results are saved in state and logged once (mind.js:594/662).
- **Evidence:** js/modules/jarun.js:361-390 (run, `var results = [], i = 0`), :434 (finishSession only at end); js/modules/japanese.js:305 (view calls run on every render); contrast js/modules/mind.js:594,662
- **Root cause:** session state in closure + pay at finish
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the running session (keys, index, results) per device in state.ui.japanese.run and resume on remount, or bank results incrementally; plus the redraw policy.
- **Flags:** effort M

### SY-10 · P2 · ux — A background redraw closes any open menu or popover — including the header bell, which is outside the page

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.show always calls closeOpenMenu(false) before drawing, also for a redraw (_nav). So a pull arriving while the notifications popover, a Filters menu or a ⋯ menu is open makes it vanish under the finger. Repro: Home, open the bell (menu .k-menu-panel.k-nt-pop present), KOS.rerender() -> menu gone.
- **Evidence:** eval sd6.js (ipad): {menuOpen:true, menuCls:'k-menu-panel k-nt-pop', menuAfter:false}. js/core/ui.js:1513-1519 (closeOpenMenu before every render)
- **Root cause:** navigation clean-up applied to redraws
- **Proposed fix:** On a redraw (KOS.ui.redrawing) keep a menu whose anchor is outside #main, and for menus inside #main defer the background redraw while a menu is open (redraw policy).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-11 · P2 · bug — Edits in a Code lesson's runnable listings are lost on a background redraw

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** A Code lesson's notes listings are editable (code.editor-input) so the learner can experiment, but their text is not kept anywhere; task editors save drafts (debounced 400 ms, state.ui.code.drafts) and survive. Repro iPad: code 1.1, type into the three listing editors, wait 900 ms, KOS.rerender() -> all three back to the shipped code (the quick-note textarea survives).
- **Evidence:** eval sd5.js: {n:4, where:['code.editor-input'x3,'topic.quick-note'], kept:[false,false,false,true]}. js/modules/code.js:629-632 (only tasks call setDraft); js/core/code.js:612-621
- **Root cause:** listing editor state only in the DOM
- **Proposed fix:** Keep listing edits as drafts too (key `listing:<lesson>:<i>`, cleared by 'Reset'), or defer redraws while an editor has focus.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-12 · P2 · ux — routines.onSession redraws the whole page whenever an auto-tracked habit ticks itself — e.g. right after a flashcard batch or Code task pass

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every logged session (flashcard batch finish, exam item marked, Code task pass, Japanese session) calls routines.onSession, which runs autoSync() and, if any auto-tracked habit ticked, KOS.rerender() (only guarded against an open dialog). So the moment a 'Review 20 cards' or '1 Code task passed' habit completes, the page that caused it is remounted: the flashcards 'Session complete' summary is replaced by a fresh deck, a Code task's passing result panel is redrawn, an exam tab returns to question 1. Only the habit chip/Home needs to change.
- **Evidence:** js/core/routines.js:697-700 (onSession -> autoSync -> KOS.rerender), :672-690 (autoSync), js/engines/flashcards.js:349 (session logged at finish), js/engines/quiz.js:428 (exam item logs a session). Not reproduced end-to-end in the harness (could not get an auto habit to tick with a hand-made ledger entry); path read from code.
- **Root cause:** blanket rerender as change notification
- **Proposed fix:** Replace the blanket rerender with a 'routines changed' event that Home/Routines/the header chip listen to; never remount a study view for it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-13 · P2 · ui — Toasts are capped at half the viewport width and, on the iPhone, sit on top of the flashcard rating buttons (and catch taps when they carry Undo)

- **Devices:** iPhone, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** #toast is positioned with inset-inline-start:50% + translate(-50%), so its shrink-to-fit width is limited to the space right of the centre line: 201 px on the iPhone (402/2) and 410 px on the iPad (820/2). A one-line message wraps to four lines (98 px tall). On the iPhone it sits 12 px above the tab bar, i.e. exactly over the Hard/Good rating buttons of a flashcard (toast 101,708 201x98 vs rate row 16,694 370x60: overlapping), and an action toast has pointer-events:auto, so a rating tap lands on the toast. Flashcards themselves raise toasts mid-set (leech, 'Type your answer first').
- **Evidence:** shots/sync-disruption/toast-over-rate-iphone.png; eval sd10.js iphone {toast:[101,708,201,98], rate:[16,694,370,60], overlap:true, pe:'auto'}, ipad {toast:[205,1092,410,64]}. css/components.css:389-408, :669
- **Root cause:** left:50% shrink-to-fit width; fixed bottom offset ignores page controls
- **Proposed fix:** Centre with inset-inline:0; margin-inline:auto; inline-size:fit-content (keep max-inline-size), and on study pages lift the toast above the active control row (or place it top-centre on phones while a study engine is mounted).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-14 · P2 · data — A redraw mid-retrieval resets the Code retrieval card's tries, hint flag and clock, so the derived grade comes out better than earned

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Code retrieval grades are derived from tries, hints and time (invariant 107, never self-reported), but the card keeps `started`, `tries` and `hinted` in its render closure. After a failed try and an opened hint, a background redraw remounts the card with tries=0, hinted=false and a fresh clock; passing then records a clean, fast first-try grade and schedules the card too far out. Task timers (code.js:601) reset the same way.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/code.js:972 (var started = Date.now(), tries = 0, hinted = false), :984 (clock), :601-611 (task clock). Code-read; the remount path is the same KOS.rerender reproduced in the other findings.
- **Root cause:** evidence inputs held in closures
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the in-progress attempt (tries, hinted, startedAt) per card in state.ui.code.attempt[key] until it is graded, and defer background redraws while a retrieval is open.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-15 · P3 · a11y — After a background redraw the open dialog's opener is detached, so closing it drops focus to <body>

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Dialogs survive a redraw (they live on body), but KOS.ui.openDialog restores focus to the opener, which the redraw replaced. Repro: Assignments, '＋ New assignment', type a title, KOS.rerender() (dialog and text survive), Cancel -> document.activeElement is BODY; the keyboard/VoiceOver user is thrown to the top of the document. cloudsync does not check for an open dialog (routines.onSession does).
- **Evidence:** eval sd7.js: {dlgAfter:true, inpAfter:'Typed title', openerConnected:false, focusAfterClose:'BODY'}. js/core/routines.js:699 has the dialog guard; js/core/cloudsync.js:723-733 has none
- **Root cause:** redraw under an open dialog
- **Proposed fix:** Defer background redraws while KOS.ui.topDialog() (policy), and in openDialog's restore fall back to an element with the same data-ui/text in the new DOM (as mobile-shell's calendarFocusSignature already does, mobile-shell.js:620-640).
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-16 · P3 · ux — Boot-time redraws of whatever page the PWA reopened on (Google read-only copy, own art pictures, sample seed)

- **Devices:** iPad (PWA restore), all
- **Detail / repro:** Three boot paths call KOS.rerender() on the current page after async work: KOS.ext boot when Google Calendar is connected (ext.js:364, after loadRo from IndexedDB), KOS.art.loadOwn (main.js:307) and the sample seed (main.js:299-304). The iPad Home Screen app restores the last route (e.g. a topic), so the page the user has just started reading or tapping is remounted a moment after launch, for data (Google's read-only chips, decorative art) that only Calendar or a painted slot needs.
- **Evidence:** js/core/ext.js:364; js/main.js:299-307
- **Root cause:** KOS.rerender used as a broadcast
- **Proposed fix:** Have these refresh only the views that read them (Calendar for loadRo; the art slots via KOS.art.apply/paint, which artslot.js already does in place) instead of a full remount.
- **Flags:** effort S

### SY-17 · P3 · arch — Inconsistent refresh policy: Google/Todoist pulls change Calendar and Reminders without redrawing them, while a state pull redraws every page

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.ext.syncAll (every cycle and the 1.5 s 'soon' sync) writes pulled events and reminders through the owners, then only notifies KOS.ext.onChange listeners — only the Connections page listens. An open Calendar or Reminders page keeps showing the old data (a task completed in Todoist stays unticked on screen) until the user navigates, whereas a cloud state pull remounts any page however unrelated. There is no single 'state changed → which views care' mechanism.
- **Evidence:** js/core/ext.js:325-337 (syncAll -> changed()), :294; js/modules/connections.js:173-177 (the only listener); js/core/extgcal.js and exttasks.js contain no redraw
- **Root cause:** no domain-scoped change notification
- **Proposed fix:** Introduce one change bus (KOS.changed(domains)) fired by cloudsync (with the keys that differed), ext, routines, inksync, files; views subscribe by domain and refresh in place (or defer while busy).
- **Flags:** effort M

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Show a quiet 'Updated from another device' chip with Refresh instead of redrawing a busy page
- Remember the CS/Maths/IT note page per topic (like the study spaces) so returning to a topic lands where you left off
- Keep a partly rated flashcard batch resumable from the Due today chip after leaving the page

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- Domain-scoped change bus (KOS.changed(domains)) replacing KOS.rerender as a broadcast; views refresh in place
- Per-view session store for transient study state, so engines resume after any remount, reload or iPad app kill
- Smoke test: for each study view set transient state, call KOS.rerender(), assert page, card, question, scroll and focus survive


---

## CM — A-Level Maths content quality (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** All 89 Maths leaves (P1.1–P10.5, S1.1–S5.3, M6.1–M9.1) have notes, flashcards, a quiz and exam items. No leaf is missing. All 38,310 $…$ maths segments render in KaTeX 0.16.11 without an error. The shipped M7.3 is not bare: it has 8 note pages, about 1,770 words, 15 worked examples, 4 figures, 21 flashcards, 13 quiz items and 15 exam items, and all of it renders on the Mac and the iPhone. Two things can still make it look bare. First, its Specification tab is mangled PDF-extraction text. Second, if the user forked M7.3 before the Mechanics rewrite (4d791ca), that fork still hides the new notes, and no notice says so (edits.js does not check whether a fork is out of date). The most important root causes:
(1) The generated spec spine js/data/maths.js holds garbled text from PDF extraction. At least 36 of the 89 leaves have broken fragments, and 25 still contain "need to learn:".
(2) The quiz never shuffles its options, and the authored answers sit at A or B 95% of the time. Of 498 base items, 380 have the answer at A; of 560 bank items, 381 have it at B.
(3) The bank-*.js files were added on top of the base files without a duplicate check. That gives 17 exact repeats and about 45 groups of cards with the same answer inside one leaf.
(4) The gen_data.py SHORT_S table never reached the stats/mech titles, so every S and M leaf is titled with an 80-character spec sentence cut off with "…".
(5) Depth is very uneven. The thinnest base notes are 8.4 (~200 words), 8.1 (~260) and 6.1 (~330), against 8,667 words for 4.1. The core skills leaves 7.2, 8.2, 6.3 and 8.6 are each a few hundred words.
(6) The worked card's "Write it out" chip is absolutely positioned and overlaps the card title on every device, and it is only 23px tall.

**Coverage.** Content: I loaded every Maths content file and both banks in Node, in index.html's load order (script audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/cm/load.js). I measured every leaf: pages, blocks, words, worked and exam-tagged worked examples, figures, callouts, long paragraphs, flashcards, quiz items, exam items (base, bank and derived), and the position of the quiz answer (cm/metrics.js → cm/metrics.json).
Near-duplicate detection used two methods: token Jaccard (cm/dups.js) and canonical-LaTeX character 3-grams plus same-answer groups (cm/dups2.js → cm/dups2.json). I checked quiz structure and formatting (odd $ or **, LaTeX outside $, long answers). All maths segments were validated in KaTeX in the browser. I dumped and read every leaf's spec spine (cm/spine.txt).
Correctness: I hand-checked about 150 flashcards and quiz items in M7.3, 6.1, 8.1, M8.3, 5.2, S4.3, 9.3, S2.3, M8.6, 4.3 and 10.4, recomputing the numbers. I found one wrong answer.
Visual: M7.3 Notes, Spec and the past-paper page on Mac (Graphite); M7.3 Notes pages 1 and 6 on iPhone; the M7.3 quiz on iPad portrait (Dawn); 8.4 Notes full page on Mac; 5.6 Spec on Mac; M7.2 Notes figure on Mac in Dawn. I measured the Write-it-out geometry at mac, ipad, ipadl and iphone.
Not examined: I did not hand-verify every numeric answer in all 1,058 quiz items and 1,500+ flashcards; there was no automated maths checker. I did not read exam-item mark schemes beyond the derivation code. I did not test a shop theme or the seeded account; Maths content does not depend on account data apart from forks. I could not see the user's own state.edits, so the fork theory for "M7.3 is bare" is inferred from code and git history, not observed.

### CM-01 · P1 · content — Specification tab shows garbled PDF-extraction text on most Maths leaves (M7.3 included)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Spec tab is drawn from js/data/maths.js content/info lines, which are raw, broken PDF extraction. Examples: M7.3 guidance reads "v = u + at r = ut +1at2 / e.g. , with vectors / 2 given in i - j or column vector form". 5.6 reads "Understand and use double a n g le f / rm u l a e ; u s e / f s in ( A ± B ), fo r m u l a e fo r / ± ± cos (A B)". 2.1 reads "× = , ÷ = - , = m n n am / The equivalence ofa and should be known." 2.10 starts mid-sentence: "into partial fractions". 25 leaves still contain the extraction artefact "need to learn:" or "students need to learn:". A heuristic found fragment lines in 36 of the 89 leaves: 1.1 2.1 2.2 2.3 2.4 2.5 2.7 2.9 3.3 4.1 4.2 4.3 5.1 5.2 5.3 5.5 5.6 5.7 6.2 6.3 6.4 7.1 7.2 7.4 8.2 8.3 8.5 8.6 9.4 10.4 S2.3 S4.1 S5.3 M7.4 M8.3 M8.6, and reading the dump shows more. Exponents, fractions, sums and integrals are lost throughout. This is very likely part of why M7.3 looks bare.
- **Evidence:** shots/content-maths/m73-spec-mac.png; shots/content-maths/p5-6-spec-mac.png; cm/spine.txt (every leaf's spec text); tools/gen_data.py:240-255 (maths_group copies clean(x) of the extracted lines)
- **Root cause:** The spec spine is generated from a PDF text extraction (/home/claude/extract) that splits equations and kerned headings, and no override or cleanup pass exists for Maths.
- **Proposed fix:** Hand-author the 89 Maths spec entries from the Edexcel 9MA0 PDF as typeset blocks (LaTeX in $…$). Either fix the extractor or add a checked-in override table that gen_data.py applies (as SHORT does), and regenerate. Drop the "need to learn:" headers. Add a smoke test that refuses fragment lines (≤4 characters, runs of single letters, "need to learn").
- **Flags:** effort L

### CM-02 · P1 · content — Quiz answers are almost always A or B, and options are never shuffled

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Of 1,058 Maths quiz items, the correct option is A in 531 and B in 481. Only 44 are C and 2 are D. Base files: 380 A, 100 B, 17 C, 1 D. Bank files: 151 A, 381 B, 27 C, 1 D. KOS.quiz.mountMCQ renders opts in authored order, and its Shuffle button only reorders the questions. A learner soon learns to pick A on a base item or B on a bank item, so the quiz score stops measuring understanding. Separately, the correct option is uniquely the longest in 317 items (30%).
- **Evidence:** cm/metrics.json ansDist per leaf; js/engines/quiz.js:66 (Shuffle shuffles order only), :88-96 (item.opts rendered in order, answer compared to item.ans at :113); e.g. js/data/content/maths-mech-m7.js:305-306 (every item ans: 0); shots/content-maths/m73-quiz-ipad.png (Q1 answer A)
- **Root cause:** Authors put the right answer first (bank authors second), and the engine trusts the authored order.
- **Proposed fix:** In mountMCQ, shuffle a per-question option permutation at mount and on Retry, seeded per attempt, and map clicks back to the original index so the stats and the why text stay valid. Optionally also rebalance the authored ans positions so a fixed order is fair too.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-03 · P2 · ui — "Write it out" chip overlaps worked-example titles on every device and is only 23px tall

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, ipadl, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** On worked cards with more than one step, the "Write it out" pill is position:absolute at the top-right, and the .k-n-worked-head row reserves no room for it. On M7.3's notes it lies over the title or src text on 4 of 15 cards on the Mac (1470), 2 of 15 on iPad portrait, 3 of 15 on iPad landscape and 9 of 15 on iPhone (402). Example on the Mac: "Write it out" is printed over "AS Specimen · P2 Q1(a)(b) · 5 marks". On iPhone it covers "Find the initial velocity". The pill measures 81×23 px with min-block-size:0, below the 44px touch minimum (invariant 69). The same overlap affects every subject's worked cards, because content.js is shared.
- **Evidence:** shots/content-maths/writeout-overlap-mac.png; shots/content-maths/m73-notes-p6-iphone.png; measured: btn rect [233,618,81,23] while .k-n-worked-head starts at y=626, width 334 (iphone); css/views/ink.css:300; js/core/content.js:373; css/views/study.css:346
- **Root cause:** An absolutely positioned control added onto a flex header that wraps freely.
- **Proposed fix:** Put the chip in the head's flex flow (margin-inline-start:auto, before Copy) instead of absolute positioning, or give the head padding-inline-end equal to the chip's and Copy's widths. Raise the hit area to 44px for coarse pointers.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-04 · P2 · arch — A curriculum fork silently hides later curriculum upgrades (plausible cause of "M7.3 is basically bare")

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.content.get and srs read state.edits first (invariant 86). A fork copies the shipped material at the moment of the first edit and is never compared with the curriculum again. M7.3 was an outline entry until commit 4d791ca ("load M6–M7 at depth, drop their outline entries"), and a9f64b5 rewrote M7.2 and M7.3. A Notes fork made before then (or one carried over from S7.3 by the ref rename, router.js:117 renamedRef) keeps showing the old, thin outline notes. Nothing tells the user that a newer, much deeper version exists. The only sign is the Edit button's data-state forked (hub.js:2828). The shipped M7.3 renders in full (8 pages), so a bare M7.3 on the user's device most likely comes from such a fork.
- **Evidence:** js/core/edits.js:1-60 (no base hash or staleness check: grep for stale/baseHash finds nothing); git log -- js/data/content/maths-mech-m7.js (a9f64b5, 4d791ca); shots/content-maths/m73-mac.png (shipped content present)
- **Root cause:** The fork model has no notion of the base version it was taken from.
- **Proposed fix:** Record a hash of the shipped kind on each fork (fork.base). When the shipped hash differs, show a banner on the tab: "The curriculum for this topic was updated after you edited it: View new · Keep mine · Merge (append your rows)". Check the user's real state for state.edits.topics['maths:M7.3'] (or 'maths:S7.3').
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### CM-05 · P2 · content — Bank files duplicate base flashcards and quiz items in the same leaf (17 exact repeats, about 45 same-answer groups)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** bank-maths-*.js were added without a duplicate check against the base items. Exact repeats in the same quiz include: 2.1 "$8^{2/3}$ =" (qz0 and qz8); 4.1 "$\binom92$" (qz0 and qz18), "Coefficient of $x$ in $(2+x)^5$" (qz8 and qz22), "Coefficient of $x^2$ in $(1+x)(1+4x+6x^2)$" (qz10 and qz24), "$(1+3x)^{-1}$ valid for" (qz4 and qz20); 5.2 "$\sin3\theta\approx$" and "$1-\cos2\theta\approx$" both twice; 5.3 "$\tan\frac{3\pi}4$" twice; 5.6 "$\cos(A-B)$" twice; 6.3 "$\log_{10}0.001$" twice; 2.6 "$(x-2)$ is a factor means" twice; 2.8 "inverse only if one-to-one" twice; 7.2 "$d/dx\sqrt x$" twice. Flashcard and quiz pairs on the same fact: M7.3 "Which equation has no t?" (fc1 and qz10, and qz1 again); M7.5 time of flight and greatest height; 8.5 "∫ln x"; 7.5 "d/dx(3y²)"; 3.3 parametric dy/dx; 10.3 midpoint; 8.1 states the FTC twice (fc0 and fc5b) and uses the same f′ setup twice (fc4 and fc6b); 6.1 has "$e^{2x}=5$" as fc10b and qz3, "features of $a^x$" as fc0 and fc7b, and the asymptotes of $e^x+3$ and $e^x+2$ as qz0 and qz6b; 10.4 has "|AC| = 4 with one unknown" three times.
