Cross-leaf: S4.2 and S4.3 repeat continuity-correction cards, and S4.1 and S5.2 repeat "$P(X\ge k)$ in cumulative form". Full list: cm/dups2.json (66 within-leaf pairs, 14 cross-leaf pairs, 45 same-answer groups).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/cm/dups2.json; e.g. js/data/content/maths-pure-p8.js:505 vs bank-maths-pure-c.js:457 ("Before integrating x²/(x−1) you should")
- **Root cause:** KOS.content.extend concatenates bank arrays blindly, and no test checks for duplicates.
- **Proposed fix:** Delete or replace the bank items flagged in dups2.json with new item shapes. Add a smoke test that canonicalises q and the answer (\dfrac→\frac, strip spaces) and fails on an exact or same-answer duplicate inside one leaf. Removing flashcards must follow invariant 87 (carry the original index as a third element).
- **Flags:** effort M

### CM-06 · P2 · content — Thin notes on core Pure and Applied leaves (depth very uneven: 200 to 8,700 words)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Base notes (before the bank's past-paper page), as approximate words / pages / worked examples: 8.4 Integration as a limit of a sum 204/1/2; 8.1 Fundamental Theorem 258/1/2; 6.1 Exponential functions 331/2/2; 6.2 Gradient of e^kx 350/1/2 (no page breaks at all); 6.5 Solving a^x=b 401/2/4; 8.6 Integration with partial fractions 414/2/3; 4.3 Sigma 452/2/3; 4.6 Series in modelling 479/2/3 (no exam-tagged worked examples, so only 2 exam items); 7.2 Differentiating x^n, e^kx, trig, ln x 486/2/4; 6.3 Logarithms 489/2/4; M8.3 Weight 558/3/4 (3 exam items); 5.4 Reciprocal and inverse trig 563; S4.3 Selecting a distribution 587 (4 exam items); 8.8 617; 6.4 627; 3.4 632; 10.4 636/1/3; 8.2 Integrating standard functions 637; 9.5 773/1. The median leaf is about 1,040 words, and 4.1 has 8,667 words and 31 worked examples. 7.2 and 8.2 carry most of Paper 1 and 2 calculus but have the depth of a summary. Leaves with 5 or fewer exam items: 2.11 3.4 4.6 5.3 5.4 6.1 6.2 8.1 8.4 8.6 8.8 9.5 S4.3 M8.1 M8.3 M8.5.
- **Evidence:** cm/metrics.json; table printed by cm/metrics.js; shots/content-maths/p8-4-notes-mac.png (8.4 is one short page)
- **Root cause:** The depth rewrite stopped short on P6 to P8 and several small leaves. The files for P1, P9, P10 and M7 to M9 are dated 8 October and P2 to P8 27 October, and the light leaves were never brought up to the contract.
- **Proposed fix:** Run the per-section depth pipeline used for 4.1, 2.x and M7 on P6 (6.1–6.5), P7.2, P8.1, 8.2, 8.4, 8.6, 8.8, 4.3, 4.6, 5.4, 10.4, 9.5, S4.3 and M8.3. Target at least 3 pages, at least 5 exam-tagged worked examples with Edexcel marks, a figure where a picture helps, and an exam toolkit page.
- **Flags:** effort XL

### CM-07 · P2 · content — Statistics and Mechanics leaf titles are cut-off 80-character spec sentences, unlike the short Pure titles

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Pure leaves have names like "Straight lines" and "Trapezium rule". Every S and M leaf is titled with the first 80 characters of its spec sentence plus "…", e.g. M7.3 "Understand, use and derive the formulae for constant acceleration for motion in …" and S4.1 "simple, discrete probability distributions" (lowercase fragment). The page title is then cut again to three lines with an ellipsis, the spine rows read "Understand and use the lang…" five times in a row under M7, and the search results and breadcrumbs show the same. gen_data.py already defines SHORT_S ("suvat (constant acceleration)", "Projectiles", …), but it is never applied to the checked-in maths.js. Its keys are bare ("7.3") while the payload's refs are "S1.1" and "M7.3", and the generator cannot run locally (it reads /home/claude/extract).
- **Evidence:** shots/content-maths/m73-mac.png (title and spine); shots/content-maths/m73-notes-iphone.png; tools/gen_data.py:249 (80-char truncation), :287-301 (SHORT_S and its lookup by c["ref"]); js/data/maths.js:989 ("S1.1"), :1287 ("M7.3")
- **Root cause:** The short-title table is keyed by refs that never match the stats and mech children, and the generator's source is not available to re-run.
- **Proposed fix:** Key SHORT_S by the final refs (S1.1…S5.3, M6.1…M9.1) and add a payload-only pass, like --mech-refs, that applies titles to the checked-in maths.js. Then regenerate.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-08 · P2 · content — Wrong flashcard answer: 9.3 Newton–Raphson from x0 = 0 gives x1 = −2.5, not 2.5

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** 9.3 FC5 (bank) asks "Why does $x_0 = 0$ fail for $f(x)=x^3-2x-5$?" and answers "it does not fail here; $x_1 = 2.5$". In fact f(0) = −5 and f′(0) = −2, so x1 = 0 − (−5)/(−2) = −2.5, which the card's own formula (2x³+5)/(3x²−2) also gives. The question also states something false: it says the method fails and the answer says it does not.
- **Evidence:** js/data/content/bank-maths-pure-c.js:646
- **Root cause:** An arithmetic sign slip in the bank author's answer, with no automated check on numeric answers.
- **Proposed fix:** Rewrite the card, e.g. "Where would NR fail for $f(x)=x^3-2x-5$?" → "Where $f'(x_0)=0$: $x_0=\pm\sqrt{2/3}$". Or keep the x0 = 0 example with "$x_1=-2.5$ (far from the root 2.09)". Keep its schedule key (invariant 87).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-09 · P3 · content — Cards filed under the wrong leaf

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** 6.1 (Exponential functions) holds solve-a^x=b items that belong to 6.5: fc3 "Solve $4^x=100$", fc10b "$e^{2x}=5$", fc13b "$2^x=20$", qz3. M8.3 (Weight and gravity) holds suvat projectile items that belong to M7.3: fc13b impact speed and fc14b rebound height. S4.3 (Selecting a distribution) holds normal-approximation and continuity-correction drill that belongs to S4.2: fc10b–fc14b, qz4b, qz5b, qz7b. 2.10 (Partial fractions) qz3 is an integral, ∫2/(x+1), which is 8.6's topic. 10.4 fc8b is an average-speed question. 8.1 fc9b ends without an answer ("a third condition is needed").
- **Evidence:** cm/load.js dumps; read with the node print script (outputs quoted in this audit); bank-maths-pure-*.js, bank-maths-mech.js, bank-maths-stats.js
- **Root cause:** Bank items were grouped by past-paper question rather than by spec point.
- **Proposed fix:** Move these items to their own leaves, keeping each card's original key as a whole-key third element (invariant 87), or replace them with items on the leaf's own skill.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-10 · P3 · content — "Past-paper patterns" pages are one wall-of-text paragraph

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** 55 of the 57 note paragraphs over 60 words sit on the bank's "Past-paper patterns" page. That is one per leaf, up to 135 words of run-on text with bold phrases and inline series lists. This goes against the user's rule of short lines and bullets, not paragraphs. On M7.3 page 8, one 6-line paragraph lists every question shape, and the lower half of the page is empty. It also repeats the M7.3 overview table of question shapes on page 1.
- **Evidence:** shots/content-maths/m73-pastpaper-mac.png; js/data/content/bank-maths-*.js (each X(…) notes: [{page:"Past-paper patterns"}, "<long string>"]); counted by a script: 57 paragraphs over 60 words, 55 after the bank page
- **Root cause:** The bank files were written as prose summaries before the short-lines rule.
- **Proposed fix:** Convert each to a {table:{head:[Question shape, Marks, Seen in]}} or a {ul} of one shape per line, or fold it into the leaf's existing overview table and drop the page.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CM-11 · P3 · content — 29 leaves have no figure where a picture would teach

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Leaves with figs=0: 2.1 2.2 2.10 2.11 3.4 4.1 4.2 4.3 4.4 4.5 4.6 5.5 5.6 5.8 5.9 6.4 6.5 7.2 7.4 7.5 7.6 8.1 8.2 8.5 8.6 8.7 8.8 9.5 S3.3. Clear candidates: 5.9 Trig in context (Ferris wheel or tide curve), 5.6 R-form (the R·sin(θ+α) graph with max and min), 8.7 Separable DEs (family of solution curves, which the spec requires students to sketch), 3.4 Parametric modelling (motion path), 7.5 Implicit differentiation (circle with tangent), 4.2 Sequences (increasing, decreasing and periodic plots), 8.2 and 8.1 (antiderivative family), 2.11 modelling curves. Per the user's notes rule, annotated {fig} diagrams are expected wherever a picture helps.
- **Evidence:** cm/metrics.json figs column
- **Root cause:** Figures were added only as each section was rewritten at depth.
- **Proposed fix:** Add declarative {fig} diagrams (core/figures.js) to these leaves during the depth pass. They are SVG-declarative, so no raster art is needed.
- **Flags:** effort L

### CM-12 · P3 · content — Units such as "m s$^{-1}$" break across lines

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Units are authored as "m s$^{-1}$" with a normal space. At wrap points the line breaks inside the unit, leaving "…5 m s" at a line end and "−1" on the next line. Seen in M7.2's worked question in Dawn on the Mac ("travels at a constant 5 m s / −1 for the next 15 seconds").
- **Evidence:** shots/content-maths/m72-notes-dawn-mac.png
- **Root cause:** A breakable space plus a separate math segment for the exponent.
- **Proposed fix:** Author units as a single KaTeX segment, e.g. $\text{m s}^{-1}$ or $5\ \text{m}\,\text{s}^{-1}$, or have inline() replace the space before a unit with U+00A0 and wrap the unit in a white-space:nowrap span.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CM-13 · P3 · content — Two-option quiz items in a 4-option quiz

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** 8.1 qz9 ("$\int_2^5 f = -\int_5^2 f$ is: true / false") and 4.5 qz12 have only two options, so a guess is right 50% of the time, and they look different from the other items.
- **Evidence:** cm/dups.json quizBad entries maths:8.1 #9 and maths:4.5 #12 (bank-maths-pure-c.js, bank-maths-pure-b.js)
- **Root cause:** Bank authoring.
- **Proposed fix:** Rewrite them as 4-option items, or as flashcards.
- **Flags:** effort S

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Per-leaf summary of what needs work (T = thin notes, D = duplicates, M = misfiled cards, F = no figure, E = 5 or fewer exam items, W = wrong answer, S = garbled spec):
1.1 S; 2.1 D,F,S; 2.2 D,F,S; 2.3 S; 2.4 S; 2.5 S; 2.6 D; 2.7 S; 2.8 D; 2.9 D,S; 2.10 D,M,F; 2.11 F,E; 3.3 D,S; 3.4 T,F,E; 4.1 D,F,S; 4.2 F,S; 4.3 T,F,S; 4.4 F; 4.5 D,F; 4.6 T,F,E; 5.2 D,S; 5.3 D,E,S; 5.4 T,D,E; 5.5 D,F,S; 5.6 D,F,S; 5.8 D,F; 5.9 F; 6.1 T,D,M,E; 6.2 T,E,S; 6.3 T,D,S; 6.4 T,F,S; 6.5 T,F; 7.2 T,D,F,S; 7.4 F,S; 7.5 D,F; 7.6 D,F; 8.1 T,D,F,E; 8.2 T,F,S; 8.4 T,E; 8.5 D,F,S; 8.6 T,F,E,S; 8.7 F; 8.8 T,F,E; 9.3 W; 9.4 S; 9.5 T,F,E; 10.3 D; 10.4 T,D,S; S2.3 S; S3.3 F; S4.1 D,S; S4.2 D; S4.3 T,D,M,E; S5.3 D,S; M7.3 D,S(spec, plus a possible user fork); M7.4 S; M7.5 D; M8.1 E; M8.3 T,D,M,E,S; M8.5 E; M8.6 S. Leaves with no issue found: 1.1 notes, 3.1, 3.2, 5.1, 5.7, 6.6, 6.7, 7.1, 7.3, 8.3, 9.1, 9.2, S1.1, S2.1, S2.2, S2.4, S3.1, S3.2, S5.1, S5.2, M6.1, M7.1, M7.2, M8.2, M8.4, M9.1.
- Add a smoke test for Maths content: duplicate detection inside a leaf (canonical q plus answer), quiz answer-position balance, at least 3 options, KaTeX parse of every segment (passes today), and fragment detection on the spec spine.
- A mnemonic callout heading that contains math renders with odd spacing (8.4: '"Sum of strips → integral; δx → dx ; the x -range → the limits"'). Keep math out of callout h, or typeset it the same way as the body.

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- Fork staleness UX: show which curriculum kinds of a topic are forked, and offer a diff and merge when the shipped version changes.
- Finish the Pure depth pipeline for P6–P8 and the remaining small leaves so every Maths leaf meets the contract smoke65/66 enforce for CS (pages, at least 3 worked examples with marks, a mnemonic, a misconception callout, an exam toolkit).
- Rebuild the Maths spec spine from a clean source (hand-typed LaTeX) with a regenerable override table.


---

## CC — A-Level CS and OCR IT content quality (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** CS content is deep and renders cleanly (all 181 CS/IT leaves' note pages swept at iPhone with no overflow, no KaTeX errors), but its practice layers have systematic quality faults: multiple-choice answers sit at A/B 94% of the time with no option shuffle, the bank files duplicate past-paper questions already authored as worked cards (202 duplicates over 88 leaves), and the depth rewrite left ~30 decks asking the same definition twice. IT is the weak side: its generated spec (tools/gen_data.py) dumps garbled assessment-criteria PDF text into the last leaf of each unit, leaves 189 fragment rows, and creates a duplicate ref F206.6.1 that makes 'Ways to reflect' (current NEA) unreachable; F201 (still to sit) has no worked answers and only 3 exam items per leaf. Shared renderer issues: the worked card's absolutely positioned 'Write it out' pill covers titles on iPhone and iPad, steps blocks misused in six 4.5.x leaves show literal **bold** in monospace, and bold-with-italic shows literal asterisks. Root causes are authoring without automated checks (no near-duplicate, answer-position, duplicate-src or validate_content gate) and the IT parser's missing section boundaries and ref-uniqueness assertion.

**Coverage.** Measured every CS (131) and IT (50 with content; F200/F202 have spec only) leaf from a jsdom dump: notes words/pages/worked/figures/tables, flashcards, quiz, exam, bare paragraphs; within- and cross-leaf flashcard near-duplicates; cards scored against other leaves; quiz answer positions and longest-option bias; exam duplicate sources; formatting (literal markdown, $, repeated words); validate_content on cs-* and it-*; code block languages. Browser: every note page of all 181 leaves swept at mac (text artefacts) and iPhone (overflow); screenshots of 4.5.2.1 and 4.10.3 (mac), 4.6.4.1 worked cards (iPhone), 4.10.4 in Dawn (mac), F201.2.3 (iPad landscape, Dawn), F206.6.1 Spec (iPad); Write-it-out overlap measured at iphone/ipad/mac. Not examined: the Flashcards/Quiz/Exam tab UI itself and the exam tally (study-core's area), shop themes, correctness of every CS fact (only spot reads), IT F200/F202 (completed units without notes). Per-leaf worklist: audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/content-cs-it/worklist.txt (162 leaves).

### CC-01 · P1 · content — IT spec text for F206.6.1, F204.6.5, F202.5.1, F201.5.2, F200.5.3 (and F200.2.x/F202.2.x) holds garbled PDF dumps of the assessment criteria

- **Detail / repro:** The generated IT spec (js/data/it.js) contains multi-thousand-character strings of shuffled PDF text in the Spec tab content/info lists for the last leaf of each unit: e.g. F206.6.1 'Ways to reflect' ends with a 2,271-char 'Lessons learnt Assessment criteria The table below gives...' string and a 2,884-char info item; F204.6.5 has 2,164/1,007/2,510-char strings; F202.5.1 2,330/538/2,502; F201.5.2 (the unit the user is sitting) has 'Urban and community planning Assessment guidance This unit is assessed by an exam...' and an 831-char run; F200.5.3 876 chars. Shorter merged rows also start with a dangling 'include:' (F200.2.2, F200.2.3, F200.2.6, F201.2.4, F202.2.2, F202.2.4, F202.2.5, F202.3.3). Column-interleaved words ('The tasks. means that the unit grade awarded') are unreadable on the Spec tab and pollute search and the Assistant's spec reads. F206 is the NEA the user is doing now. Beyond the long dumps, 189 short spec rows in 39 IT leaves are fragments ('□ Does', '□ Techniques To', '• Diagnostic Prescriptive □', a bare '□'), e.g. F201.2.3 Data mining techniques reads ['□ Data mining','□ Data mining techniques','• Descriptive','• Diagnostic Prescriptive □','□','□ Does','□'] and F201.2.6 omits 'Factor analysis' which the notes teach.
- **Evidence:** js/data/it.js:2246-2283 (F206.6.1 content/info); node scan of dump.json: strings >260 chars listed per leaf (F206.6.1 2271/894/2884, F204.6.5 2164/1007/2510, F202.5.1 2330/538/2502, F201.5.2 324/328/308/831, F200.5.3 331/308/876)
- **Root cause:** PDF parser in tools/gen_data.py appends trailing unit-level sections (criteria tables, guidance, synoptic tables) to the last topic leaf and mis-splits two-column 'To include' boxes.
- **Proposed fix:** Fix tools/gen_data.py's IT parser to stop each leaf at the 'Assessment criteria'/'Assessment guidance'/'Synoptic assessment' headings and at page-column breaks, and to join 'To include:' continuation lines into bullet rows; regenerate it.js (invariant 24) and add a smoke check that no IT spec string exceeds ~300 chars or starts with 'include:'.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CC-02 · P1 · data — IT spec has a duplicate leaf: F206.6.1 'Ways to reflect' and 'Overview of the assessment' share a ref, so 'Ways to reflect' cannot be opened

- **Detail / repro:** F206 Topic Area 6 has two children with the SAME ref F206.6.1: 'Ways to reflect' and 'Overview of the assessment' (qualification-structure text: entry codes H019/H119, GLH). The spine lists both rows, but both resolve to one topic: opening F206.6.1 (either row) shows the title 'Overview of the assessment' and its Spec (entry codes, GLH), with the 'Ways to reflect' notes, flashcards and quiz underneath. The real 'Ways to reflect' spec (Boud, Keogh and Walker; effectiveness of processes and tools; lessons learnt) is unreachable, and progress, RAG, attachments and FSRS keys are shared by the two rows. F206 is the NEA the user is doing now. Also F201.5.3 'NEA Units' is an empty phantom leaf in it.js (hidden by the spine, which counts F201 as 15, but present for any reader of KOS_DATA).
- **Evidence:** js/data/it.js:2246 and :2285 both 'ref': 'F206.6.1'; js/data/it.js:946-950; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/content-cs-it/it-f2061-spec-ipad.png (title 'Overview of the assessment', Notes 4 / Flashcards 14 are Ways to reflect's); eval on #cols: rows 'Ways to reflect F206.6.1' and 'Overview of the assessment F206.6.1', h1 after KOS.show('ref',{subject:'it',ref:'F206.6.1'}) = 'Overview of the assessment'
- **Root cause:** Generator does not filter non-topic headings and has no uniqueness check on refs.
- **Proposed fix:** In gen_data.py drop qualification-overview nodes ('Overview of the assessment', 'NEA Units') and assert ref uniqueness at generation; regenerate it.js; rename/prune any stored state keyed to F201.5.3 at the store gate like IT_NOT_TAKEN.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-03 · P1 · content — Quiz answers are guessable: the correct option is A or B in 94% of CS questions (D only 12 of 1,347) and options are never shuffled

- **Detail / repro:** Across all 1,347 CS quiz questions the correct index is A 677, B 590, C 68, D 12; IT's 288 are A 17, B 211, C 57, D 3. The correct option is also the uniquely longest one in 688/1,347 CS (51%) and 201/288 IT (70%) questions, against ~25% by chance. KOS.quiz.mountMCQ renders item.opts in authored order (only the question order has a Shuffle), so a learner quickly learns 'pick A/B, pick the longest', and 'Quiz best' on the topic inspector and RAG overstate recall. 46 leaves have 60%+ of answers in one position (e.g. 4.4.1.3: 11 of 12 are A; 4.5.2.1 9/11 A).
- **Evidence:** node content-cs-it/quiz.js over dump.json: compsci [677,590,68,12], it [17,211,57,3]; longest-correct compsci 688/1347, it 201/288; js/engines/quiz.js:88 item.opts.forEach renders in authored order (shuffle at :25 is only applied to question order, :66)
- **Root cause:** Authoring habit (answer written first) plus an engine that never permutes options.
- **Proposed fix:** Shuffle each question's options at render with a deterministic per-question seed (map ans through the permutation; keep keyboard 1–4 on the displayed order) and rebalance the longest-option bias by trimming correct options or lengthening distractors in the authored banks; add a smoke check that no leaf has >50% of answers at one index after shuffling is off.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-04 · P1 · ui — 'Write it out' pill on worked examples overlaps the example's title on iPhone and iPad

- **Devices:** iphone, ipad, ipadl (some), mac (1)
- **Detail / repro:** The worked-example card's 'Write it out' button is absolutely positioned at a fixed inset from the top-right, so whenever the title runs that far it sits on top of the words. On iPhone 5 of 8 worked cards on 4.6.4.1 page 4 are covered ('The bottle-filling conveyor', 'Process X: circuit and expression', 'XOR without an XOR gate', 'Draw without simplifying', 'Only AND, OR and NOT'); on iPad portrait (820) 14 of 72 buttons across 4.6.4.1, 4.5.4.4, 4.10.4 and 4.7.3.5 overlap text, on the Mac 1 of 72. The pill is also 81×23 px, under the 44 px touch minimum (invariant 69).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/content-cs-it/cs-4641-p3-iphone.png; eval at iphone: button rect x233 y824 w81 h23 position:absolute over title text; ipad hit 14/72, mac 1/72; css/views/ink.css:300 .k-n-writeout { position: absolute; inset-block-start: var(--sp-8); inset-inline-end: calc(var(--sp-8) * 9) }; js/core/content.js:373
- **Root cause:** Absolute positioning with a fixed inset instead of taking part in the header's layout.
- **Proposed fix:** Put the button in the worked card's head row as a flex item (title flex:1, min-width:0; actions group with Copy), wrapping under the title below 560px; give it the touch minimum height on coarse pointers.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-05 · P2 · content — Same past-paper question appears twice in a CS leaf's Exam questions (worked card + bank item): 202 duplicate groups across 88 of 131 leaves

- **Detail / repro:** Each CS leaf's exam list = examFromWorked(notes) + bank-cs-*.js items concatenated by KOS.content.extend. The bank re-writes questions already authored as worked exam cards, so the learner meets the same AQA question twice in a row of practice, with different wording and differently formatted source labels ('AQA June 2025 · P1 Q01.1' vs 'AQA 2025 P1 Q1.1'). Example 4.2.6.1 Hash tables: items 1/6 (2025 P1 Q1.1), 2/5 (Q1.2), 3/7 (Q1.3); 4.6.1.4 has 'Describe two functions of an operating system.' twice verbatim (2022 P2 Q4.3). Worst leaves: 4.7.3.5 (7 groups), 4.2.2.1, 4.6.2.1 (5), 4.2.4.1, 4.2.6.1, 4.5.5.3, 4.5.6.7, 4.6.4.1, 4.7.3.2, 4.7.3.7, 4.8.1, 4.10.3 (4). Exam-question counts and 'Done exam questions' progress are inflated accordingly.
- **Evidence:** node script over dump.json keyed on year-paper-question: 202 groups, 88 leaves, 1194 items; js/core/content.js:515 extend() concatenates exam without de-duplication; js/core/content.js:546 builds 'AQA June …' src vs bank files' 'AQA 2025 P1 Q1.1'
- **Root cause:** Bank files were authored independently of the worked cards; extend() concatenates blindly.
- **Proposed fix:** Drop the bank item when a worked card of the same leaf cites the same series/paper/question (do it in the bank files, or dedupe in extend() by a normalised src key), and normalise src labels to one format; add a smoke66 check for duplicate src within a leaf.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CC-06 · P2 · content — Steps blocks in 4.5.x notes render prose as monospace code with literal **bold** and a redundant '1.' heading

- **Detail / repro:** In 4.5.2.1 (Decimal → binary), 4.5.4.1, 4.5.4.3, 4.5.4.5, 4.5.6.4 and 4.5.6.7 the authored {steps} use h:'1.', h:'2.' (duplicating the numbered circle) and put prose with markdown in m. The renderer escapes m into a preformatted maths/code line, so the page shows '1.' next to circle 1 and a monospace box reading 'record the **remainder** (MOD)'. 21 numbered headings and 10 markdown-in-m steps.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/content-cs-it/cs-4521-steps-literal-bold-mac.png; js/data/content/cs-p2-45a.js:104 { h: '1.', m: 'Divide by the base; record the **remainder**…' }; js/core/content.js:404 esc(st.m)
- **Root cause:** Authoring misuse of the steps schema (m is the monospace working line, n the inline note).
- **Proposed fix:** Move the prose to n (rendered through inline()) and drop the numeric h in those six leaves; optionally have validate_content.js flag markdown inside steps[].m and h matching /^\d+\.$/.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-07 · P2 · content — ~30 CS flashcard decks ask the same fact twice ('Bandwidth?' + 'Define bandwidth.'), plus 11 cards duplicated across neighbouring leaves

- **Detail / repro:** The depth rewrite added 'Define X.' / 'What does the MAR hold?' cards beside the original terse 'X?' / 'MAR holds…' cards without removing them, so the same definition is scheduled twice under FSRS. Confirmed by reading: 4.1.1.10 subroutine; 4.1.2.1 paradigm; 4.1.2.3 inheritance; 4.2.4.1 weighted graph and directed graph; 4.2.5.1 BST rule; 4.3.5.2 merge sort; 4.4.2.1 Mealy machine; 4.6.1.1 'Define software.' twice verbatim; 4.6.1.2 four types of system software; 4.6.5.1 'X + X̄·Y' and 'Simplify A + ¬A.B' (same identity, two notations); 4.7.3.1 MAR and MBR; 4.7.3.6 interrupt sources; 4.7.4.1 barcode reader; 4.7.4.2 optical disk; 4.9.1.1 'What is crosstalk?' twice verbatim; 4.9.1.2 bandwidth and latency; 4.9.2.1 physical and logical topology; 4.9.4.6 private ranges; 4.9.4.7 DHCP; 4.9.4.11 thin client (x2); 4.10.1 attribute; 4.10.2 primary key; 4.10.5 record locking and timestamp ordering. Across leaves: interface of a subroutine (4.1.1.10#15 / 4.1.1.11#2), data abstraction (4.2.1.1#7 / 4.4.1.3#20), floating vs fixed point advantages (4.5.4.4#17-18 / 4.5.4.7#0-1), four types of system software (4.6.1.2#0 / 4.6.1.3#7), three sources of interrupts (4.7.3.2#14 / 4.7.3.6#9), IPv6 and NAT (4.9.4.5#7 / 4.9.4.8#2), pitch opening (F204.6.1#6 / F206.5.1#5). The same Boolean deck also mixes overbar (X̄) and ¬ notation.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/content-cs-it/strict.txt (43 within-leaf pairs, confirmed by reading), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/content-cs-it/cross.txt (11 cross-leaf pairs), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/content-cs-it/dupes.txt (looser 800-line list); js/data/content/cs-p2-*.js flashcards arrays
- **Root cause:** Cards appended during the depth rewrite without de-duplicating against the original deck; no automated near-duplicate check.
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the fuller 'Define X.' card and delete the terse twin in each pair; when deleting, add the original position as the third element to every later card (invariant 87) so schedules do not shift. Pick one Boolean notation per deck. Add a smoke66 near-duplicate check (token Jaccard on the question ≥ 0.9 within a leaf).
- **Flags:** effort M

### CC-08 · P2 · content — IT F201 (the unit still to sit) is much thinner than CS: no worked answers, 3 exam items and 5–6 quiz questions per leaf

- **Detail / repro:** Every F201 leaf has 0 worked examples, 0 figures, exactly 3 exam questions (no source), 5–6 quiz questions and 8–11 flashcards; notes run 493–1,281 words over 3–7 pages (F201.2.2 Data preparation 493 words and no table; F201.2.3 509; F201.5.1 517). CS leaves average ~1,900 words, 6–8 worked exam answers and 9–12 exam items. F201 is a 60-mark, 1 h 30 exam with six-mark and levels-of-response questions, yet there is no worked levels-of-response answer showing what a top-band 'Discuss' looks like, and 15 IT exam questions put '[6]' in the question text on top of the marks badge. The spec notes F201.2.6 lists 'Factor analysis' which the generated spec text does not (the spec row reads '□ Techniques To').
- **Evidence:** content-cs-it/metrics.txt (it F201.* rows: w0 fg0 ex3 q5-6); node scan: 15/150 IT exam q end with '[n]'; dump.json F201.2.3 spec ['□ Data mining','• Diagnostic Prescriptive □','□','□ Does','□']; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/content-cs-it/it-f2013-p1-ipadl.png (a 60-word note page, half the iPad landscape screen empty)
- **Root cause:** IT content was written to a lighter contract than smoke65/66 hold CS to; no IT depth smoke.
- **Proposed fix:** Add per F201 leaf: two worked exam answers (one 6-mark levels-of-response 'Discuss' with a band commentary), 6+ exam items across Describe/Explain/Discuss, 10 quiz questions, a scenario-based case per page; strip the '[n]' suffix from IT exam q text (marks already carry it).
- **Flags:** effort L

### CC-09 · P3 · content — Bold containing an *italic* word renders literal ** in three IT F204 callouts

- **Detail / repro:** KOS.content.inline's bold rule /\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/ cannot contain an asterisk, so '**health *and* home**' shows as '**health and home**' with the italic applied and literal asterisks. Seen in F204.1.1 'Sectors overlap' callout, F204.2.2 'How to write M6', F204.5.3 'A structure that scores' (this one also has a stray quote: '**(3) exactly what the solution does"**').
- **Evidence:** kos eval: KOS.content.inline('a **b *c* d** e') -> 'a **b <em>c</em> d** e'; js/core/content.js:109; js/data/content/it-f204.js:72, :544, :2189; render sweep content-cs-it/sweep-121.json
- **Root cause:** Inline markdown regex excludes * inside **…**.
- **Proposed fix:** Allow single-asterisk italics inside bold (process em inside strong: /\*\*((?:[^*]|\*(?!\*))+)\*\*/), and fix the misplaced quote at it-f204.js:2189.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-10 · P3 · content — Flashcards filed under the wrong leaf

- **Detail / repro:** Cards that test a different topic than the leaf they sit in (confirmed by reading): 4.3.4.3 Binary tree search #13 asks which TRAVERSAL finds a range (4.3.2.1); 4.12.1.3 Function application #7 'head (tail (tail [4,7,1,9]))' is list processing (4.12.3.1); 4.5.6.1 Bit patterns #8 'How are instructions represented?' is the instruction set (4.7.3.3); 4.7.3.2 Fetch-Execute #14/#16 are interrupt cards (4.7.3.6); 4.9.4.3 IP address structure #9/#13 are IPv6/IPv4-exhaustion cards (4.9.4.5); 4.4.1.3 Kinds of abstraction #17 'Link to functional programming?' and #22 'Fraction built from…?' are synoptic asides as recall cards.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/content-cs-it/fit.txt (cards scored against other leaves' notes vocabulary, top hits confirmed by reading)
- **Root cause:** Cards authored as synoptic links ended up as plain recall cards in the wrong deck.
- **Proposed fix:** Move each card to its home leaf (with a whole-key third element so its schedule follows, invariant 87) or delete it where the home leaf already has the fact.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-11 · P3 · content — validate_content reports 9 errors in the CS merged leaves (cs-m-*) and 92 in IT: bare top-level paragraphs

- **Detail / repro:** CLAUDE.md states `node tools/validate_content.js js/data/content/cs-*.js` reports no errors, but it reports 9 ERRORs, all bare string paragraphs in the merged-leaf files (4.4.1.9, 4.4.4.6, 4.5.1.1, 4.5.3.1, 4.5.4.1, 4.5.4.5, 4.5.6.1, 4.5.6.4 ×2: 'Steps an ADC performs (3 marks)…', 'Minimum file size = width × height × colour depth…'). These exam-technique paragraphs are exactly the content that should be a tip/formula callout. IT content has 92 bare-paragraph errors (F201.2.4 7, F201.2.3/2.5 5, F204.3.3 5 …), mostly the 'Characteristics: … When used: …' rows in F201 that render as loose prose between callouts.
- **Evidence:** node tools/validate_content.js js/data/content/cs-*.js → '45 file(s) checked — 9 error(s), 190 warning(s)'; it-*.js → '92 error(s), 120 warning(s)'; js/data/content/cs-m-errors.js, cs-m-bit-patterns.js
- **Root cause:** The merged-leaf files were written after the validator pass, and the validator is not part of the smoke gate.
- **Proposed fix:** Wrap each flagged paragraph in the fitting callout (tip for exam technique, formula for file-size rules); for IT, turn the 'Characteristics/When used' lines into a kv or table block per technique; add validate_content to the smoke gate so it cannot regress.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-12 · P3 · content — AQA mark-scheme shorthand (I., A., R., MAX, DPT) is used ~228 times with no explanation anywhere

- **Detail / repro:** Worked steps' notes read 'I. an incorrect gate for B·C.', 'I. missing spaces; A. each digit on a new line', 'R. a column with more than one value', 'MAX 2 if not fully correct'. These are AQA examiner abbreviations (Ignore / Accept / Reject); a student meeting them on a phone reads 'I.' as a roman numeral or a first-person note. No legend exists in content.js, the worked card or Help.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/content-cs-it/cs-4641-p3-iphone.png (step 3 note 'I. an incorrect gate for B·C.'); grep '"[IAR]\. ' js/data/content/cs-*.js bank-cs-*.js → 228; js/data/content/cs-p1-411a.js, cs-p1-42c.js, cs-p1-43b.js
- **Root cause:** Mark points copied in AQA's house style without a reader-facing gloss.
- **Proposed fix:** Expand them at render in worked-step notes and mark-scheme bullets (I. → 'Ignore:', A. → 'Accept:', R. → 'Reject:') with a title on MAX/DPT, or add a one-line legend to the Exam questions tab and the worked card's footer.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CC-13 · P3 · content — Functional-programming code is tagged 'text' (no Haskell label or highlighting) and assembly shows the raw label 'asm'

- **Detail / repro:** Every Haskell listing in 4.12.x (map/filter/fold definitions, partial application, composition, list processing — 11 blocks) is a {code} with lang 'text', so its head says 'text' and nothing is highlighted, while the C# twin beside it is coloured; the 4.7.3.5 assembly blocks print 'asm' because LANG_NAME has no entry for it.
- **Evidence:** dump scan of notes code blocks: 4.12.1.2/1.3/1.4/1.5/2.1/3.1 lang 'text', 4.7.3.5 lang 'asm'; js/core/content.js:70 LANG_NAME (no haskell/asm), :74 esc(LANG_NAME[lang] || lang)
- **Root cause:** No highlighter/label for the two non-C# languages the AQA spec uses.
- **Proposed fix:** Tag those blocks lang 'haskell' and add 'haskell: "Haskell"', 'asm: "AQA assembly"' to LANG_NAME with a small keyword set in highlightCode (comments --, ::, ->).
- **Flags:** effort S

## CO — Code study space (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### CO-01 · P1 · ui — Stage path on touch devices: unit squares become 28x44 bars and capstone diamonds render as skewed slashes

- **Devices:** iPad portrait/landscape, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On iPad and iPhone (pointer: coarse) the desk hero's stage path (and every KOS.codeUI stage path) draws each unit square U1..U12 as a 28x44 tall rectangle and each capstone diamond as a 16x44 box rotated 45deg, which reads as a broken diagonal stroke rather than a diamond. Mac (fine pointer) is correct. Reproduce: #/subject/code at ipad/iphone.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-ipad.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-iphone.png; eval at iphone: .k-cx-dia offsetWidth 16 x offsetHeight 44 with transform rotate(45deg); .k-cx-sq 28x44. css/base.css:115-119 (coarse min-block-size: var(--hit-coarse) on every button) vs css/views/code.css:132,137 (fixed inline-size/block-size), buttons made at js/core/codeui.js:556,567
- **Root cause:** The global coarse-pointer rule raises min-block-size of every <button> to 44px (invariant 69) but the path's square/diamond buttons only set inline-size/block-size, so height grows and width does not; the diamond is then rotated.
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-cx-sq and .k-cx-dia min-block-size: 0 (they are visual marks; the diamond is tabindex -1) and put the 44px hit area on a transparent ::before or wrapper, or raise width with height for the squares (invariant 69: a round/square control must raise its width too).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-02 · P1 · ui — iPhone desk: Daily 20 and Continue squeezed side by side; the Open-lesson button's label spills out of its pill

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px the desk's side column (.k-subj-side) switches to a row, so Daily 20 and Continue are each ~180px wide. The Daily 20 body wraps to five lines, and the 'Open 1.1 Your first program' button is nowrap: its text (153px) overflows the 144px pill on both sides, so the label crosses the button border. Continue is a tall card with its content floating in the middle of ~200px of empty space, and its title wraps.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-iphone-d20.png (dpr 2), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-iphone.png; eval iphone: [data-ui=code.daily20-lesson] 144x44, scrollWidth 153, white-space nowrap; .k-cx-d20 width 180. css/views/study.css:1074-1076 (side becomes a row of flex:1 1 0 cards), button at js/modules/code.js:221-223
- **Root cause:** The phone/compact rule lays the side column as a row of two equal cards whatever their width; k-btn is nowrap with no max-inline-size or ellipsis, and the label names the lesson in full.
- **Proposed fix:** Under 560px stack the two side cards (flex-direction: column) or fold Continue into the Daily 20 card when the Daily 20's job is 'Finish 1.1 first' (they point at the same lesson). Shorten the button to 'Open 1.1' or allow wrapping/ellipsis (.k-fit-btn).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-03 · P1 · ui — Desk side column: a real Daily 20 task crushes the Continue card to 41px and clips its 'CONTINUE' label (Mac, iPad landscape)

- **Devices:** MacBook, iPad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** With a task in the Daily 20 (tier pill, title, three concept chips wrapping to two rows, the why line, Start), the Daily 20 card grows to 239px of the side column's fixed 292px (the hero's height). The Continue card gets 41px while its content needs 62px; overflow: hidden cuts the CONTINUE kicker in half under the Daily 20 card's border. Same at iPad landscape (d20 327 of 459, Continue 121, fits) and worse whenever the why line wraps to three lines. The concept chips (k-cx-cchips) wrap one per row on narrow cards, adding rows.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-prog-mac.png (CONTINUE at y~396 cut); eval mac: .k-cx-d20 239px tall, [data-ui=study.continue] clientHeight 41 vs scrollHeight 62; side 292 = hero 292. css/views/study.css:50 (.k-subj-side > :first-child flex: 1 1 auto), :54 (contain: size), :55 (> * { min-block-size: 0; overflow: hidden }); js/modules/code.js:124, 228-234, 51-55
- **Root cause:** The side column is size-contained to the hero's height and its children may shrink to zero with overflow hidden; the Daily 20 card (first child, flex 1 1 auto) takes what it needs, so the second card is what gives way and is clipped rather than the layout growing.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the Continue card flex: 0 0 auto (never shrink below its content) and let the Daily 20 card scroll/clip instead, or drop size containment when the content is taller than the hero; render concept chips as one non-wrapping scroller or limit to two chips + '+1'.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-04 · P1 · bug — Task Run can only feed ONE line of input: a correct answer to any multi-line task crashes with ArgumentNullException

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The task page's STDIN box is a single-line <input type=text>, prefilled with only the first line of the first example (split('\n')[0]). 158 of 258 tasks have multi-line example input. Pressing Run with the reference answer to 2.9 Guessing game (example input 42/50/30/42) gives 'Runtime exception ArgumentNullException line 5 Value cannot be null', because the second Console.ReadLine() returns null. A learner can never try their program on the example before Check, and the error points at correct code, which teaches the wrong lesson.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/task-run-multiline-mac.png; eval: set editor to KOS.code.task('2.9-guess').answer, click Run -> output 'ArgumentNullException line 5'; Object.values(KOS_CODE.TASKS) with multi-line examples[0].stdin = 158. js/modules/code.js:633-635 (input type text, split first line), :694 (stdin: stdin.value)
- **Root cause:** Run's input was built as a one-line text field and deliberately truncates the example to its first line; an <input> cannot hold newlines.
- **Proposed fix:** Use an auto-growing <textarea> (rows = example line count) prefilled with the whole first example stdin; keep Ctrl+Enter to run. Optionally a menu to load example N.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-05 · P2 · ui — Subject analytics card stretches its eight tiles over the Retrieval-due list's height, leaving ~120px gaps between rows

- **Devices:** MacBook, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** On the Code desk the analytics card (k-sa) is in a row with Retrieval due. With 4+ due skills the row is 412px tall and the grid (flex 1 1 0, grid-auto-rows 1fr, tiles justify-content center) spreads two rows of figures across it: Mastery/Lessons at y~560, Skills proven/Tasks passed at y~740, mostly empty dark card between. On iPad portrait (empty account) the 8 tiles stack 2 per row over ~450px, five of them showing only a dash. Conversely with nothing due the Retrieval-due card is a 250px card holding one sentence.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-prog-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-ipad.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-mac.png; eval mac: .k-sa 412px tall = retrieval-due 412px. css/views/study.css:77-78
- **Root cause:** grid-auto-rows: 1fr with flex: 1 1 0 makes the tile rows absorb whatever height the neighbouring card imposes; tiles centre their content vertically.
- **Proposed fix:** Size the grid to its content (grid-auto-rows: auto, align-content: start) and let the Retrieval-due list cap/scroll, or put the shorter column on sticky ride-along (the Reports pack rule). On narrow widths use 4 columns of compact tiles or hide zero/dash tiles (invariant 77).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-06 · P2 · ux — Continue keeps pointing at 1.1 after the learner has worked through 22 lessons

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.code.stats().current is the first core lesson whose status is not 'done'. Completed needs all four checks, and the fourth (a retrieval passed) cannot be earned until the card's first review comes due days later. So after reading every page, running every listing and passing every task in lessons 1.1-3.5 (and starting 3.6), the desk's Continue card still says '1.1 Your first program' at 75%, and the hero's current unit ring stays on U1. The learner's real place (3.6) is nowhere on the desk.
- **Evidence:** eval after marking pages/listings/tasks for 22 lessons: stats().current.ref = '1.1', evidence('1.1').checks = [true,true,true,false]; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-prog-mac.png (Continue 1.1). js/core/code.js:870 (current = first not done), js/modules/code.js:250-262
- **Root cause:** 'Where to continue' is derived from completion (which includes a time-gated retrieval) instead of coverage (KOS.code.studied) or the last lesson touched.
- **Proposed fix:** Continue at the first lesson not yet studied (KOS.code.studied false), or the most recently touched unfinished lesson; leave pending retrievals to the Retrieval card.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-07 · P2 · data — Retrieval's 'Next reviews will ask…' dates come from a hard-coded SM-2 formula and contradict the real FSRS schedule

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Under a card the page projects its next three reviews as 'in 3 days / in 11 days / in 31 days', computed with ivl*2.5 (SM-2). Answering the card first try (graded Easy by FSRS) shows 'Proven · Easy · next in 8 days' directly above a table still saying 'in 3 days'. Since 20 October every card is scheduled by KOS.fsrs (invariant 111); the projection is invented and never matches. The page subtitle also keeps '6 due today' after the tab count dropped to 5.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/retrieval-after-check-mac.png (next in 8 days vs table in 3 days); js/modules/code.js:1013-1020 (var ivl = Math.max(3, row.ivl||2), ef = 2.5 … ivl*ef)
- **Root cause:** The projection predates the FSRS switch and was not moved to KOS.fsrs; it is not redrawn after a rating.
- **Proposed fix:** Project with KOS.fsrs (Good each time from the card's current s/d with KOS.srs.weights() and the retention) or drop the dates and keep only the variation list; redraw after finish().
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-08 · P2 · a11y — Capstone step bar: steps 2-6 are 26x40 targets on iPhone and every step is 40px tall on iPad

- **Devices:** iPhone, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** The capstone's six-step bar (Closed-book quiz, Brief & build, Rubric, Kurenai review, Write-up, Result) collapses the inactive steps to number circles on iPhone: each button is 26x40, under the 44px touch minimum in both directions, and the circles sit 4px apart. On iPad every step is 40px tall (75x40 for inactive ones).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/capstone-C1-iphone.png; DOM measurement iphone: k-cx-step 26x40 (x5), ipad 75x40. css/views/code.css:505 (.k-cx-step min-block-size: var(--sp-40)) — a class rule that beats the zero-specificity coarse rule in css/base.css:115-119
- **Root cause:** The step's own min-block-size (40px) overrides the global touch minimum and no coarse-pointer tier raises it; compact steps have no min-inline-size.
- **Proposed fix:** Under (pointer: coarse) give .k-cx-step min-block-size and min-inline-size var(--hit-coarse) (circles stay drawn at 26px inside the 44px button).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-09 · P2 · ux — On iPhone every Code sub-page loses its own title: a task, a track, the skill map, katas and settings all read just 'Code'

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The phone title bar takes the current subject pill's label ('Code') and the page's own heading (.k-pagehead-txt) is hidden. So #/code/task/2.9-guess shows 'Code' and a statement with no task name or tier; #/code/track/T1 shows 'Code' over an Overview card with no 'T1 · Connectivity and persistence'; Skill map, Curriculum map, Katas, Settings and Rider projects likewise. Nothing on screen says where you are, and there is no back/breadcrumb on the task page.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/task-2.9-guess-iphone.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/track-T1-iphone.png; eval iphone on #/code/task/2.9-guess: h1.k-ptitle-h 'Code' visible, .k-pagehead (with h1 'Guessing game') display:none. js/modules/mobile-shell.js:551 (title = data-phone-title-text || cur.label || h1), css/components.css:651-652
- **Root cause:** syncTitleBar prefers the current destination's label over the page's own h1; Code's sub-pages are all under the 'Code' destination and set no [data-phone-title-text].
- **Proposed fix:** In Code's pages mark the page title with data-phone-title-text (or make syncTitleBar prefer the page h1 when the view has a sub-page arg), keeping the kicker 'Code · task' as the bar's section line.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-10 · P2 · a11y — Lesson listings' Run / Reset / Copy are 33px tall on iPad and iPhone

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Every runnable listing in a Code lesson has Run, Reset and Copy buttons 64x33, 61x33 and 59x33 on touch devices (the most-pressed control in a lesson). On iPad, the topic page reports 15 sub-44px targets; the reader's page select is 274x30.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/topic-1.1-iphone.png; eval iphone: [data-ui=code.listing-run] height 33.25; DOM measurement ipad #/ref/code/1.1. css/views/code.css:152 (.k-cx-listing .k-ced-head .k-btn { min-block-size: var(--hit-dense) }) overrides css/components.css:678
- **Root cause:** A dense override for the listing head with no (pointer: coarse) tier; specificity beats the coarse .k-btn rule.
- **Proposed fix:** Wrap code.css:152 in @media (pointer: fine) or add a coarse tier restoring var(--hit-coarse).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-11 · P2 · ui — iPad portrait: 'Start focus' wraps to its own 44px row and the subject pills cut 'Creatives' down to a lone dot

- **Devices:** iPad portrait
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px the stage head (subject pills + Code's section links + Start focus) does not fit one line: Start focus wraps alone onto a second row at the right (≈64px of mostly empty band above the hero), while the subject pill scroller is clipped so only Creatives' yellow dot shows at its right edge with no label or scroll affordance. Same chrome on every Code page (map, skills, retrieval, kata, settings, track).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-prog-ipad.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-ipad.png; eval ipad: Start focus at top 138 in .k-page-actions row of 44px below the nav row. css/layout.css:346 (.k-stage-head flex-wrap: wrap), :349; js/modules/code.js:89-91
- **Root cause:** The stage head wraps rather than compacting; #subnav (subject pills + 4 Code links) is wider than 820 minus the button, and the pill scroller has no fade/arrow at that width.
- **Proposed fix:** At ≤860 move Start focus into the hero (or make it an icon button), or put Code's four links in a second compact row; give the pill strip a declared KOS.ui.scroller with edge arrows so a partial pill is never shown as a bare dot.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CO-12 · P3 · ui — Stage path scroller clips the current unit's square and its focus ring at its left edge

- **Devices:** iPhone, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** On iPhone the first square U1 (state current, with a 2-ring box-shadow) starts exactly at the scroller's left edge (both x=34), so the outer ring and the square's left border are cut off.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/desk-empty-iphone-d20.png; eval iphone: U1 left 34 = scroller left 34. css/views/code.css:134 (ring drawn by box-shadow outside the box)
- **Root cause:** The scroller has no inline padding to hold the box-shadow ring of the first/last item.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the path row padding-inline of 2*focus-width (or scroll-padding) so the ring is inside the clip.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-13 · P3 · content — Phone 'Best on a computer' task shows the example OUTPUT without its input

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On iPhone a later task (e.g. 2.9 Guessing game) is shown read-only to save for the computer. Its example box shows 'Example output: Lower / Higher / Correct in 3 guesses' but not the input 42/50/30/42 that produces it, so the example cannot be understood. The box is also half-width beside nothing.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/task-2.9-guess-iphone.png; js/modules/code.js:907 (only t.examples[0].out)
- **Root cause:** phoneLater renders only the out half of the example.
- **Proposed fix:** Render the same two-column Input/Output example as the desktop task panel, full width.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-14 · P3 · bug — Rider project's 'Lessons it proves' is string-sorted: 10.1, 10.10, 10.2, 10.3 …

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On #/code/project/rp-u10-library the side card lists '10.1 Why test, 10.10 Practise: test a legacy module, 10.2 xUnit …' — 10.10 sits second. Same for any project whose lessons pass .9 (and T1.10+ in tracks).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/project-rp-u10-library-mac.png; js/modules/coderider.js:71 (refs.sort() — lexicographic)
- **Root cause:** Array.prototype.sort with no comparator on dotted refs.
- **Proposed fix:** Sort by the lesson's spine index (C().lesson(r).idx) or a numeric dotted comparator.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-15 · P3 · ux — After a task passes the page offers no next step and its clock keeps running

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Passing 2.9 Guessing game (4 of 4, +26 XP) leaves the page as it was: no 'Next task', 'Back to the desk' or 'Next in the Daily 20' action beside the reward line, so the only way on is the breadcrumb. The task clock in the header keeps counting (00:47 → 00:50 three seconds later) after every test passed.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/code-space/task-checked-mac.png; eval: header time 00:47 then 00:50 after pass
- **Root cause:** The pass handler writes the reward line but does not stop the task timer or render a forward action.
- **Proposed fix:** On a proven pass stop the clock (show the time taken) and add a primary 'Next' (the next task of the lesson, or back to the Daily 20/desk).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CO-16 · P3 · content — Daily 20 marked Done still says 'you haven't proven Input loops in code yet'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** After the Daily 20 task passes, the desk card shows '✓ Done today · +26 XP' under the unchanged why line 'You studied 2.9 and haven't proven Input loops in code yet.', which is now false.
- **Evidence:** eval #/subject/code .k-cx-d20 innerText after passing 2.9-guess; js/modules/code.js:234-240 (why printed whatever plan.done)
- **Root cause:** planDay's why is computed for the morning pick and printed even when done.
- **Proposed fix:** When plan.done, replace the why with what was proven ('Proven today: Input loops, while, Ternary').
- **Flags:** effort S

## JM — Japanese and Mind study spaces (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### JM-01 · P2 · ui — Japanese stage path labels truncate ('Intermedi...', 'Upper int...', 'Advanced' on iPad) on the desk hero and the Checkpoints page

- **Devices:** mac, ipad portrait
- **Detail / repro:** The six stage headers of the stage path (Kana, Foundations, Bridge, Intermediate, Upper intermediate, Advanced) get equal narrow slots after the stones; the later stages' names are ellipsised. Mac desk: 'Intermediate' and 'Upper intermediate' 69px wide, ellipsised; iPad portrait: 'Intermediate', 'Upper intermediate' and 'Advanced' 52px; Checkpoints page mac: 'Upper interme...'. Only a hover title (absent on the touch devices) reveals the full name. A future stage holds 2 grid columns (one dashed stop), while a built stage's columns are its units + checkpoints (js/core/jaui.js:169-176), so each future stage label gets ~2/n of the width.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-ja-desk-mac.png (hero bottom row), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-ja-checkpoints-mac.png; DOM measure: ELLIP b 'Intermediate' 828,396 69x19, 'Upper intermediate' 933,396 69x19 (mac); 559,612 52x19 / 642,612 / 724,612 (ipad)
- **Root cause:** Stage label column width derived from the number of stones in the stage; empty future stages get the minimum slot
- **Proposed fix:** Give the not-yet-built stages (which have no stones) a label-width minimum or let labels wrap to two lines; alternatively collapse the three future stages into one 'Later: Bridge → Advanced' group. jaui stage path in js/core/jaui.js.
- **Flags:** effort S

### JM-02 · P2 · ux — Mind lesson inspector has no status selector, confidence (RAG) or progress bar, unlike every other study space

- **Detail / repro:** On a Code, Japanese or Creatives lesson the inspector carries the shared status row (Not started · Started · Paused · Completed select), the R/A/G confidence picker and a percent bar (invariant 107: the status 'may still be set by hand, as in a subject'). A Mind lesson's inspector shows only 'STATUS 0 of 4 checks' and the four read-only checks: no way to mark a lesson Paused or set confidence, and no percentage, although the desk and spine count Mind lessons by that same shared status.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/mindtopic.js:361-386 ctx.status builds only the head and checkgrid; compare js/modules/jatopic.js:295-318, js/modules/crtopic.js:220-243, js/modules/codetopic.js:160-210 (status select + KOS.rag.picker). Screenshots audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-mind-F1.1-mac.png vs audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-ja-1.1-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Each study space's topic module re-implements the inspector status block; Mind's copy omitted the row
- **Proposed fix:** Reuse the same status/RAG block (ideally hoist it into one shared helper used by all four topic modules, since it is copy-pasted three times) and add it to ctx.status in mindtopic.js.
- **Flags:** effort S

### JM-03 · P2 · a11y — Touch targets under 44px in Japanese and Mind: stage-path checkpoints 21px wide, Journal kind chips 32px tall, Daily-minutes slider 16px tall

- **Devices:** ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** On iPad (portrait and landscape) and iPhone: (1) the stage path's checkpoint buttons (data-ui ja.path-checkpoint) are 21x44 — a thin target between unit squares, and they are the only way to open a checkpoint from the desk's path; (2) Mind Journal's kind filter chips (.k-mind-kindpick: All, Insight, Quote, Question, Position, Reflection) are 32px tall on touch; (3) Japanese settings' Daily minutes range input is 129x16.
- **Evidence:** DOM measure (pointer:coarse): ipadl ja.path-checkpoint '合' 305,641 21x44 and 3 more; iphone same at 68,548 21x44; ipadl/iphone button.k-mind-kindpick 'All · 9' 68x32 … 'Reflection · 2' 140x32; ja.daily-min 129x16 (ipad, iphone). css/views/mind.css:338 min-block-size: var(--hit) (32px) with no coarse override; css/components.css:677-684 raises only .k-btn/.k-seg-item/.k-menu-item/.k-rail-item/.k-iconbtn.
- **Root cause:** Page-specific controls use --hit (32px) and are not in the shared coarse-pointer list
- **Proposed fix:** Under (pointer: coarse) give .k-mind-kindpick min-block-size var(--hit-coarse); give .k-ja-cp an invisible-free hit area of 44px wide (pad the node, not a pseudo overlap — invariant 69) or make the circle 28px with 8px spacing within a 44px column; give .k-range a 44px tall track box on coarse pointers.
- **Flags:** effort S

### JM-04 · P3 · data — Mind path: Decisions and strategy units are numbered Y3.1/Y3.2/Y3.3 while every other unit is <path>.U<n>

- **Detail / repro:** On #/mind/path every psychology/philosophy unit reads Y1.U1, Y2.U3, P1.U2..., but Y3's three units read Y3.1, Y3.2, Y3.3 — the same shape as a LESSON ref (F1.1). Once Y3 is released its lessons would be Y3.1.1 next to Y1.U1.1, and the bare 'Y3.1' looks like a lesson of a unit Y3 in search, chips and the spine.
- **Evidence:** js/data/mind.js:33 u("Y3.1",...), u("Y3.2",...), u("Y3.3",...) vs :31-32,34-36 '.U<n>'; docs/mind-plan.md:113-119 uses the same Y3.1 naming; screenshot audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-mind-path-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** Rename to Y3.U1–Y3.U3 in js/data/mind.js (nothing released references them yet) and the plan.

### JM-05 · P3 · content — Japanese lesson inspector reads '0 of 1 sessions'

- **Detail / repro:** On a Japanese Stage 1 lesson (e.g. #/ref/ja/1.1) the inspector's check 'Passed the practice' has the sub-line '0 of 1 sessions' — a hard-coded string with the wrong plural.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/jatopic.js:297 ev.practised ? "passed" : "0 of 1 sessions"; screenshot audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/ja-mind/e-ja-1.1-mac.png (inspector, third check)

## CR — Creatives study space (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** Creatives is broadly complete and renders without console errors at every size, but its flows have several rough joins. The most important root causes are: render-time side effects (the Practice Log opens a work dialog from the route on every redraw, so the dialog bounces back after Promote/Take out, and app dialogs are never closed on navigation); the phone shell only carries page actions marked data-phone-action, so on iPhone Creatives settings are unreachable and every sub-page is titled 'Creatives'; hand-rolled controls that skip shared primitives (the 'Your days' circles are off-centre ovals, the inspector checklist collides with the check page's .k-cr-check grid, the subject pill strips use an undeclared .k-seg overflow that hides the active pill on iPad); and evidence rules that are looser than invariant 154 (a project pays 150 XP for three bare 'Mark done' taps, a Writing study saves with only its given setup, the Writing Desk saves twice on a double tap and makes a new draft on every Start). Layout polish issues remain on the desk (taller hero and stretched analytics vs other spaces, an empty paused Daily card), the check page (no beginner picture, blank re-check), the Add page and the iPad-portrait sketchbook.

**Coverage.** Empty account and a seeded dense account with 28 planted works (art pictures, writing texts, design), promoted pieces, milestones and a drill batch. DOM measurement script (overflow, clipping, overlap, off-centre glyphs, ovals, small targets) over #/subject/creatives (desk), #/creatives (tracks), write, write/<draft>, drills and all six drill types, refs, portfolio, log, log/<id>, check/<id>, project/A1,W1,A2, settings, add/<study>, sketch/<study>, and lessons A1.1, W1.1, D1.1, A3.3, at mac 1470x832, ipad 820x1180 and iphone 402x874; ipadl 1180x820 spot-checked on the sketchbook and a lesson. Screenshots read: desk (mac/ipad/iphone, empty and dense, Dawn), analytics help, portfolio, log (mac, iphone, empty), work dialog, Writing Desk list and draft, lesson notes and Micro-studies tabs (mac), iPhone lesson, check page, project, drills index, Hierarchy (iphone), Palette (Dawn), References, sketchbook (ipad), Add page, Tracks (iphone), settings (ipad, shop theme azure-butterfly). Interactions exercised by eval: deep-link dialog and redraw, Take out of Portfolio, Writing Desk double save, study Start twice, project Mark done x3, invalid routes, phone section menu. Not examined: the Kurenai critique request/reply (needs a live AI route), sprint in Focus end to end, Momentum mode drawing with a real pen, the generated reference kinds other than Forms, iPhone Safari's 760 px viewport separately, and VoiceOver/keyboard traversal beyond names and target sizes.

### CR-01 · P1 · bug — Practice Log work dialog reopens itself after every action and stacks on each redraw (#/creatives/log/<id>)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Saving from the Writing Desk, a drill or Add your work lands on #/creatives/log/<id>, whose render schedules openWork(id). Any KOS.rerender() while that route is current (the dialog's own 'Take out of Portfolio', the Promote dialog's 'Promote', or any background redraw) draws the page again and opens the work dialog AGAIN. Repro: go to #/creatives, then #/creatives/log/<id>; click 'Take out of Portfolio' -> the dialog closes and immediately re-opens (now showing 'Promote…'). KOS.rerender() with it open -> two identical stacked dialogs (10 buttons). The route never drops the id, so the user cannot act on the work without it bouncing back.
- **Evidence:** eval: after cr.promote click count of [data-ui="cr.dialog"] stays 1 with button now 'Promote…'; after KOS.rerender() count 1 -> 2; js/modules/creatives.js:567 'if (wid && CR().work(wid)) setTimeout(function () { openWork(wid); }, 0);' inside logPage; :542 and :559 call d.close(); KOS.rerender(); landing routes js/modules/crwrite.js:147, crdrills.js:85, creatives.js:620
- **Root cause:** Render-time side effect: logPage opens a dialog from the route arg on every render, and the route keeps the id.
- **Proposed fix:** Open the deep-linked dialog only on navigation, not on redraw (check KOS.ui.redrawing / a one-shot flag), and after opening replace the route with #/creatives/log (KOS.show with replace) or clear the id when the dialog closes; the row's open state can stay as a highlight.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-02 · P1 · ux — On iPhone, Creatives settings (and Start focus) cannot be reached: the gear lives in the page actions, which the phone shell hides

- **Devices:** iphone
- **Detail / repro:** The ⚙ 'Creatives settings' button and 'Start focus' are put in #page-actions by spaceHead/KOS.shell.actions. Under 700 px the stage head is display:none and only nodes marked data-phone-action are moved into the phone title bar; neither button is marked, and the section switch menu lists only Code, Japanese, Mind, Creatives, A-levels, Tracks, Writing, Drills, References, Portfolio. So on the iPhone there is no way to change Your days, Minutes or the Exam Season pause (which hides the Daily Creative for weeks) except by typing #/creatives/settings.
- **Evidence:** eval --size iphone on #/subject/creatives: [data-ui="cr.settings-open"] height 0, hidden by DIV.k-stage-head; [data-ui="cr.focus"] height 0; section menu items as listed (no Settings); css/layout.css:445 ':root[data-shell="ready"] .k-stage-head { display: none; }'; js/modules/mobile-shell.js:559-562 (moves only [data-phone-action]); js/modules/creatives.js:69-70 settingsBtn and :295-296 spaceHead (no data-phone-action)
- **Root cause:** Space page actions are not marked for the phone title bar.
- **Proposed fix:** Mark the gear (and Start focus, or rely on the Focus tab) with data-phone-action so the phone title bar carries it, or add 'Settings' to KOS.crPages' phone menu.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-03 · P2 · ux — A work dialog stays open over other pages after navigation (Back, hash change), stacking duplicates

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With the work dialog open on #/creatives/log/<id>, navigating to #/creatives/portfolio or #/home leaves the dialog (and its scrim) covering the new page; returning to the deep link opens a second copy. During the measurement run three copies of a deleted work's dialog were stacked over Portfolio. Browser Back / iPad edge-swipe therefore does not dismiss the dialog the way a user expects (invariant 64: browser chrome and OS gestures are one stack).
- **Evidence:** eval: [cr.dialog] count 1 after location.hash='#/creatives/portfolio', 2 after returning to log/<id>, still 2 on #/home; shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-portfolio.png (first capture: dialog of a removed work over Portfolio); js/core/ui.js:155 openDialog has no navigation hook; js/core/router.js:302 hashchange handler does not close open dialogs
- **Root cause:** KOS.ui.openDialog dialogs are not tied to the route; the router never closes them.
- **Proposed fix:** In router.js, before rendering a navigation (not a redraw), close every open KOS.ui dialog (or have openDialog register a close on the next route change); keep it out of KOS.rerender.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-04 · P2 · ui — Creatives desk hero is taller than the other study spaces' heroes (341 vs 292-339 px) and its analytics card is stretched with empty bands

- **Devices:** mac, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** At 1470x832 the desk-top row is 341 px for Creatives vs Code 292, Mind 296, Japanese 339: four track bars in the hero push it taller than Code/Mind, so the study-space heroes are not one size. The second row is worse: the 'Subject analytics' card is 378 px tall (253 px on Code, Japanese and Mind) because it stretches to the Portfolio side card; its two tile rows are 163 px apart instead of 100, leaving a ~60 px empty band under each value.
- **Evidence:** eval on #/subject/<code|ja|mind|creatives> at mac: study.desk-top heights 292/339/296/341; section.k-sa height 253/253/253/378; tile label tops 591 and 754 (Creatives) vs 511 and 611 (Code); shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-desk.png
- **Root cause:** The desk skeleton lets each space's hero content set its height; the analytics row stretches to its side card.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the four study-space heroes one fixed min/max height token (or cap the track-bar block to the ring's height with a compact bar layout), and align the analytics row with align-items:start plus a capped Portfolio list (3 items) so the stat grid keeps its natural 253 px height.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-05 · P2 · ux — Daily Creative card in Exam Season is a tall empty card with no way to do today's study anyway

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** During Exam Season (on by default) the Daily Creative reads only 'Paused for Exam Season / Back after Sat 5 Dec.' in a 207 px card (mac) beside the hero: half of it empty, and no action ('Do one anyway', 'Change in settings'). The pause is a setting but the card offers no route to it, so the space's main daily entry point is a dead end for weeks.
- **Evidence:** shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-desk.png, dense-ipad-desk.png, dense-iphone-desk.png; js/modules/creatives.js:160-200 (dailyCard)
- **Proposed fix:** Add a quiet 'Do one anyway' (planDay ignoring the pause) and a 'Settings' link inside the paused state; shrink the card to content or let Continue take the freed height.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-06 · P2 · ui — Subject pill strip hides the active 'Creatives' pill off the right edge on iPad portrait

- **Devices:** ipad
- **Detail / repro:** At 820x1180 the subject group (Code · Japanese · Mind · Creatives · A-levels) is a .k-seg with overflow-x:auto and hidden scrollbars, 281 px wide for 456 px of pills, scrolled to 0. The current subject, Creatives, sits at x 355-457 while the strip ends at 369, so only a 14 px white sliver of the active pill shows and A-levels is unreachable without discovering a hidden horizontal scroll.
- **Evidence:** eval --size ipad: active .k-seg-item 'Creatives' left 355 right 457, scroller .k-seg left 88 right 369 scrollLeft 0 scrollWidth 456 clientWidth 281; shots/creatives/n/dense-ipad-desk.png (white half-pill beside 'Mind'); css/components.css:52 .k-seg overflow-x:auto; scrollbar-width:none; strip built in js/core/ui.js:1445-1480
- **Root cause:** Undeclared overflow on the shared .k-seg; no reveal of the active item.
- **Proposed fix:** Build the subject group through KOS.ui.scroller({reveal}) so the active pill is scrolled into view and the overflow is declared with arrows/fade (invariant 50), or collapse the group to the active subject plus a menu below 1080 px.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-07 · P2 · ui — Settings 'Your days': the S M T W T F S letters sit at the left edge of their circles, and on touch the circles are 40x44 ovals

- **Devices:** all; oval on ipad and iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Every day button's letter is 12-15 px left of centre at all three sizes (text-align inherits 'start'). On iPad and iPhone (pointer: coarse) the shared touch rule raises only the height to 44 px, so each 40 px circle becomes a 40x44 oval, which invariant 69 forbids ('a round control must raise its width too').
- **Evidence:** measure: button[cr.day] dx=-14.6/-13.0/-15.0/-12.3 px at mac, ipad and iphone; size 40.0x44.0 at ipad/iphone; computed text-align:start, min-height 44px; shots/creatives/n/dense-ipad-settings.png; css/views/creatives.css:179 (.k-cr-day: inline-size/block-size var(--cr-day)=40px, no text-align, no display:grid); css/base.css:24 ':where(button){text-align: inherit}'; css/base.css:115-120 coarse min-block-size; css/tokens.css:420 --cr-day: 40px
- **Root cause:** A hand-rolled round button that does not centre its label nor follow the coarse-pointer circle rule.
- **Proposed fix:** .k-cr-day { display:grid; place-items:center; } and under (pointer: coarse) set inline-size and block-size to var(--hit-coarse) (44px) together (or use the shared round icon-button primitive).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-08 · P2 · ux — A Writing study's '▶ Start' (and a project's 'Write it') makes a NEW draft every time instead of reopening the unfinished one

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Start a Writing study, type, leave without 'Save to Practice Log', come back to the lesson: the study card still says '▶ Start' and pressing it creates a second draft from the setup text, so the user lands on a fresh page and their writing seems gone (it is only in the drafts list). Same for 'Write it' on a Writing project milestone. Pressing Start twice produced two identical 'W1.1·a Ten things, ten sentences' drafts.
- **Evidence:** eval: KOS.crWrite.openStudy('W1.1','a') twice -> #/creatives/write/mvzi1k0viya1 and #/creatives/write/mvzi1lmk5f7t; drafts list shows two identical rows; js/modules/crwrite.js:34-43 (openStudy/openMilestone always create()); js/modules/crtopic.js:40 and :71-72 (study card offers only '▶ Start'/'Another rep'); js/modules/creatives.js:601
- **Root cause:** Draft creation is not keyed to its study; the study card does not read drafts.
- **Proposed fix:** In openStudy/openMilestone look up the newest unsaved draft with the same lesson+study (or unit+ms) and open it; on the study card show 'Continue draft' when one exists, keeping 'Another rep' for a deliberate new one.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-09 · P2 · bug — Writing Desk 'Save to Practice Log' (and Add your work's Save) saves twice on a double tap

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Writing Desk save button is never disabled while the IndexedDB write runs, so a double click/tap creates two identical works in the Practice Log (and two check records to manage). The sketchbook and drills already guard their save (disabled + 'Saving…'); the Writing Desk and the Add page do not.
- **Evidence:** eval on #/creatives/write/<draft>: b.click(); b.click() -> works +2, two 'W1.1·a Ten things, ten sentences 49' records; js/modules/crwrite.js:136-148 (no disabled); js/modules/creatives.js:617-631 (addPage go.onclick, no guard) vs js/modules/crsketch.js:67 and crdrills.js:83 which disable
- **Proposed fix:** Disable the button and show 'Saving…' on first press (as crsketch.js:67), re-enable on failure.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-10 · P2 · data — A Writing study can be 'saved' with only the given setup text: the untouched 80% counts as made work

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** openStudy pre-fills the draft with the study's setup. Save only checks that the textarea is non-empty, so pressing ▶ Start then 'Save to Practice Log' without writing a word records a 49-word work, ticks 'studies saved' for the lesson and leads straight to the self-marked check that pays 8 XP/1 gold. That contradicts invariant 154 ('creative evidence is made, not claimed').
- **Evidence:** eval: draft opened by KOS.crWrite.openStudy('W1.1','a') saved unchanged -> work 'W1.1·a Ten things, ten sentences' words 49; js/modules/crwrite.js:36-39 (text: s.setup) and :139 (only area.value.trim() checked)
- **Proposed fix:** Refuse to save (toast 'Write your part first') while the text still equals the setup, or count only words beyond the setup (store setup length on the doc and use it for words/the guard).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-11 · P2 · ux — Writing Desk drafts cannot be deleted or tidied: every 'New draft' and every study Start adds a permanent row

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The drafts list (#/creatives/write) has rows that only open a draft; there is no Delete, archive or 'saved to log' status anywhere in crwrite.js. 'New draft' writes an empty 'Untitled' record immediately, and studies create a new draft on each Start, so abandoned and already-saved drafts pile up forever on the device with no way to remove them.
- **Evidence:** grep -i 'delet|remove|.del(' js/modules/crwrite.js -> no match; js/modules/crwrite.js:75 (New draft creates immediately), :83-88 (row = open only); shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-write.png
- **Proposed fix:** Add a row menu (Delete with confirm, through KOS.ui.confirm danger), mark drafts already saved to the Practice Log ('Saved · 2 Nov'), and create a 'New draft' record only on first input.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CR-12 · P2 · ui — Lesson inspector checklist: each label sits half a column away from its checkbox (.k-cr-check class collision)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On every Creatives lesson the inspector's four evidence checks (Read the notes, Saved the studies, Passed the check, A theory card held) render the checkbox at the left and the label 132 px to the right in a second grid column, leaving an empty gap between them. The class .k-cr-check is used both for these labels and for the check page's two-column layout; the later rule (display:grid; repeat(2,1fr); gap 20px) wins.
- **Evidence:** shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-lessonA11.png; eval on #/ref/creatives/A1.1 at mac: label.k-cr-check display grid, grid-template-columns 111.5px 111.5px, input left 1207, label span left 1339; css/views/creatives.css:107 (.k-cr-check flex, intended) overridden by :117 (.k-cr-check grid for 'Good for this unit') and :358; js/modules/crtopic.js:240 (label class 'k-ts-check k-cr-check') and js/modules/creatives.js:391 (check page 'k-cr-check')
- **Root cause:** Two unrelated components share one class name in css/views/creatives.css.
- **Proposed fix:** Rename one of the two (e.g. the check page's container to .k-cr-checkpage) and keep .k-cr-check flex for the inspector rows; smoke55 could flag duplicate class definitions in one view file.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-13 · P2 · ui — iPhone lesson page: the tab strip gets 181 px, so only 'Notes' and half of 'Micro-studies' show; Theory cards and Project are hidden behind an arrow

- **Devices:** iphone
- **Detail / repro:** At 402 px the topic tab row shares one line with the 'Working' and 'Edit' buttons (about 95 + 66 px). The tabs scroller is left 181 px for four tabs (Notes 16-67, Micro-studies 85-185, Theory cards 203-298, Project 316-361), so a Creatives lesson's own studies, cards and project are effectively off-screen; the user has to discover the › arrow. The Studies tab is where the lesson's work is started, so this hides the main action on the phone.

### CR-14 · P2 · bug — The check page shows an Art study's 'Expected beginner output' as text only: the beginner picture is dropped

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The check (31e) exists to put your work beside the expected beginner output. For Art lessons the content's beginner block carries art: 'A2.1' and the imported picture cr.beginner.A2.1.webp exists (the lesson page draws it), but checkPage builds the card from beginner.text and beginner.note only, so an art student compares their drawing with two sentences. The card also sits under the work in the left column instead of beside it, leaving the right column empty below the form.
