- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-15 · P2 · ux — Reopening a study's check shows a blank form and silently overwrites the earlier result (a pass can become 'Not yet')

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** 'Do the check' / 'Check' on a work that already has a check draws both questions unanswered with no sign of the earlier answer, note or date. Saving again replaces w.check entirely, so re-answering 'Not yet' on a passed work un-passes it and can untick the lesson's 'Passed the check' evidence, with no confirmation. The page also has no way back except the header nav until after Save.
- **Evidence:** shots/creatives/n/dense-mac-check.png (work A2.1.a #0 whose check is pass, form blank); js/modules/creatives.js:354 'answers = L.check.rows.map(function () { return null; })' (never reads w.check), :364 setCheck overwrite; js/core/creatives.js:163-170 (w.check replaced)
- **Proposed fix:** Prefill answers and note from w.check and show 'Checked 2 Nov · passed'; when a pass would turn into Not yet, say so on the button ('Change to Not yet') or keep the earlier pass on the record; add a 'Back to the lesson' link in the page header.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-16 · P2 · a11y — Hierarchy drill: five identical 'Bold' toggles with no line in their name, 32 px tall on touch; − M + read as plain text

- **Devices:** all; target size on ipad/iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Each line of the Hierarchy drill has −, a size letter, + and a 'Bold' toggle. The five Bold buttons are all named just 'Bold' (the −/+ buttons do name their line), so a screen reader hears 'Bold, toggle' five times. On iPad/iPhone each Bold is 57x32 px, under the 44 px touch minimum (invariant 69). The −/+ icon buttons have the card's own background and no border, so on the phone they look like loose glyphs beside an 'M' rather than controls, and the size letter (S/M/L/XL…) is unexplained. On mac the long fifth line truncates ('Very friendly, small whi…', 428>410 px; 428>133 on iPhone).
- **Evidence:** shots/creatives/n/iphone-hierarchy.png; eval --size iphone: Bold 57x32, aria-label null x5; −/+ 44x44 bg oklch(0.2 0.009 265) border 0; measure clip span.k-crd-ctrl-t 428>410 (mac), 428>133 (iphone); js/modules/crdrills.js:220 (bold button, no aria-label), :223; css/views/creatives.css:259-262
- **Proposed fix:** aria-label 'Bold: <line>' (keep aria-pressed); give .k-crd-opt the coarse 44 px height; draw −/+ with the bordered icon-button style and label the size readout ('Size M'); let the line name wrap to two lines instead of ellipsis.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-17 · P2 · ux — On iPhone, Creatives sub-pages lose their title: Practice Log, Settings, The check and Project all read 'Creatives'

Pages that are not in KOS.crPages (log, settings, check, project, add, sketch) light the 'Creatives' subject pill, so the bar says 'Study / Creatives' and the real page header ('Practice Log · Only you see this', 'Creatives settings', 'The check', 'Project') is hidden with the stage head. A drill page shows 'Drills' instead of the drill's name. The user cannot tell where they are, and the Practice Log's privacy line disappears.

### CR-19 · P2 · ui — 'Add your work' is a bare 720 px form with a raw browser file button, a 490 px dead column beside it, no preview and no drop target

The file field is an unstyled native <input type=file> ('Choose file  No file chosen', system grey), there is no preview of the chosen picture, no drop/paste target (the app's Files flow supports drop, paste and Scan), no Cancel/back to the lesson, and the study line is plain text above the Title. It looks unfinished next to the rest of the space.

### CR-20 · P2 · data — A unit project pays 150 XP, 40 gold and +10 HP for three 'Mark done' taps with no work saved

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On a fresh account, #/creatives/project/A2 offers 'Mark done' on every milestone even though no study or milestone work exists ('Best after A2.3's studies'). Three taps reached every milestone and paid the project (XP 0 -> 150, gold 0 -> 40, paid['project:A2'] set) with zero works in the Practice Log. Invariant 154 says creative evidence is made, not claimed, and every other Creatives reward needs a saved piece; projects are the largest reward in the space and need none. 'Undo' after the payout keeps the pay.
- **Evidence:** eval on empty account: 3 x click 'Mark done' on #/creatives/project/A2 -> governor xp/gold [0,0] -> [150,40], creatives.paid {project:A2}, works 0; js/core/creatives.js:200-218 (markMilestone 'or by hand', pays when all reached); js/modules/creatives.js:~600 (Mark done button per milestone)
- **Root cause:** markMilestone accepts a bare tick as evidence and pays on it.
- **Proposed fix:** Let a hand-marked milestone count only when a work is attached (offer 'Attach a piece from the Practice Log' / 'Add your work' instead of a bare tick), or keep hand ticks for progress but pay the project only when each milestone has a saved work.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CR-21 · P3 · ui — Work dialog: the picture sits left with a 252 px empty strip beside it, the note is jammed under it, and nothing says when/where the piece was made

The header mark is the generic 創 rather than the track's mark used on the row.

### CR-24 · P3 · ux — Palette drill: the Hue sliders are plain accent bars with no spectrum and no value, so you pick a hue blind

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Each colour in 'Palette and contrast' has Lightness and Hue range inputs styled identically (accent fill on a grey track). The hue track shows no colour wheel/spectrum and neither slider prints its value, so moving Hue gives feedback only in the swatch; on a drill about colour this is the main control.
- **Evidence:** shots/creatives/n/dawn-mac-palette.png; js/modules/crdrills.js:273-280 (k-crd-range, aria-label '… · Hue', 0-359)
- **Proposed fix:** Paint the hue input's track with a linear-gradient over oklch hues at the colour's lightness/chroma and the lightness track from dark to light; show the numeric value beside each label.
- **Flags:** effort S

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Practice Log rows show the track glyph instead of a thumbnail for picture works; a small thumbnail would make the log scannable (js/modules/creatives.js workRow).
- Practice Log has no filter by track or lesson and renders every work at once; add a track filter and 60-row lazy batches before it grows.
- Empty Practice Log and Writing Desk empty states have no action ('Start A1.1', 'New draft').
- Project page milestones that are done do not link to the work that reached them, and the page has no link back to its unit's lessons.
- The desk's 'Start here' card (empty account) is 340 px tall for a sentence and a button.
- Writing Desk drafts autosave on a 600 ms debounce only; flush on pagehide so a draft typed just before closing the iPad app is kept.
- The subject strip lights 'Creatives' on every page outside KOS.crPages (log, settings, check, project); a lit 'Practice Log'/'Settings' state would orient the user.

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- A Portfolio view per track with a lightbox and export/share of a chosen piece.
- Attach saved Practice Log pieces to project milestones instead of bare ticks.
- A phone composition for the sketchbook and Writing Desk on iPad portrait (toolbar-first, side panel as a drawer).


---

## PR — Focus, Reminders, Routines, Daily Review, Calendar, Pacing (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** Productivity is broadly functional on all three devices, but four P1 problems stand out. (1) KOS.ui.menu panels sit at --z-menu 110, under the modal layer at 120, so any menu inside a dialog is invisible and can't be clicked. This is why the Afternoon review's 'Place in a free block' does nothing, and About to Study's 'Change' is broken the same way. (2) KOS.show assigns state.ui.view only after rendering, so every page header paints the previous page's band after in-app navigation. (3) About to Study ignores the Focus page's 'General study — no link' choice and links the session to the first assignment, even through 'Just start'. Time is then banked on the wrong assignment. (4) The running Focus screen has no width cap on the assignment chip, so long titles run off the viewport. Calendar has no Agenda or Day view on Mac/iPad: Agenda only exists in the phone branch of render(). The rest is polish: dead space (Routines offer card, the Reminders inspector, the Focus page while minimised, the routine Today columns), touch targets under 44px (Pacing ticks and day selects, Reminders circles, routine pick boxes drawn as 22x44 capsules), the review time strip's overlapping labels, and wording (repeated streak notes, raw ISO due dates).

**Coverage.** Seeded and empty accounts. Mac 1470x832, iPad 820x1180 and 1180x820 (touch), iPhone 402x874. A DOM measurement sweep ran over #/focus, #/reminders, #/routines (today, routines, templates, history, settings, edit/habits), #/calendar and #/pacing (week and braid) at mac/ipad/iphone, seeded and empty. Screenshots I looked at: Focus idle, About to Study, running stage, break, mini player (Mac), phone dock, switch dialog, completion review (Mac and iPhone); Reminders All and Today with 'From your plan' and the empty state; Routines Today offer, routine card, list, editor, player (Mac and iPhone), history, settings; Calendar Month (Mac, iPad landscape), Week (Mac, iPad portrait), phone Agenda/Month; Pacing week (Mac, iPhone) and braid; Daily Review afternoon (Mac, iPad) and evening. The 'Place in a free block' bug was reproduced and root-caused by measurement and a programmatic click. Not examined: the idle watch end to end (30-minute timers; code not traced), the Stopwatch, Custom and Study-until modes running, the Morning review's 'pick three' step, the Templates detail and the routine editor's step '⋯' menu, the habit editor with real habits (the seed has none), Reminders' edit inspector, sub-tasks and recurrence editor, the Calendar event modal, the Pacing edit-week and class-days dialogs, the Dawn theme and a shop theme (no time left), and the iPad Home Screen standalone mode.

### PR-01 · P1 · bug — Afternoon review: 'Place in a free block' menu opens BEHIND the review dialog, so it does nothing

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Daily Review > Afternoon > step 3 Time left. Pressing 'Place in a free block' sets aria-expanded=true and appends the menu panel to <body>, but the panel is z-index var(--z-menu)=110 while the review overlay is var(--z-modal)=120, so the menu is painted under the dialog's scrim and panel: nothing visible appears, and a tap where the item would be lands on the dialog (elementFromPoint at the first item's centre returns another row's 'Place in a free block' button). The item's onSelect itself works: invoking it programmatically creates the study block (15:30, 90 min, type study, assignmentId 1) with an Undo toast. Same root cause affects EVERY KOS.ui.menu used inside a KOS.ui.openDialog (the primitive never raises a menu above an open modal). Also reproduced on About to Study's 'Change ▾' task menu (preflight dialog): panel z=110, elementFromPoint at its first item returns the dialog's k-field, not the menu.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/dr-place-open-mac.png (menu open per aria-expanded, nothing shown); eval: panel z=110, overlay z=120, panel parent BODY, topEl at item centre = 'k-btn k-btn--sm ui.menu-button'; css/tokens.css:1031-1032 (--z-menu:110, --z-modal:120); css/components.css:334 (.k-menu-panel z-index var(--z-menu)); js/core/ui.js:813 (place() appends panel to document.body); js/modules/dayreview.js:609
- **Root cause:** Menu layer token (110) below the modal layer token (120); menus are portalled to body regardless of an open modal.
- **Proposed fix:** In KOS.ui.menu place(): when the button is inside an open dialog (btn.closest('[data-ui~="ui.dialog"], .k-dialog-overlay')), append the panel inside that overlay (or set a state the CSS maps to z-index above --z-modal, e.g. a --z-menu-modal token 130). Add a smoke test that opens a menu inside openDialog and checks elementFromPoint hits the item.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-02 · P1 · bug — Every page header shows the PREVIOUS page's painted band after in-app navigation

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.ui.pageHeader picks its art from KOS.store.state.ui.view, but KOS.show sets state.ui.view only AFTER the view function has rendered. So navigating Pacing -> Routines draws Pacing's band on Routines, Routines -> Focus draws Reminders' band (Routines maps to 'reminders') on the Focus Timer, Focus -> Reminders draws none (Focus has no PAGE_ART entry), etc. Only a reload (where ui.view was persisted) shows the right picture. Repro via eval navigating #/pacing,#/routines,#/focus,#/reminders,#/calendar,#/routines: header images were reminders, pacing, reminders, none, none, calendar respectively. Affects every page that uses pageHeader, not only Productivity; the art audit just released depends on it.
- **Evidence:** eval output: '#/pacing [app.hero.page.reminders.webp]', '#/routines [app.hero.page.pacing.webp]', '#/focus [app.hero.page.reminders.webp]', '#/routines [app.hero.page.calendar.webp]'; shots/productivity/focus-mac-seed.png and routines-mac-seed-2.png (sticky-note pinboard band on Focus/Routines); js/core/ui.js:589-592 (art = PAGE_ART[state.ui.view]); js/core/ui.js:1541-1543 (view rendered, then ui.view assigned)
- **Root cause:** Ordering: state.ui.view is assigned after render in KOS.show (js/core/ui.js:1543), while pageHeader reads it during render.
- **Proposed fix:** Set a module variable (e.g. KOS.ui.currentView = viewId) BEFORE calling KOS.views[viewId] and read that in pageHeader; or have each view pass opts.art explicitly. Add a smoke check: navigate A->B and assert B's band id.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-03 · P1 · data — Focus: 'General study — no link' is overridden by About to Study, which silently links the session to the first assignment (even with 'Just start')

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On #/focus with subject 'General study — no link' and 'No assignment', pressing Start focus opens About to Study pre-filled with the overdue NEA assignment. Pressing 'Just start' (or Start Focus) starts a session with subject 'compsci' and assignmentId 1. The completed session then banks its minutes on that assignment (invariant 4a) and counts as CS study, i.e. time is misattributed. The only escape in the dialog, 'Change ▾', is a KOS.ui.menu that opens behind the dialog (same z-index bug), so there is no way to keep 'no link' short of turning off About to Study.
- **Evidence:** eval: Focus page left on General study/no assignment -> Start focus -> preflight.just-start -> session {subject:'compsci', assignmentId:1}; shots/productivity/focus-running-mac.png (About to Study shows NEA); js/modules/routineplayer.js:279-287 (taskOf falls back to taskChoices()[0] when ctx has no subject/assignment), 257 (go(skip) merges task even when skip); js/modules/focus.js:2253
- **Root cause:** taskOf() treats an empty subject as 'nothing chosen' and fills it from Today's three/assignments; go(true) still merges the filled task.
- **Proposed fix:** taskOf(ctx) must respect an explicit 'no link' from the Focus page: pass ctx.explicit = true from focus.js and return {title:'General study'} without falling back; offer the ladder's suggestion as a 'Use: <task>' choice instead of a default. 'Just start' should start exactly the Focus page's config (ctx without task). Fix the menu z-index too.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-04 · P1 · ui — Running Focus: a long assignment title runs off the screen (left edge on Mac, right edge on iPhone)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** The objective/context column on the running Focus screen holds the assignment chip ('課 <title>') with no max width, wrapping or ellipsis. With the NEA assignment the k-fx-ctx box is 616px wide: on the Mac it starts at x=-131 (text cut at the window's left edge: 'tion: stakeholder interviews…'), on iPhone it ends at x=648 in a 402px viewport. The due date is printed raw ('due 2026-10-29'), as are the Focus page's assignment options ('· due 2026-10-29'). On iPhone the context card is also washed out over the bright window art (chips barely legible).
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/focus-running-mac.png (left edge), shots/productivity/focus-running-iphone.png; eval: .k-fx-ctx rect x=-131..485 (mac), 32..648 (iphone, vw 402); js/modules/focus.js:879-883 (chip('課 '+asg.title), chip('due '+asg.due)), 1018, 2175; css/views/productivity.css:113-114
- **Root cause:** Chips are nowrap and the flex column is aligned to the end with no width cap, so long content overflows to the left (end-aligned) or right.
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-fx-ctx max-inline-size:100% (min-inline-size:0 on the column) and the chip a max-inline-size with text-overflow:ellipsis (title attr for the full name); format due with KOS.ui/hub dayLabel ('due Thu 29 Oct', 'overdue 4 d'). On phone put the context card on a stronger scrim (var(--s1) at ~80%).
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-05 · P2 · ui — Afternoon review time strip: end label '22:00 夜' overprints the last hour tick; 30-minute block text wraps to two lines

- **Devices:** all, worst on iPad portrait/iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** In the Time left strip the hour ticks are left-anchored at their position and run up to the last hour before the evening review, while the end label is pinned at inline-end. With from=04:46 and until=22:00 the 21:00 tick starts at ~94% of the width, exactly under '22:00 夜' (the screenshot shows '2̶2̶:̶0̶0̶' overprinted). Short free blocks (30 min = ~31px) wrap '30 min' onto two lines and overflow their outline. Same overlap happens at any width; worse on iPad portrait and phone where the strip is narrower.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/dr-afternoon-timeleft-mac.png (top-right of strip, and the 08:00 block); js/modules/dayreview.js:593-604 (tick loop t<to; tend appended unconditionally); css/views/routines.css:340-341; shots/productivity/dr-afternoon-ipad-empty.png (iPad portrait: ticks 20:00/22:00 and '22:00 夜' overprinted, '30 mi' clipped)
- **Root cause:** No collision handling between left-anchored ticks and an inline-end end label; fixed hourly tick density regardless of width.
- **Proposed fix:** Skip any tick whose position is within ~1 label width (e.g. >88%) of the end label, or render the end label as the final tick; thin ticks by available width (every 2h/3h below ~700px). For blocks narrower than the label, show the length only in the aria/tooltip or as a single-line '30m' with white-space:nowrap and no padding.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-06 · P2 · ux — Calendar has no Agenda (or Day) view on Mac or iPad; Agenda exists only under 700px

- **Devices:** mac, ipad, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** At >700px the Calendar offers only Month and Week (js/modules/calendar.js:1545 mode is 'week'|'month'; buttons at 1589-1591). The Agenda list (renderPhone, the stored 'week' mode) is drawn only when matchMedia('(max-width:700px)') matches (calendar.js:1562-1563, 1613). So the user cannot get the agenda on the MacBook (1470) or iPad (820/1180). There is no Day view at any size. Also the same stored value calMode='week' means 'Week grid' on desktop and 'Agenda' on phone, so an iPad in Split View flipping across 700px silently changes view type.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/cal-week-mac.png, cal-week-ipad.png, cal-month-ipadl.png (segmented control: Month | Week only); shots/productivity/calendar-iphone-seed.png (Agenda | Month); js/modules/calendar.js:1545,1562-1563,1589-1591,1613-1620
- **Root cause:** Agenda was designed as the phone composition (frame 15g) and is hard-wired to the phone branch of render().
- **Proposed fix:** Make the mode a 3-value per-device setting {month, week, agenda} (+ optional day). Factor the phone agenda list (renderPhone's day lists + agendaRows) into a renderAgenda(board) usable at any width: on desktop/iPad draw it as the board beside the existing Today/Countdowns side column (a 2-week scrolling list grouped by day with time column, hue bar, derived chips). Keep phone default Agenda; migrate stored 'week' on phone to 'agenda'.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### PR-07 · P2 · ui — iPad portrait Calendar: '+ New event' wraps to its own row leaving a full-width empty band

- **Devices:** ipad
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px the header row (‹ title › Today Show Month|Week) fills the width and '+ New event' drops alone to a second line at the left, leaving ~600px empty beside it and pushing the grid down ~60px.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/cal-week-ipad.png (y≈235)
- **Root cause:** .k-cal-head is a wrapping flex row with no layout tier between desktop and phone.
- **Proposed fix:** Below 1080 put Today/Show on a second toolbar row with New event at its end, or collapse '+ New event' to the '+' icon button used on phone; or let the title shrink (clamp font-size) so the row fits.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-08 · P2 · ui — Routine offer checkboxes render as 22x44 tall pills on touch devices

- **Devices:** ipad, ipadl, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** In 'Start with these?' (Routines Today/Routines/Templates tabs) each pick box (.k-rt-check, routine.offer-pick) keeps its 22px width while the coarse-pointer rule raises its height to 44px, so on iPad and iPhone each checkbox is a white 22x44 capsule instead of a square. Invariant 69: a control raised for touch must raise its width too.
- **Evidence:** measure: 'SMALL button.k-rt-check[routine.offer-pick] 22x44' on ipad and iphone; shots/productivity/routines-iphone-seed.png (tall white capsules)
- **Root cause:** Global coarse-pointer min-block-size for buttons without a matching min-inline-size on this control.
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-rt-check an explicit aspect-ratio:1 / inline-size equal to block-size under (pointer:coarse), or keep the visual 22px box and grow the hit area with the whole row as the label (the row is already a button-like card).
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-09 · P2 · ui — Routines 'Start with these?' card leaves ~400px dead on the right on Mac

- **Devices:** mac, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** The offer card spans the full content width (1210px) but the illustration + a ~560px text/list column use only the left two thirds; the right ~400px is empty. Violates the no-dead-space rule.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/routines-mac-seed-2.png
- **Proposed fix:** Either cap the card's width and place a second card beside it (e.g. Today's reviews), or lay the three template picks out as three columns across the width (they are short), or move the illustration to the right edge so the text sits between.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-10 · P2 · ui — Reminders: the right inspector column is a small hint card over a dead column

- **Devices:** mac, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** With nothing selected, the 320px right column holds only 'Select a reminder to edit…' (120px tall) and is empty below for the whole list length.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/reminders-mac-seed.png, rem-today-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** When nothing is selected, collapse the inspector and let the list widen, or fill it with useful context (Today's count, upcoming this week, 'From your plan' summary, quick-add with date).
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-11 · P2 · ux — Reminders 'Today' leaves out overdue reminders

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Today shows 2 items while 8 reminders are overdue; they are only under Overdue/All. A user working from Today never sees what slipped. Most reminder apps (Apple Reminders) show overdue at the top of Today. Also 'From your plan' rows each carry a '↻ From your plan' chip under a 'FROM YOUR PLAN' heading (redundant), and the 'Open plan' links and priority chips sit on different right edges (≈1082 vs ≈1056).
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/rem-today-mac.png; js/modules/reminders.js:334-349
- **Proposed fix:** Include overdue items in Today under an 'Overdue' group (count it in the Today badge or show '2 + 8 overdue'); drop the per-row chip in the plan group; align the trailing column.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-12 · P2 · ui — Focus page while a session is minimised is one banner over an empty page

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With a session running and minimised, #/focus shows only 'Session in progress — Computer Science' and 'Return to the stage →'; the rest of the 1210x500 content area is blank (Your record, The deal, today's sessions all disappear). Dead space and a lost chance to show live context (objective, cycle progress, notes, today's total).
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/focus-page-minimised-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the idle page's right column (Your record with today's live total, The deal with the running session's live award preview from KOS.governor.focusAward) and in place of the setup card show a compact live card: clock, cycle bar, objective, Switch…, notes, End early/Return to the stage.
- **Flags:** effort M, needs a design prompt

### PR-13 · P2 · a11y — Pacing week: lesson ticks (18x18) and lesson-day selects (62x24) are far below the 44px touch minimum on iPad/iPhone

- **Devices:** ipad, ipadl, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Every class lesson row's tick input (.k-pace-tick-in) measures 18x18 and its day <select> (pace.lesson-day) 62x24 at 820px and 402px with a coarse pointer; the week strip's scroller arrows are 32x32. On the iPad (the user's study device) these are the main controls of the page.
- **Evidence:** measure prod/m-ipad-__pacing.json, m-iphone-__pacing.json: 'SMALL input.k-pace-tick-in 18x18', 'SMALL select.k-pace-lesson-day 62x24', 'SMALL button.k-scroller-arrow 32x32'; shots/productivity/pace-week-iphone.png
- **Proposed fix:** Make the whole lesson row a <label> for its tick (hit area = row height ≥44) and raise the day select to min-block-size 44px under (pointer:coarse) (or use the dense 24px-with-spacing exception explicitly, invariant 69).
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-14 · P3 · ui — Daily Review marks sit in the Week view's all-day row and their time is truncated away

- **Devices:** mac, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** Review marks carry a time (Morning review · 07:00 etc.) but Week view lists them in the all-day row, where the 115px column truncates them to 'Morning revie…', so the time never shows; they also push every day's all-day row to 5-6 lines, shrinking the timed grid.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/cal-week-mac.png (all-day rows 5-6 chips tall); eval: 21 review chips at y 297-401; js/modules/calendar.js:600-606, 1799-1801
- **Proposed fix:** Draw timed derived marks (reviews, routine windows) as thin dashed markers in the timed grid at their time, or as one compact '朝 昼 夜' glyph row per day with the times in the tooltip/aria.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-15 · P3 · ux — Routines: page-header 'Templates' button duplicates the Templates tab 60px below

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The page header's actions are 'Templates' and '+ New routine', and the tab strip right under it has a Templates tab going to the same place. Invariant 50b: never offer the same destination twice in-page.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/routines-mac-seed.png; js/modules/routines.js (routine.templates button)
- **Proposed fix:** Drop the header 'Templates' button (keep + New routine), or drop the Templates tab and keep the button.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-16 · P3 · content — Daily habits card repeats the page sub-line, truncated, and has no add button when empty

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Routines Today: the Daily habits card heading's sub-line 'a habit is a routine with one …' is the page header's sub-line again and is ellipsised; empty it shows a paragraph and only a small 'Add habits ›' text link at top-right (on iPhone the link overlaps the truncated sub-line per measurement). Card height then leaves a dead lower half.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/routines-mac-seed-2.png (Daily habits card); measure iphone: 'OVERLAP span.k-rt-hd-m.k-ellipsis x button.k-link[routine.habits-edit]'; measure mac DEADH routine.habit-card used 95/244
- **Proposed fix:** Remove the sub-line (or make it unique, e.g. 'tick-only, auto-tracked'), and in the empty state put a primary '+ Add a habit' button in the body.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-17 · P3 · content — Session review repeats '36-day streak' four times and hides what the bonus gold was for

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Ending a Pomodoro paid +59 gold (5 for the session + 3+7+14+30 streak milestone bonuses that had not been recorded). The review and the toast print the note list '36-day streak · 36-day streak · 36-day streak · 36-day streak' and a single '+59 gold' while The deal had promised +5 gold, so the user cannot tell why. Each milestone pushes the CURRENT streak length as its note instead of the milestone it paid. The toast also sits over the review's 'Save review' button on Mac.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/focus-review-mac.png, focus-review-iphone.png; eval KOS.governor.lastAward() = {gold:59, notes:['36-day streak'×4]}; js/core/governor.js:330-338 (notes.push(stk + '-day streak'))
- **Proposed fix:** Push one note per milestone: ms + '-day streak bonus +' + ms + ' gold' (or merge into one: 'Streak bonuses 3·7·14·30 days +54 gold'); show the session's own award and bonuses as separate lines in the review. Keep toasts above the dialog footer (offset by the footer height when a dialog is open).
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-18 · P3 · a11y — Reminders: complete circles are 32x32 on touch

- **Devices:** ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** The round complete button (rem.check) stays 32x32 with a coarse pointer on iPad and iPhone (invariant 69 asks 44 for coarse pointer, raising width with height for a circle). The sort/priority pill selects are 30px tall on iPad.
- **Evidence:** measure m-ipad-__reminders.json / m-iphone-__reminders.json: 'SMALL button.k-rem-check 32x32' (×28), 'SMALL select.k-pill-select 161x30'
- **Proposed fix:** Under (pointer:coarse) give .k-rem-check a 44x44 hit box (visual circle can stay 22-24px via an inner span) and the pill selects min-block-size 44px.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-19 · P3 · ui — Daily Review step tabs: the active tab's focus ring is clipped to two vertical crimson bars

- **Devices:** mac, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** After a step tab is clicked, its focus outline renders only as two vertical crimson lines at its left and right (top and bottom are clipped by the step strip's overflow), which reads as a rendering glitch rather than a focus ring. Seen on Mac and iPad, in Afternoon and Evening reviews.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/dr-afternoon-timeleft-mac.png (around 'Time left'), dr-afternoon-ipad-empty.png, dr-evening1-mac.png (around 'Tomorrow')
- **Proposed fix:** Use :focus-visible only (no ring after a pointer click) and an inset ring (outline-offset negative or box-shadow inset) so the scroller's overflow cannot clip it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-20 · P3 · ux — Evening review: 'Tomorrow's first task' says nothing is due while overdue plan rows exist; lesson rows show bare dashes

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In the evening review the first-task card reads 'Nothing is due soon, so tomorrow starts wherever you like.' although the afternoon review and Reminders list overdue carried-over plan rows; the candidates leave out Pacing rows. The card is then a tall empty box beside the timetable. Tomorrow's lessons with no class time print '–' in the time column.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/dr-evening1-mac.png; js/modules/dayreview.js:585 (empty-candidates text)
- **Proposed fix:** Feed carried-over plan rows (KOS.pacing.dueThisWeek().carried) and the Focus ladder's next into the candidates; omit the time cell (or show 'lesson') when a lesson has no time.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-21 · P3 · content — Running Focus in a break: red 'End early' stays in the header beside 'End session'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Once a Pomodoro cycle is banked, the stage shows '✓ End session' (pays +35 XP…) but the header still offers a red 'End early', so there are two end controls with conflicting wording and colour.
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/focus-stage-break-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** Hide 'End early' (or relabel it 'End' in neutral style) when canComplete() is true.
- **Flags:** effort S

### PR-22 · P3 · ui — Routines Today: three equal columns stretch the reviews and habits cards to the routine card's height

- **Devices:** mac, ipadl
- **Detail / repro:** With a routine due, Today's reviews (3 rows, ~200px of content) and Daily habits (one paragraph) are stretched to the Morning Routine card's ~460px, leaving large empty lower halves; the routine card's header is cramped ('Leave by 07:45 / 163 min from now' wraps to 4 lines while the side cards are empty).
- **Evidence:** shots/productivity/rt-today-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** Give the routine card 2 of 3 columns (or a full row) and stack reviews + habits in the third column; align-items:start for the side cards.
- **Flags:** effort S

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Daily Review Time left: after placing a block, mark the row 'Placed 15:30' (and remove that free block) so the same item isn't placed twice.
- Week view: open on the first event or 07:00 when 'now' is outside working hours, instead of centring on 05:00.
- Focus mini player (Mac): show the subject or objective beside the clock; the tiny progress ring next to the time is barely readable.
- Routine player (Mac): it has both an ✕ and a 'Close' in the side panel; keep one.
- Reminders: a 'Place in a free block' / 'Focus on this' action on a reminder, to tie Reminders to Focus and the Calendar.
- Calendar: stored events with unknown types (e.g. 'other' from imports or the seeder) render with no hue at all; run the stored events through KOS.calendar.normalise once at boot.

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- Routines inside routines: a step can reference another routine. Model: add step.sub = <routineId> in normaliseStep (js/core/routines.js:142). Display text and minutes are DERIVED from the sub-routine (mins = the sum of its required steps' mins), never copied. Integrity, enforced in normalise(rec, base) and in the editor: (a) no cycles: walk sub links depth-first from the routine being saved, refusing any step whose sub reaches the parent (also cap depth at 2, so a sub-routine cannot itself contain subs); (b) the sub-routine's total cannot exceed the parent step's budget. Either make the step's mins read-only (= sub total), or, if the user sets a budget, refuse a save of the sub that would push it over and say which parent step it breaks; (c) a sub-routine must be on demand or share the parent's days (kind/anchor), otherwise it would also show on its own schedule. Keep 'days: null' (on demand) as the rule for anything used as a sub; (d) deleting a routine used as a sub is refused, or offers to unlink (steps become plain text with the last derived minutes). Logging: ticking a sub step in the player expands the sub's steps inline, and the parent step counts done when the sub's own completion rule is met that routine day. The sub's log entry is written under its own key with a parent tag, so it pays nothing on its own (pay once per invariant 143: only the parent pays). snapOf must store the sub's id and its frozen step list in the parent's snapshot (x[6] = sub snapshot), so editing the sub never rewrites a past day. The cloud merge needs no change (steps merge whole with the routine). Player: a nested progress bar, 'Shower · from Morning hygiene (3 steps)'.
- Calendar Agenda view for Mac and iPad: a 2-week scrolling day list beside the Today/Countdowns column, sharing the phone's agendaRows.
- Focus page while running: a live in-page session card (clock, cycles, objective, notes, Switch) in place of the empty banner.
- Unify KOS.ui.menu layering: a single top-layer root (or native popover API) that every menu, picker and toast uses above modals.


---

## MG — Money (Santander import) and Goals (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** Money's import is the weak point and explains everything the user reported. The importer only reads a CSV whose FIRST line is a header with exact-match names. Santander midata ('Merchant/Description', 'Debit/Credit', a semicolon delimiter or a blank first line) and Online Banking's CSV with its 'Account:/From:' preamble are refused before the column mapper is shown, and .txt, QIF and OFX are not supported at all. All six reconstructed Santander samples failed through the real engine. The duplicates come from the de-dupe key date|amount|payee: importing the same statement again with the description mapped to Note (or Payee left unmapped) gives an empty payee, so nothing matches and every row is added again. That row then shows the category name 'Other' as its title and the bank text as its subtitle, exactly as in the user's screenshot. A second path to double counting: confirming a recurring rule makes its own transaction, which never matches the bank row. Imports can be undone only on the dialog's last step: no import history, no source filter, no select-all. Imported data is not useful because everything goes to Other, payees are raw bank strings, and the Type and Balance columns are thrown away. The import dialog's preview hides the payee and amount on the Mac. On iPhone, Money's sub-pages (including Transactions and Import) and Goals' period tabs cannot be reached, because the phone title bar lists the Productivity subnav instead of the page tabs. One cross-app bug was also found: page header art shows the previous page's picture. Proposed revamp: (1) Format sniffing (CSV with header search and delimiter voting, Santander .txt, QIF, OFX with FITID), plus a row-width check. (2) Store the raw bank text on every row. De-dupe on date+amount+normalised raw text, and on FITID or the balance chain when present. (3) Keep an import history (state.money.imports: file, range, count) with Undo, Delete and Replace this period. (4) Normalise merchant names ('HERON FOODS HOYLAND (VIA APPLE PAY), ON 29-10-2026' -> 'Heron Foods'). Learn payee rules when the user sets a category, and ship a UK merchant table. (5) Match imported rows to due recurring occurrences instead of adding new ones; suggest rules from DD/SO types and repeating merchants. (6) Reconcile with the bank's Balance column. (7) Add spend by merchant and by week, a top-merchants card in Month and year (in place of the 'Never printed' designer note), and student-specific views (Student Finance instalment runway: weeks left per £ until the next payment). Goals pages are mostly sound. They have the stale header art, a sparse detail page that does not link to what it counts, a low-contrast status chip on the band, and small zero-count issues.

**Coverage.** Money: Overview, Transactions, Budgets, Recurring, Pots and Month and year, each on the seeded account (117 typed transactions over 95 days, 4 rules, 2 pots, 3 budgets, plus my CSV imports) and the empty account (first-run). Import dialog: the failure step and the Columns/Preview step at Mac size. Import engine: KOS.money.csv.parse/guess/preview/import run in the browser on six reconstructed Santander files in scratchpad/qa/money-samples (midata comma and semicolon, Online Banking Excel CSV, .txt, QIF, OFX), plus a renamed-header workaround and a re-import with a different mapping. DOM measurements: all six Money views and #/goals, /week, /month, /done at mac, ipad and iphone. Screenshots read: Mac for every Money view, Goals list and a goal page; iPhone for Money Overview, Recurring, Goals and the New goal sheet; iPad for Transactions and Goals This week. Not covered: the Atelier Dawn theme (the harness --theme flag set data-theme but the page still drew dark, so Dawn could not be judged; this may be a harness problem, not reported as an app bug); shop themes; iPad landscape; the import dialog on iPhone and iPad; the Goal editor's type forms and the exam goal types; the Wishlist purchase handoff; cloud sync of kos_money; PDF statements (no PDF import path exists in money.js, so there is nothing to test).

### MG-01 · P0 · data — Duplicate transactions after re-importing: de-dupe key includes payee, so a second import mapped differently is all 'new'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Exactly the user's screenshot: the same Heron Foods row twice, once titled with the payee and once titled 'Other' with the bank text as the subtitle. Reproduced: import a statement with Description->Payee, then the same file with Description->Note (or payee unmapped); preview reports 0 duplicates and every row imports again. The key is date|minor|payee.lower(), and an empty payee never equals the first import's payee. The second row reads 'Other' because txRow falls back to the category name when payee is empty and shows the note as the sub-line.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:1025-1031 (key), js/modules/money.js:587,591 (title/subtitle fallback); shots/money-goals/tx-dups-all-mac.png; eval repro: second preview {dups:0}
- **Root cause:** Dedupe depends on user-chosen mapping; raw source text not kept.
- **Proposed fix:** Key duplicates on date+amount+kind+normalised bank text (payee OR note OR raw description, stored as tx.raw), with the bank's own id (OFX FITID / balance-chain) when present; keep the raw description on every imported row; offer 'Replace the previous import of this period' in the preview when a batch overlaps.
- **Flags:** effort M

### MG-02 · P1 · integration — Santander midata CSV is refused: 'Merchant/Description' and 'Debit/Credit' headers are not recognised

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The midata export's header 'Date,Type,Merchant/Description,Debit/Credit,Balance' maps only Date; GUESS has no pattern for 'Merchant/Description' (payee) or 'Debit/Credit' (signed amount), so read() fails with 'It has no header row with a date and an amount.' before the user ever reaches the column mapper, even though the mapper could have fixed it by hand. Reproduced through KOS.money.csv.parse/guess on money-samples/midata-comma.csv: mapping {date:0}, dialogAccepts false.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:970-977 (GUESS), js/modules/money.js:937-940 (hard refusal before the mapper); repro: scratchpad/qa/money-samples/midata-comma.csv, scratchpad/qa/mg-import.js
- **Root cause:** Exact-match header regexes plus a hard gate before the manual mapper.
- **Proposed fix:** Add patterns (merchant/description, debit/credit, 'amount (£)', 'money in/out (£)'); and never hard-fail on unguessed headers: open the Columns step with the unmatched columns and let the user map them.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-03 · P1 · integration — Import only reads the first line as the header: midata with a leading blank line or a semicolon delimiter, and Online Banking CSV with an account preamble, all fail

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** parseCSV picks the delimiter from line 1 only (a leading blank line forces ','), and takes rows[0] as the header. Santander midata variants (';' delimited, trailing ';', blank first line) parse into one column; the Online Banking CSV/Excel export starts with 'Account:'/'From:' preamble lines so the header is 'Account:,Santander…'. All refused with the same generic message.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:947-969; browser repro of 6 sample files (midata-semicolon.csv delimiter ',' header one cell; santander-online-excel.csv header ['Account:',…]); all dialogAccepts=false
- **Root cause:** Header = rows[0], delimiter sniffed from text.split(/\r?\n/)[0].
- **Proposed fix:** Detect the delimiter over the first ~10 non-empty lines; scan the first 20 rows for the first row that matches date+amount header patterns and treat everything above as preamble (show it as 'Skipped: 3 header lines'); drop trailing empty columns.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-04 · P1 · integration — Santander's other exports (.txt, QIF, OFX) are not supported at all; the picker accepts .txt but cannot read it

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Online Banking 'Download transactions' offers text (Date:/Description:/Amount:/Balance: blocks with non-breaking spaces), Excel CSV, Quicken QIF and Microsoft Money OFX. The importer is CSV-only, yet the file input accepts text/plain, so the user's .txt is taken and fails with 'It has no header row with a date and an amount.' OFX would also give a stable FITID for exact de-duplication.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/money.js:889 (accept .csv,text/csv,text/plain), js/core/money.js:947; repro: money-samples/santander-online.txt, santander.qif, santander.ofx all refused
- **Root cause:** Single CSV parser path.
- **Proposed fix:** Add format sniffing: OFX/QFX (parse STMTTRN, use FITID as the dedupe key, LEDGERBAL to reconcile), QIF (D/T/P/M/^ records), the Santander .txt block format; name the format detected in the Columns step. Keep CSV for everything else.
- **Flags:** effort M

### MG-05 · P1 · ux — No way to see, undo or replace an import after the result dialog closes

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Each import has a batch id, but 'Undo this import' exists only on the Done step of the dialog (money.js:915). Once closed there is no import history, no filter by source/batch in Transactions (list() filters kind/cat/tag/search only), and bulk select has no 'select all' over a range, so removing a bad 200-row import means ticking rows one by one.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/money.js:915-917; js/core/money.js:1050-1056 (undoBatch only caller), list() keep() js/core/money.js:~700-712
- **Root cause:** Batch metadata is not stored anywhere but on rows; UI only exposes it in a transient dialog.
- **Proposed fix:** An 'Imports' list (Transactions › Imports or Settings): file name, date range, rows, when imported, with Undo/Delete and 'Replace with a new file'. Store {batch, fileName, from, to, n, at} in state.money.imports. Add a Source filter and Select all in view.
- **Flags:** effort M

### MG-06 · P1 · data — Unquoted thousands separators shift columns: '£1,234.56' imports silently as £1.00

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** A comma-delimited bank file that writes amounts like £1,234.56 without quotes (common in hand-rolled UK exports, and what users get after re-saving midata in a text editor) splits the amount into two cells. The parser never checks a row's cell count against the header, so the Student Finance row imported as +£1.00 and the balance column swallowed '234.56'. No warning in the preview.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:947-969 (no per-row width check), 1005-1013; eval repro: row ['2026-10-27','in',100,'STUDENT FINANCE ENGLAND SLC']; shots/money-goals/tx-dups-all-mac.png shows +£1.00
- **Root cause:** Parser trusts column positions without validating row width.
- **Proposed fix:** Flag rows whose cell count differs from the header ('Row 3 has 6 cells, the header has 5'), and for the known Santander layout re-join a split £n,nnn.nn amount; show the running Balance column so a mismatch is visible.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-07 · P1 · data — A recurring rule's 'Confirm' and an imported bank row for the same payment double-count

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Confirming an occurrence (Google One £1.59, Phone £10) writes its own transaction r:<rule>:<date> with payee = rule name; importing the bank statement then adds 'GOOGLE *Google One, ON 27-10-2026' £1.59 as a separate row. Neither side looks at the other: preview's dedupe compares payee text ('google one' vs the bank string) and confirm() never searches existing transactions. Spend, budgets, Safe to spend and Reports count the payment twice. This is a second path to the duplicates the user saw.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:414-433 (confirm creates tx unconditionally), 1025-1031 (dedupe by exact payee); recurring page shows Google One 'Due' for Sep/Oct even though the user has bank rows for them (shots/money-goals/recurring-mac.png)
- **Root cause:** Rules and imports are two independent writers of the same real-world payment.
- **Proposed fix:** On import, match rows to due occurrences (same amount ±0, date within ±3 days, payee alias set on the rule) and MARK the occurrence done pointing at the imported tx instead of creating a new one; on Confirm, offer 'Use the bank entry from 27 Oct' when one matches.
- **Flags:** effort M

### MG-08 · P1 · ux — Imported rows are unusable as information: raw bank strings, everything in Other, Type and Balance columns discarded

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Every imported row lands in Other/Other income (preview opts.outCat), payee is the raw bank text ('HERON FOODS HOYLAND (VIA APPLE PAY), ON 29-10-2026', 'CARD PAYMENT TO …,3.45 GBP, RATE 1.00/GBP ON …'), so Overview's 'This month by category' is one 'Other' bar, the Selected card shows a 4-line shouting title, spend-by-merchant is impossible and nothing learns from the user's re-categorising. The bank's Type (DEB/DD/FPI/FPO/SO) and Balance columns are dropped, so direct debits are not detected and the import cannot be reconciled. There is no merchant normalisation, no payee->category memory and no recurring detection anywhere in money.js.
- **Evidence:** js/core/money.js:1019-1021 (cat = other), 1041; grep of money.js/modules/money.js finds no merchant/auto-category code; shots/money-goals/tx-dups-all-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Import engine was built as a column copier ('the pages are phase 2' comment at money.js:946).
- **Proposed fix:** Revamp (see summary): store raw text, derive a clean merchant (strip 'CARD PAYMENT TO', '(VIA APPLE PAY)', ', ON dd-mm-yyyy', ',n.nn GBP, RATE …', store numbers, title-case); payee rules {match -> merchant, category} learned when the user sets a category ('Always put Heron Foods in Food?'); a built-in UK merchant table (Tesco, Heron Foods, Greggs, Stagecoach, Google, Spotify, SLC…); Type DD/SO -> suggest a recurring rule; Balance -> reconcile and show 'Matches the bank: £120.10'.
- **Flags:** effort L, needs a design prompt

### MG-09 · P1 · bug — iPhone: Money's Transactions, Month and year, Import/Export CSV and Goals' This week/This month/Done are unreachable

- **Devices:** iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Under 700px the page's section tabs ([data-phone-tabs]) are hidden on the promise that the title bar's switch offers them, but mobile-shell's destinations() only falls back to page tabs when the section has no subnav. Productivity has a subnav, so the 'Switch within Productivity' sheet lists Focus Timer…Money, Goals, and Money's six views and Goals' four views vanish. On the Money overview the only visible links are Budgets →, Recurring →, Pots →; there is no way to open Transactions (where Import CSV lives) or Month and year. Goals: This week/This month/Done tabs measure 0x0.
- **Evidence:** css/components.css:652-654; js/modules/mobile-shell.js:457-463 (subnav wins); js/modules/money.js:128, js/modules/goals.js:303; eval at iphone size: visible controls on #/money list no Transactions/Import; goals tabs 0x0; shots/money-goals/overview-iphone.png, goals-iphone.png
- **Root cause:** Phone title bar prefers the section subnav and the CSS hides page tabs unconditionally.
- **Proposed fix:** In destinations(), when the page carries [data-phone-tabs] list those tabs in the sheet (as a second group under the section list), or keep the page tabs visible as a k-scroller strip under the title bar for Money and Goals.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-10 · P1 · ui — Import preview hides the payee and amount, and the Import button is below the fold

- **Devices:** mac, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** On the Mac (1470x832) the import dialog is 760px wide (it borrows Goals' --gl-dialog-w), the Columns card takes --mn-map-w, and the Preview card gets 264px. Its 4-column row grid (150px chip, 74px date, 1fr payee, 90px amount) leaves the payee 0px wide and pushes the amount to x=1148, outside the card (right edge 1087) — the preview shows only '+ New' and a date, so the user cannot check what will be imported. The 'Cannot read' row's reason is hidden the same way. The dialog body scrolls (907 > 713) and the footer with 'Import 4 entries' sits at y=860, off screen.
- **Evidence:** shots/money-goals/import-preview-mac.png; eval: pvcard w=264, pv grid '150px 74px 0px 90px', amount x=1148, foot y=860; css/views/money.css:135,169,176
- **Root cause:** Dialog width token from Goals + fixed grid columns.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the import dialog its own wide token (~1080px, full sheet on iPad), stack Columns above Preview below ~900px, shrink the state chip to its content, pin the footer (sticky) so Import is always visible.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-11 · P2 · data — Month and year: 'In' shows expected income as if received

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** For the current month the side card's In figure is ins.planned (received + still expected), e.g. 'IN £450.00 · £450.00 still expected' when nothing has come in yet. The headline figure should be what came in, with expected as the sub-line.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/money.js:1695-1697; shots/money-goals/month-mac.png
- **Root cause:** inFig uses ins.planned
- **Proposed fix:** fig('In', cur.income, expected ? '+' + said(expected) + ' still expected' : null).
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-12 · P2 · content — 'Never printed' card shows designer rules to the user

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Month and year's side column contains a card titled 'Never printed' explaining 'Never a bare 0 or +∞%' — an implementation rule from the design notes, meaningless to the user and violating invariant 50a (rules belong in Help & Guide).
- **Evidence:** js/modules/money.js:1698; shots/money-goals/month-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Design annotation shipped as UI copy.
- **Proposed fix:** Remove the card; use the column for something useful (top merchants this month, biggest change, the month's subscriptions).
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-13 · P2 · ui — Money's section tabs jump between pages (Add entry button appears on some pages only)

- **Devices:** mac, ipad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** On Overview/Transactions/Recurring the tab strip spans x≈617–1180 with '+ Add entry' after it; on Budgets/Pots/Month and year there is no Add entry and the strip sits at x≈742–1305 and vertically centred differently (y 183 vs 204). Switching tabs makes the control you just clicked move under the pointer. The strip and Hide amounts pill also sit over the painted header band, Hide amounts touching its right edge on Budgets/Pots.
- **Evidence:** shots/money-goals/overview-mac.png vs budgets-mac.png, pots-mac.png, month-mac.png
- **Root cause:** header() actions differ per view in js/modules/money.js
- **Proposed fix:** Keep Add entry (or a placeholder of equal width) on every Money page, or put the section tabs on their own row left-aligned under the title as other Productivity pages do.

### MG-14 · P2 · ui — Pots: empty right column beside the pot cards, and Move money's tabs/select clipped

- **Devices:** mac
- **Detail / repro:** With two pots the grid shows Emergency and the 'Saving for something?' tile, leaving a ~410px empty column on the right (x 1036–1446). The Move money card clips 'Between pots' at its right edge and the To select shows 'New laptop fc'.
- **Evidence:** shots/money-goals/pots-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Fixed-column pot grid; segmented control without wrap.

### MG-15 · P2 · ui — iPhone Recurring: the third occurrence tile is cut off at the card edge

- **Devices:** iphone
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px each due rule shows three occurrence tiles in a row; the third (Sat 28 Nov · Upcoming) runs to x=410, past the card and viewport, reading "Upcomi" / "Sat 28 No". It is not a declared scroller (invariant 50). The New rule form is also always expanded at the bottom of the phone page and the + FAB covers its Next-dates line.

### MG-16 · P2 · ui — Goal page: status chip unreadable on the painted band, and a mostly empty page

- **Devices:** mac, ipad
- **Detail / repro:** On #/goals/<id> the 'Too early to call' chip sits on the header painting at very low contrast (grey text on mid-tone art). The page body is one stats card and two small side cards; for a Collection titles goal it never lists which titles count or what to read next, and it does not link to them, leaving ~400px of empty page at 1470x832. Fractional 'The line says 0.3 titles' for a count of titles reads oddly.
- **Evidence:** shots/money-goals/goal-detail-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Detail page draws only aggregate facts.
- **Proposed fix:** Give band chips a solid scrim background (as the Hide amounts pill has); for count goals list the counted/remaining items with links into the vault (connected OS); round the line for integer units ('on pace: 0 of 12 by today').

### MG-17 · P2 · bug — Painted page header shows the PREVIOUS page's picture (Goals shows Money's jar, Money shows Goals' mountain)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.ui.pageHeader picks its art from KOS.store.state.ui.view, but KOS.show only sets ui.view AFTER the view function has rendered. So the first render of any page that relies on PAGE_ART draws the art of the page you came from; a redraw on the same page then corrects it. Reproduced: money -> goals gives app.hero.page.money.webp on Goals; goals -> money/budgets gives app.hero.page.goals.webp on Money. Affects every page in PAGE_ART (Review, Assignments, Exams, Pacing, Calendar, Reminders, Routines, Goals, Money, Wishlist, Governor, Reports, Archive...).
- **Evidence:** js/core/ui.js:589-592 (reads ui.view), js/core/ui.js:1540-1543 (view set after render); eval sequence output: '#/goals => app.hero.page.money.webp', '#/money/budgets => app.hero.page.goals.webp'; shots/money-goals/goals-mac.png (jar on Goals)
- **Root cause:** Ordering in KOS.show: state.ui.view assigned after render.
- **Proposed fix:** Set KOS.store.state.ui.view (or a transient KOS.ui.rendering = viewId) before calling KOS.views[viewId], or have pageHeader take the view id from the router's current call.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-18 · P2 · ui — CSS class collision: first-run steps and the import stepper share .k-mn-steps/.k-mn-step, breaking both

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** css/views/money.css defines .k-mn-steps/.k-mn-step/.k-mn-step-n twice: once for the import dialog's horizontal stepper (158-163) and again for the first-run checklist (319-321). The later column layout makes the import stepper a vertical centred list that eats ~170px of the dialog; the earlier align-items:center leaks into the first-run list, so its numbered circles sit at different x (906/934/910) because each step is centred by its own width.
- **Evidence:** css/views/money.css:158-163 vs 319-321; shots/money-goals/import-midata-fail-mac.png (vertical stepper), money-empty-mac.png (misaligned 1/2/3)
- **Root cause:** Duplicate selectors in one view stylesheet.
- **Proposed fix:** Rename one set (e.g. .k-mn-istep for the import stepper) and set align-items: stretch on the first-run list.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-19 · P2 · data — Transactions 'This view' totals cover only the rows loaded so far

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With range 'Everything' the side card reads '60 entries so far · In +£1,337.80 · Out −£509.51' — totals of the first lazy batch, not of the period. A user checking a month's spend gets a partial figure that changes as they scroll.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/money.js drawSide (sums `shown`); shots/money-goals/tx-dawn-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Totals derived from the rendered batch.
- **Proposed fix:** Compute the range totals with KOS.money.rangeSummary(from,to) (index read, no rendering) and keep the lazy list for rows only.

### MG-20 · P2 · ui — iPad: Money header wraps Add entry to its own row and clips Hide amounts; filter selects are 30px on touch

- **Devices:** ipad

On Transactions the Category and Dates selects are 30px tall and rows 41px, under the 44px touch minimum (invariant 69); Pots' ⋯ menu button is 44x32 and Budgets' month-start select 70x30.

### MG-21 · P3 · ux — Phone section sheet stays open across navigation and shows the wrong page ticked

- **Devices:** iphone
- **Detail / repro:** Opening 'Switch within Productivity' on Goals and then navigating (back gesture / hash change) leaves the sheet over the new page with 'Goals ✓' while Money is shown; it even survived a viewport change to iPad width.

- **Proposed fix:** Close activeSectionOverlay on every KOS.show / hashchange in mobile-shell.js.
- **Flags:** effort S

### MG-22 · P3 · ui — Goals: zero counts printed and empty-state card leaves a dead half

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** This week with no goals shows the filter pill 'All 0' (invariant 77) and an empty-state card whose picture and one line sit in the left 60% with no action, next to the 'Aim at something new' card. 'Your run' paints '0 behind this week' in the alarm colour even when nothing is behind.
- **Evidence:** shots/money-goals/goals-week-ipad.png, goals-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Pill count and stat tone not zero-aware.
- **Proposed fix:** Hide the count at 0 (or the whole domain pill row when empty); put New goal / templates in the empty card; use muted tone for a zero 'behind'.
- **Flags:** effort S

## EX — Exams hub, Files, ink/Pencil surfaces (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### EX-01 · P1 · bug — Marks by question: a question added with '＋ Question' can't be given its Max or its topic

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On #/exams/attempt/<uid>/marks the ＋ Question button adds a row whose only controls are Got and Redo: Max prints '—' and Topic is an empty read-only span. Only questions filled from the past-paper index carry max/refs. For a school mock, class paper or any paper not in the index (the common case) there is no way to record the out-of, so 'full marks', 'untagged · n', the total check and the post-mortem can't work, and a Redo on such a question has no leaf to be scheduled on (invariant 124 says a redo item is due on its question's leaf). Reproduced: clicking ＋ Question gave a row with inputs ['Got, question Q5', 'Redo question Q5'] and text 'Q5 | / | — | Redo'. The Topic column is therefore empty for every hand-logged attempt (dead column).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-marks-addq-ipad.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-marks-ipad.png; js/modules/examprac.js:410 addRow pushes {max:null, refs:[]}; :436-438 the row renders node title (read-only) and q.max as text; js/modules/tracker.js:192 questions only come from an index fill
- **Root cause:** Marks page was built around index-filled questions; addRow never got editors for max/refs
- **Proposed fix:** Make Max a small number input on rows without an index max, and the Topic cell a KOS.topicPicker button (scoped to the assessment's leaves) on every row; keep index-filled values as defaults.
- **Flags:** effort M

### EX-02 · P1 · ui — Exam Room on iPhone: the 'Before you go in' checklist is drawn on top of the rapid-recall list

- **Devices:** iPhone (also any short landscape window)
- **Detail / repro:** At 402px the room's body (ring, plan, five recall facts, blacklist) is taller than the screen, but .k-exr-body is a shrinkable flex item with min-block-size 0 inside the fixed, scrolling .k-exr column. It is squeezed to the leftover height and its content overflows visibly, so the checklist chips ('Black pens', 'Candidate number', 'Phone off and away') sit over facts 3–5 and both are unreadable; the blacklist is pushed off below. This is the screen meant to be used minutes before an exam on the phone.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-room-iphone.png; css/views/exams.css:505 (.k-exr flex column, overflow-y auto) and :513 (.k-exr-body flex: 1 1 auto; min-block-size: 0); phone rules :933-937 don't change it
- **Root cause:** flex item allowed to shrink below its content in a scroll container
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-exr-body flex-shrink: 0 (or min-block-size: auto) so the room scrolls instead of overlapping; at phone width also consider putting the checklist right under the ring.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-03 · P1 · ui — On iPhone the floating ink palette is 500px wide on a 402px screen: Undo and Redo are off-screen

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Under 700px the palette always floats in one row (the docked bar isn't offered). At 402px it measures 500px (x 20→520): grip 32, six tools at 44, the colour button still showing its text 'Ink · Mediu…' at 76, Undo and Redo at 44, plus gaps. The colour menu is cut and Undo/Redo are unreachable; dragging can't help because place() clamps x to max(20, W−w−20) = 20. Every pad, Handwritten tab, sketchbook and drill on the phone is affected.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-ink-iphone.png; eval at iphone: ink.palette rect [20,716,500,62], scrollWidth 500, buttons '32x32 44x48 ×6 76x50 44x48 44x48'; js/core/inkui.js:1146-1171 (six tools + colour + history), css/views/ink.css:201-203 ('icons' state hides tool labels only)
- **Root cause:** Palette content width not budgeted for the phone tier
- **Proposed fix:** In the 'icons' state also hide the colour button's text (dot + caret only), and when root.scrollWidth > innerWidth − 40 move Shape and Ruler into the grip's More menu (or wrap to two rows). Clamp place() so a palette wider than the screen is centred and scrollable rather than clipped.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-04 · P2 · bug — A transparent PNG pasted or dropped onto an ink page gets a black background

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** readImage() in inkui.js draws every picture (paste, drop, Image… from the palette) onto a canvas and keeps it as toDataURL('image/jpeg'). JPEG has no alpha, so every transparent pixel becomes black: a diagram, a screenshot with transparency or a cut-out pasted onto a lined page shows a black box behind it on the paper.
- **Evidence:** js/core/inkui.js:1400-1413 (c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.86) with no fill first); compare js/core/imagecrop.js:110-124 which keeps PNG/WebP or fills a background before JPEG
- **Root cause:** Unconditional JPEG encoding of user pictures
- **Proposed fix:** Keep image/png when the source is PNG/WebP/GIF (or when the canvas has any alpha < 255), or fill the canvas white before drawImage when JPEG is chosen.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-05 · P2 · ui — Readiness band line on iPhone squeezes the sentence into a ~80px column, one word per line

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On #/exams/readiness at 402px the band chip and 'limiting: RAG' take the left of the bandline and the explanation ('SAMPLE — Mock exam (edit me) · Sat 7 Nov · 5 days. RAG is holding it at Not enough evidence.') is crushed into a narrow right column running nine lines, beside empty space under the chip; 'Plan the gap' drops to its own row.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/empty-readiness-iphone.png (top card); js/modules/examviews.js:45-48 builds the line with .k-ex-grow; css/views/exams.css:20 .k-ex-grow { flex: 1 1 0; min-inline-size: 0 } inside .k-ex-bandline (exams.css:279, flex-wrap: wrap)
- **Root cause:** flex-basis 0 on a wrapping flex item never wraps, it shrinks
- **Proposed fix:** In .k-ex-bandline give the text a real basis (flex: 1 1 16rem) so it wraps to its own full-width line when narrow; or stack the bandline in a column under 560.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-06 · P2 · ui — Planner rows on iPhone truncate every topic name to a few letters and wrap the group total

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** #/exams/plan at 402px: each priority row is a three-column grid (text, session squares, time). With a 'learn first' chip inside the same ellipsised line the topic shows as 'Methods o…', 'Laws of indic…', 'Quadratic function…', so the user can't tell what to study. The group header's total '5 h 50' wraps onto two lines beside the Mock chip.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/empty-plan-iphone.png; js/modules/examviews.js:239-243 (chip and title inside one .k-ex-ell); css/views/exams.css:384 .k-ex-prow grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr) auto var(--sp-64)
- **Root cause:** Fixed three-column row with single-line ellipsis at phone width
- **Proposed fix:** Under 560 put the session squares under the text (grid-template-areas) and let the title wrap to two lines (line-clamp 2) with the chip on the why line; give the header total white-space: nowrap.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-07 · P2 · a11y — Files list is one big aria-live region: every keystroke in search re-announces up to 300 cards

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Files list container carries aria-live='polite' and paint() empties and refills it on every debounced search input, filter change and index event, so VoiceOver reads out the whole result list (names, kinds, sizes, links) after each letter typed.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/files.js:185 (aria-live on files.list), :238-249 paint() rebuilds it; search handler :202
- **Proposed fix:** Drop aria-live from the list; announce one short line through KOS.a11y.announce ('12 files match') after a search/filter change (invariant 66).
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-08 · P2 · a11y — Marks by question on touch: tag chips are 24px tall and the Got field 28px

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The ten 'Why marks went' tags are the main input of this page and sit in a dense 24px-tall wrap; the Got number field is 64x28. On the iPad (the device used for marking with a paper beside it) these are below the 44px touch minimum (invariant 69) and easy to mis-hit (adjacent chips 4px apart).
- **Evidence:** measure on ipad #/exams/attempt/j3hkb7ji/marks: button.k-ex-tag 88x24, 60x24 …; exams.got 64x28; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-marks-ipad.png
- **Proposed fix:** Under (pointer: coarse) raise .k-ex-tag and .k-ex-gotin to 44px min-block-size (the row can grow), or switch to a two-column tag grid on touch.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-09 · P2 · a11y — Exam Room text over the classroom painting is low-contrast (blacklist on Mac, plan line on iPad)

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** On the Mac the right column ('Your blacklist · three most frequent tags' and each step's explanation) lies over the bright windows of the painting: the grey sub-lines and the eyebrow are barely visible, and the numbered circles fade into the window frames. 'The plan: About 1.5 min a mark…' under the ring and 'Leave the Exam Room' are similarly faint on iPad and Mac.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-room-mac.png (right column), audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-room-ipad.png (plan line under 'Sports hall · seat 14')
- **Proposed fix:** Put the side columns on the same frosted card the recall items use (with a darker scrim), or darken the painting layer behind text (a gradient scrim on the art layer 'exam:room'); raise muted text to --text-2 in the room.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-10 · P2 · ui — Exam Season on iPhone: 'Highest-risk topics' shows each topic as 'Finite …', 'Abstr…' (51px)

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The risk row is a fixed four-column grid (name, a fixed-width bar, %, Cram). At 402px the bar keeps its desktop width and the topic name gets 51px, so the card that tells you what to cram doesn't say which topics; the paper name under it wraps to two lines.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-hub-iphone.png (Highest-risk topics card); measure: div.k-ex-strong 51x20 at x=38; css/views/exams.css:408 grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr) var(--ex-bar-w) var(--sp-40) auto

### EX-11 · P2 · ui — Planner's 'Next week' preview is one run-on paragraph of titles and times

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With several assessments the preview prints every group as 'Title 1 h 40 · Title 1 h · …' plus the 'leaves the plan' sentences in one paragraph; on the Mac it runs 12+ lines where names and durations blur together ('Revision block 32 1 h 40 · Revision block 4 1 h 40 …'). The same page shows this week as a legend of name/time pairs.

### EX-12 · P2 · a11y — Floating palette's grip (the only way to Text, Image and Paper while floating) is 32x32

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The ⠿ grip both drags the palette and, on a tap, opens 'More tools' (Text, Image…, Paper…, Dock). It is 32x32 on the iPad and iPhone, below the 44px touch minimum, and nothing hints that it opens a menu; on the phone (always floating) Text and Paper are reachable only through it.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-ink-float-ipad.png; eval at ipad: grip 'Move the palette, or more tools' 32x32; css/views/ink.css:204 (min-inline-size: var(--hit))

### EX-13 · P3 · content — Pen and ink says a pen without pressure follows 'speed and tilt'; tilt is never read

- **Devices:** iPad
- **Detail / repro:** The 'Your pen' card says 'It reports no pressure, so the width follows your speed and tilt.' Neither ink.js nor inkui.js reads tiltX/tiltY/altitudeAngle; width without pressure comes from speed only (invariant 132).
- **Evidence:** js/modules/inkset.js:56 text; grep 'tilt' js/core/ink.js js/core/inkui.js returns nothing
- **Proposed fix:** Say 'so the width follows your speed.'
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-14 · P3 · ui — Readiness 'Coverage' rows on iPhone ellipsise topic names while the Pacing note keeps full width

- **Devices:** iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Rows like 'Quadratic functions & …', 'Graphs of functio…', 'Parametric …' are cut while 'Pacing: week of 18 Jan 2027' is shown whole. The topic name is the primary information.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/empty-readiness-iphone.png (Coverage list); measure: span.k-ex-ell widths 84-146px at 402px
- **Proposed fix:** Let the note go under the title at phone width, or shorten it to the date ('18 Jan') with the word Pacing in the column header.

### EX-15 · P3 · a11y — Exam season and Quiet the rest switches: 20px tall hit area on touch, and no role=switch

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The hub header's 'Exam season' switch and the Season page's 'Quiet the rest' are an input.k-switch (34x20) inside a label only as tall as its text (20px), so the touch target on iPad/iPhone is 20px tall (invariant 69 asks 44). Unlike storage.auto (role=switch) they announce as checkboxes.
- **Evidence:** measure on ipad: exams.season label height 20, input 34x20; js/modules/exams.js:263-270, js/modules/examviews.js:287-295 vs js/modules/storage.js:113
- **Proposed fix:** Give .k-ex-seasonsw/.k-ex-quietsw min-block-size 44px under (pointer: coarse) and add role='switch' to both inputs.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-16 · P3 · ux — Files list silently stops at 300 files

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** paint() renders rows.slice(0, 300) with no 'and N more' line or pagination, so past 300 files (easy with a past-paper library: papers are listed here too) the rest just don't appear and nothing says so.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/files.js:249
- **Proposed fix:** Lazy-load in batches of 60 like the vaults (invariant 8) or print 'Showing 300 of N — search or filter to narrow'.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-17 · P3 · ui — Pen and ink page leaves a dead block under 'On this device' on iPad landscape and Mac

- **Devices:** iPad landscape, Mac
- **Detail / repro:** The four/five cards sit in a two-column grid with row-aligned heights: 'On this device' is ~180px tall beside 'Your pen' (~300px), leaving ~120px empty, and Ink sync is a short card beside the tall Kurenai card. The standing rule asks for no dead space; Reports uses masonry for uneven cards.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/empty-pen-ipadl.png (1180x820)
- **Proposed fix:** Use the masonry/column flow (columns: 2 or the Reports 'pack' helper) for .k-pen-grid so short cards stack.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-18 · P3 · bug — Exam Room's keep-awake is lost after the iPad is switched away and back

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The Exam Room requests a screen wake lock once on render. The browser releases a wake lock whenever the page is hidden (app switch, Control Centre, a notification opened); nothing re-requests it on visibilitychange, so after one switch the screen dims and locks during the countdown. KOS.desk (Focus) handles this by re-syncing on visibilitychange.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/examroom.js:154 (single navigator.wakeLock.request); compare js/core/desk.js:25-36
- **Proposed fix:** Reuse KOS.desk's wake-lock helper, or listen for visibilitychange while the room is connected and re-request.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-19 · P3 · ui — Marks page: 'Use 19 · Keep 40' are unstyled text links that wrap apart, and a 'Keys · no mouse needed' card shows on touch devices

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** When the questions don't add up to the total, the note ends with two k-link buttons 'Use 19' · 'Keep 40' that look like body text; on iPad 'Keep 40' wraps to its own line with a large gap, so the choice reads as a broken sentence. Below, a keyboard-shortcuts card ('no mouse needed': Tab, 1–0, R, arrows, Enter) is shown on an iPad with no keyboard.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-marks-ipad.png (TOTAL card and Keys card); js/modules/examprac.js:457-458, :400
- **Proposed fix:** Render Use/Keep as two small buttons on their own row; show the Keys card only when (hover: hover) or a keyboard has been used (KOS.desk's hold-⌘ detection).
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-20 · P3 · content — Exam Room's countdown says 'until 09:00' when the exam is weeks away

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Opened 40 days before the exam (the route works at any time) the ring reads '40 days' over 'until 09:00', which reads as today at nine.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-room-mac.png; js/modules/examroom.js:107 (until = a.time ? 'until ' + a.time …)
- **Proposed fix:** When the exam is not today, say 'until Sat 12 Dec · 09:00'.

### EX-21 · P3 · a11y — Small touch targets in the assessment page: Goals '＋' is 12x44, 'Edit' 22x44, checklist boxes 18x18

- **Devices:** iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** On an assessment page the Goals card's add control is a k-link '＋' only 12px wide, 'On the day › Edit' is 22px wide, and the Before-you-go-in / checklist checkboxes are 18x18 (the Exam Room ticks too). On touch these are below invariant 69.

### EX-22 · P3 · ui — Planner allocation legend: a long name wraps under its colour dot, leaving the dot alone on a line

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** In the allocation legend, entries whose title is long ('SAMPLE — Mock exam (edit me)', 'Graph traversal topic test with …') put the dot on one line and the title on the next, so their rows sit 14px lower than neighbours and the dot looks orphaned.

### EX-23 · P3 · ux — Practice attempts: an unlabeled checkbox on every row

- **Devices:** all
