### EX-24 · P3 · ux — Analytics opens on the nearest assessment even when it has no evidence, while Calibration below counts every paper

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** #/exams/practice/analytics selects the first chip (a topic test with no papers): 'from 0 marked papers', 'Needs one marked paper…', 'Pace needs one timed paper', 'None timed yet' — while CS Paper 2 has six logged papers, and the Calibration card on the same page says '6 papers'. The page looks empty despite data, and mixes per-assessment and global scopes without saying so.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-analytics-ipad.png

### EX-25 · P3 · content — Predicted paper 1 for CS Paper 2 totals 99 marks and 2 h 29 min against a 100-mark, 2 h 30 board paper

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The paper's side card says Marks 99, Time 2 h 29 min, while its own note says 'Shaped like AQA CS Paper 2: 100 marks'. The blueprint fill stops one mark short and the time is derived from the marks, so a timed sitting is a minute short of the real exam.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-paper-ipadl.png (This paper card)
- **Proposed fix:** Let KOS.examPaper.build top up to the blueprint total (a 1-mark item or allow one item over) and take the time from the preset's durationMins.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-26 · P3 · content — Handwritten tab tells you pages 'stay on this device', but ink syncs to your account when signed in

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The empty band says 'Pages belong to 4.10.1 and stay on this device.' unconditionally. Since the ink sync release (invariant 136, kos_ink) pages replicate stroke by stroke to the user's own account when cloud sync is signed in; the Pen and ink page says so. The band's line misleads (and could put someone off writing on the iPad to read on the Mac).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-ink-ipadl.png; js/modules/inktab.js:178
- **Proposed fix:** Branch on KOS.cloud.signedIn(): 'and sync to your other devices' when signed in, 'stay on this device (and in the full backup)' when not.
- **Flags:** effort S

### EX-27 · P3 · ui — Topic tab strip on iPhone doesn't reveal the selected tab (Handwritten is off-screen)

- **Devices:** iPhone

- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/exams-files-ink/seed-ink-iphone.png (tab strip)
- **Proposed fix:** Call the scroller's reveal on the active tab after openTab (KOS.ui.scroller({reveal})).
- **Flags:** effort S

## CL — Collection vaults, Shrine, Wishlist, Sync (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### CL-01 · P1 · ui — Collection section strip hides the Games vault on iPad portrait (inner pill group scrolls invisibly)

- **Devices:** iPad portrait 820x1180 (any 701-860 width)
- **Detail / repro:** On every Collection page at 820px the vault pill group (Overview, Anime, Books, Visual Novels, Games) is squeezed to 408px of its 484px and scrolls inside itself with its scrollbar hidden. Games is cut off at the right edge with no arrow or fade; the outer section scroller sees no overflow (sw 716 = cw 716) so its arrows never appear. A user on the iPad has no visible way to reach Games from the strip.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/empty-mediasync-ipad.png (strip shows Overview..Visual Novels, no Games); eval: .k-seg sw=484 cw=408 x=88, Games item x=483 w=85; shell.subnav-scroller sw=716 cw=716
- **Root cause:** css/components.css:52 `.k-seg { max-inline-size:100%; overflow-x:auto; scrollbar-width:none }` makes the group's min-content 0, so as a flex child of `.k-subnav` (components.css:80) it shrinks and scrolls itself instead of overflowing the scroller mobile-shell.js:103-125 wraps around the subnav.
- **Proposed fix:** Give the section's pill group `flex: none` inside `.k-subnav` (css/components.css:80-82) so the overflow moves to the outer `k-subnav-scroller`, which already has arrows; or drop overflow-x:auto from `.k-seg` when it is a child of `.k-subnav`.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-02 · P2 · content — Collection Overview prints zero-only facts and truncates them on the module cards

- **Devices:** all; truncation on Mac 1470
- **Detail / repro:** Empty account: the four module cards read '0 entries · 0 watching · 0 completed', '0 series · 0 reading · 0 completed · 0 vols …', '0 tracked · 0 playing · 0 routes cleared · 0 …', '0 tracked · 0 playing · 0 hours logged · 0 c…'. Zero supporting facts should be suppressed (invariant 77), and three of the four lines end in an ellipsis at Mac width, so even with data the line is cut.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/empty-matrix-mac.png; measurement: ELLIP span.k-mrow-sub 247x18 x3 at 1470
- **Proposed fix:** Build the sub-line from non-zero facts only (show one primary fact, e.g. 'Nothing tracked yet', when all are zero) and cap it at two facts so it fits 247px, or allow it to wrap to two lines.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-03 · P2 · ui — 'Where each medium stands' card renders a legend over an empty body on an empty account

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With no titles the Overview's 'Where each medium stands' card shows its title and the Completed/In progress/Planned/Dropped legend but no bars and no empty-state line, so it reads as a broken chart (invariant 76 asks for an honest low-data state).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/empty-matrix-mac.png
- **Proposed fix:** When every module total is 0, hide the legend and print one line ('Statuses appear once a vault has titles') or hide the card and let Airing soon take the row.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-04 · P2 · bug — Mangaka is marked as the Anime page (strip lights Anime; phone header reads 'Anime')

- **Devices:** all; most visible on iPhone where the page title says Anime
- **Detail / repro:** Mangaka is built from the Books vault ('Every author on your shelves', its Actions go to the Books vault) but the section strip highlights Anime as current, and the phone shell's section title above the page reads 'Anime ▾'. Current-location marking is wrong and confusing (you came from Books).
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-mangaka-mac.png (Anime pill current); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-mangaka-iphone.png (title 'Anime')
- **Root cause:** js/core/ui.js:1467 `if (viewId === "seasonal" || viewId === "mangaka") activeView = "anime";`
- **Proposed fix:** In js/core/ui.js:1467 map mangaka to 'books' (keep seasonal → anime).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-05 · P2 · perf — Mangaka takes ~3 s to render on the dense account (reads and groups every book on each open)

- **Devices:** Mac measured; slower on iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** Navigating Home → #/mangaka with 1,107 books took ~2.9 s until the DOM settled (Books vault 1.5 s, Anime 1.1 s, Overview 0.8 s). The page queries the whole Books module, groups by author and computes works/volumes/chapters per author in the sort comparator (worksOf/mkVols/mkChapters re-filter per comparison), every paint and on every search keystroke.
- **Evidence:** eval timing: {'#/mangaka':2925ms,'#/books':1554,'#/anime':1074,'#/matrix':792,'#/shrine':882}; js/modules/books.js:1651 (full query), 1600-1603 (sort comparator recomputing worksOf per comparison)
- **Root cause:** Per-comparison recomputation in the sort (O(n log n × works)) plus an unbounded module read on every open.
- **Proposed fix:** Precompute per-author aggregates once per filter change (a map name→{works, vols, ch}) and sort on the cached values; cache the grouped rows between visits keyed on the vault's change counter.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CL-06 · P2 · a11y — Small touch targets across Collection controls on iPad/iPhone (sort selects 30px, Mangaka letters 32px, Wishlist checkboxes 18px, Sync radios 13px)

- **Devices:** iPad (both orientations), iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** With a coarse pointer: vault sort selects (Anime/Books/VN/Games/Shrine) are 173x30, Seasonal season select 104x30 and year field 96x31, Wishlist sort 132x30, Wishlist 'Simulate buying' checkboxes 18x18, Mangaka jump letters 32x32, Sync's autonomous-sync switch 34x20 and the XML import-mode radios 13x13. Invariant 69 asks for 44px on coarse pointers.
- **Evidence:** DOM measurement at ipad/iphone (col-seed-ipad.json): SMALL ui.status-select vault.sort 173x30; mangaka.jump 32x32; plan.check 18x18; sync.auto-toggle 34x20; input radio 13x13
- **Proposed fix:** Apply the coarse-pointer min block size to `.k-select`/status-select and the year input in components.css; wrap the Wishlist checkboxes and Sync radios in 44px labels; give `.k-mk-key` 44px under (pointer:coarse).
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-07 · P2 · ui — Vault toolbar wraps into three ragged rows on iPad portrait ('Find new' alone on a row, count alone above)

- **Devices:** iPad portrait 820
- **Detail / repro:** At 820px the Books/Anime/VN/Games controls block is 131px tall: the count ('738 series') sits on its own line, search/sort/filters/view/actions on a second, and the primary 'Find new' button alone on a third line at the left, under a separate 'Status & lists' row. The grid starts ~713px down the page. Ragged wrap with dead width on rows 1 and 3.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-anime-ipad.png; eval: vault.count @88,588; search @88,619 253x44 … Actions @721,619; 'Find new' @88,675 111x44; controls block 716x131
- **Root cause:** js/modules/medview.js toolbar() lays the count, the control row and the primary button out as separate flex items that wrap independently below the 1080 tier.
- **Proposed fix:** In the medview toolbar, put the count inside the search row (as on Mac) and let the primary button take the Actions slot (or fold Find new into Actions) under 860px, so the toolbar is one row plus the Status & lists button; or make 'Status & lists' share that row.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-08 · P2 · bug — Back (or any route change) leaves a vault editor open over the next page; Save then edits a title from another vault

- **Devices:** Mac browser (Back key/gesture), iPad Safari tab (edge swipe)
- **Detail / repro:** Open an Anime title's editor, then press Back: the page behind changes to the previous route (Games) but the Anime editor drawer stays open on top of it. Navigating by hash with the editor open does the same — screenshots of #/books and #/vn still show 'Collection entry 686' (an anime) with Log episode. The modal no longer belongs to the page under it and its Save writes the anime row while the user is looking at Games/VN.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-books-mac.png and audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-vn-mac.png (anime editor over Books/VN); eval: open=1 hash=#/anime → history.back() → afterBack=1 hash=#/game h1=Games
- **Root cause:** js/core/router.js:283-297 onPop re-renders the view with KOS.show but nothing closes overlays opened by KOS.ui.openDialog (js/core/ui.js:155), which live outside #main.
- **Proposed fix:** On a routed navigation (router.js onPop/renderRoute, and KOS.show without _redraw) close every open KOS.ui.openDialog overlay first (cancel semantics, or ask if dirty), or have openDialog push a history entry so Back closes the dialog instead of the page.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CL-09 · P2 · data — Books status rail counts the whole module while the grid shows one lens ('All 1107' beside '738 series')

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On Books (Digital lens, the default) the rail reads All 1107 / In progress 221 / … while the toolbar count says 738 series and the grid holds 738. Switching to Physical Vault keeps the same rail numbers. Clicking a status therefore shows fewer titles than its count promises.
- **Evidence:** eval on #/books: rail 'All 1107','In progress 221'…; vault.count '738 series'; lens 'Digital:true'
- **Root cause:** js/modules/medview.js:1704-1712 reload() counts `KOS.mediadb.query({module})` — every row of the module — with no knowledge of the Books lens set in js/modules/books.js:1131.
- **Proposed fix:** Pass the active lens predicate to filterRail (e.g. filterRail(module, onChange, {filter: lensFn})) and count only rows that pass it; recount when the lens changes.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-10 · P2 · bug — 'Hide amounts' does not hide money on the Wishlist, Mangaka or the Books shelf; currency is formatted three different ways

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** With Money's Hide amounts on (state.ui.moneyHidden = true) the Money page shows no figures, but the Wishlist still prints 'GBP 10.00', 'GBP 60.00 left of GBP 60.00', 'GBP 105.00', and Mangaka prints '£755 spent' on every author card. Invariant 5a says Hide amounts masks every amount on every surface. The same values are also formatted inconsistently: Money/Wishlist 'GBP 10.00' (ISO code + space, mono), Books/Mangaka a hard-coded '£755' / '£12.50' that ignores the user's currency setting.
- **Evidence:** eval with moneyHidden=true: money page matches [] ; wishlist ['GBP 10.00','GBP 60.00',…]; mangaka ['£755 spent',…]; audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-wishlist-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-mangaka-mac.png
- **Root cause:** js/modules/wishlist.js:544-548 and js/modules/books.js:363,1531 format money with string concatenation instead of the shared KOS.ui.amount primitive that owns masking and currency.
- **Proposed fix:** Route every Collection amount through KOS.ui.amount (minor units, currency from KOS.money): wishlist.js money() (544-548), books.js:363 (Shelf value), books.js:1531 (author 'spent'), books.js:454 placeholder, and the Overview/analytics 'spent' figures.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-11 · P3 · qol — No link out to the title's AniList/VNDB page, and nowhere to keep 'where I watch/read it' (QoL)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The editor shows 'Linked to AniList' and knows anilistId/malId/vndbId, but only Games has an outbound link ('View on Steam ↗', games.js:333). Anime/Books/VN have no 'Open on AniList/VNDB' and no personal 'Where I'm watching/reading' URL. Proposal for the watch link: a manual-layer field `links: [{label, url}]` (or a single `watchUrl`) added in mediadb.normalise (js/core/mediadb.js:542, sanitised to http(s) only, no index so no DB version bump — like physical.seriesTotal), preserved in merge() beside notes/tags (mediadb.js ~1062: `inc.links = old.links.length ? old.links : inc.links`), unioned in foldLocal (mediadb.js:934) and covered by materialJSON automatically; never pushed (invariant 13 payloads stay status/progress/score) and excluded from search (71). UI: an input on 'Your layer' with a 'Use clipboard' helper; a '▶ Watch' / '📖 Read' button beside '+1 ep' on the card hover row, the Spotlight hero and the editor header, opening in a new tab (rel=noopener). The provider page link (AniList/VNDB) can be derived from the ids and shown on the Record tab.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-anime-editor-layer-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-anime-editor-record-mac.png; grep: only js/modules/games.js:57,333 build an outbound title URL
- **Proposed fix:** Add the field to normalise/merge/foldLocal as above, a URL input in the shared editor's personal layer (KOS.mediaEditors), and a Watch/Read action in medview's card and hero.
- **Flags:** effort M

### CL-12 · P3 · a11y — Favourite heart on vault cards is a generic 'Toggle favourite' with no visible label and an easy mis-tap

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The heart button on every card is labelled 'Toggle favourite' (aria-pressed is set, good) but sits at the card's top-right where a tap meant to open the card toggles the favourite instantly with no toast/undo; a Shrine membership changes silently. Measured 34x34 (below 44 on touch).
- **Evidence:** eval: card button aria-label 'Toggle favourite', aria-pressed 'false', 34x34; reproduced an accidental favourite by clicking the card's first button
- **Proposed fix:** Label as 'Favourite <title>' / 'Remove <title> from favourites', show a toast with Undo ('Added to the Shrine'), and give it 44px on coarse pointers.
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-13 · P3 · content — Spotlight says 'the last one is next' when every route/episode is already done

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** A VN in progress at 2/2 routes reads '2 routes in — the last one is next.' The branch fires when left === 0, i.e. nothing is left; 'the last one is next' describes left === 1. Same for an anime/book at its final episode/chapter count.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/collection/seed-vn-mac.png (hero: '2 routes in — the last one is next.', chip 2 / 2 routes)
- **Root cause:** js/modules/medview.js:1318
- **Proposed fix:** left === 0 → 'All N in — ready to mark it complete?' (offer a Complete action); left === 1 → 'the last one is next.'
- **Flags:** effort S

### CL-14 · P3 · a11y — Seasonal hero's art credit is unreadable on the painted scenery (Mac/iPad)

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** The credit 'art · kamo_nasus' is 11.5px muted grey (oklch 0.62) laid straight over bright foliage with no scrim, jammed against the Art menu button; at 1x it is close to invisible (on iPhone it sits on a darker patch and reads). The credit is a licensing obligation for hot-linked scenery (invariant 94).

## AA — Assistant, notifications, Archive (data, backups, storage, connections) (partial)

_Interrupted auditor: coverage notes were not written. Treat everything not listed below as unaudited._

### AA-01 · P1 · bug — 'Undoable' Assistant actions cannot be undone: the undo each tool returns is collected and never offered

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Permissions labels every reversible tool 'Undoable action' / 'Default · runs, undoable', and the Assistant runs them without asking on that promise. Each such tool hands back an undo ({label, run}); the orchestrator pushes it into req.undos and lastUndos, but no UI ever reads KOS.ai.orchestrator.lastUndos(): the 'Verified action complete' card has only text (no Undo), no toast offers one, and Activity is read-only. Reproduced with a stubbed Ollama returning todo_add_task: the reminder 'Revise BFS and DFS' was created, the card shows 'Add task / done / Target · #1 / todo_add_task' and the thread's only button is Copy. The only way back is to find and delete the record by hand.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-tool-add-mac.png; eval: buttons in .k-asst-thread = ['Copy'], state.reminders.items[0].title 'Revise BFS and DFS'. js/core/aitools.js:1919-1920 (undo returned); js/core/aiorchestrator.js:754 (req.undos.push), :451, :1046 (lastUndos exposed); grep finds no caller of lastUndos/undos outside aiorchestrator.js; js/modules/assistant.js:2518 ('runs, undoable')
- **Root cause:** Undo plumbing finished in the orchestrator but the presentation half was never built
- **Proposed fix:** Render an Undo button on each verified-action card (and a toast with Undo, as Day Review does) wired to that call's undo.run, marking the card and the audit row 'Undone'. Keep it available until the conversation changes.
- **Flags:** effort M

### AA-02 · P1 · bug — Reopened chat starts at the oldest message, and from then on new replies and approval cards land off-screen

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad, iPhone
- **Detail / repro:** The thread only follows the tail when it was already within 60px of the bottom. On first render the scroller is still detached, so render() sets scrollTop on a 0-height box and, once mounted, the thread shows the TOP of the conversation. Every later render measures 'not at bottom' and keeps scrollTop 0: the user's own sent message, Kurenai's reply and the 'Confirm & run / Decline' approval card all appear below the fold while the side panel says 'Waiting for your approval'. Reproduced: leave the Assistant and return (thread scrollTop 0 of 1110, client 617), send a message → scrollTop still 0, scrollHeight 1268; the approval card for todo_delete_task was in the DOM but not visible.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-approval-mac.png (approval pending, card not visible, thread at top); eval {reopen:{st:0,sh:1110,ch:617}, after send:{st:0,sh:1268}}. js/modules/assistant.js:1615-1623 (atBottom measured before the scroller is in the document; no scroll on own send or on a pending card)
- **Root cause:** atBottom computed on a detached node; no explicit scroll on send/approval
- **Proposed fix:** Scroll to the end after the first mount (requestAnimationFrame after insertion) and always when the user sends or a confirmation card appears; keep the 'only if at bottom' rule only for streamed additions, and show a 'New message ↓' pill when not following.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-03 · P2 · ui — Composer hint 'Shift + Enter for a new line' floats mid-height while the send button sits at the bottom

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad
- **Detail / repro:** The composer row is align-items:flex-end (send docks to the bottom edge) but the hint is align-self:center. With one line the three items already sit on three different centres (placeholder line ~759, hint 769, send 780 in a 60px textarea); as the textarea grows the hint drifts into the middle of the right side, detached from both the text and the send button. It is also shown on iPad (touch, often no hardware keyboard), where Shift+Enter means nothing.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/composer-multiline-crop.png (5 lines: hint y=729, send y=760..800, textarea 675..800); chat-mac.png; chat-ipad.png shows the hint on touch. css/views/assistant.css:214 (.k-asst-composer align-items:flex-end), :220 (.k-asst-hint align-self:center); js/modules/assistant.js:913 (hint omitted only when compact)
- **Root cause:** .k-asst-hint align-self:center inside a flex-end row
- **Proposed fix:** Give the hint align-self:flex-end with a margin-block-end that puts its centre on the send button's centre (or move it under the box as a small caption, left of the send). Hide it under (pointer:coarse) / when no hover, not only in compact mode.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-04 · P2 · bug — Opening a Morning/Evening review notification lands on Routines Today and never opens the review

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The review notice is pushed with view 'routines' and arg {review: slot}. KOS.notify.open() calls KOS.show('routines', {review:'morning'}), but KOS.views.routines only reads arg.edit or a tab string, so the arg is dropped and the page shows Routines › Today. The review sheet does not open; the user has to find the header chip. The same item reaches the bell, the Notifications page and closed-app pushes (sw notificationclick → kos-open → KOS.show), so all three dead-end.
- **Evidence:** eval: clicking the 'Morning review' row on #/notifications → location.hash '#/routines', no dialog open. js/core/dayreview.js:713 (view:'routines', arg:{review:slot}); js/core/notify.js:196-199 (open); js/modules/routines.js:469-473 (arg.review never read); grep finds no reader of arg.review anywhere.
- **Root cause:** Producer and consumer of the notification's arg disagree; no handler for {review}
- **Proposed fix:** In KOS.views.routines (or better, in notify.open for kind 'review') call KOS.reviewSheet.open(arg.review) after drawing Today, the way the header chip opens it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-05 · P2 · bug — History's 'Open Account & Cloud Sync' button opens the Collection's Sync & Import page, which has no sign-in

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Assistant › History, signed out, shows 'History is kept after cloud sign-in' with a button 'Open Account & Cloud Sync'. It calls KOS.show('mediasync'), the Collection's Sync & Import page (AniList, VNDB, Games…), which has no email/password fields. The sign-in card lives on Archive › Backup & Restore (#/data). The user lands on the wrong page with no way forward.
- **Evidence:** js/modules/assistant.js:2075 onclick KOS.show("mediasync"); eval KOS.show('mediasync') → headings 'Sync & Import, AniList, VNDB, Games…', no input[type=email]; the sign-in form is on #/data (shots/assistant-archive/data-mac.png).
- **Root cause:** Hard-coded wrong view id
- **Proposed fix:** KOS.show('data') and focus the account card's email field (one shared helper, e.g. KOS.cloudUI.openSignIn(), used by every 'sign in first' message).
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-06 · P2 · ux — Every 'sign in first' message points to 'Archive → Account & Cloud Sync', a page that does not exist, and offers no button

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The Assistant's failure card (signed out, the default Gemini route), Memory's empty state, cloud.js, gameapi.js and vndb.js all say 'Archive → Account & Cloud Sync'. The Archive has no page of that name: the strip reads Backup & Restore, Help & Guide, Notifications, Files, Storage, Pen and ink, Connections; the card is a section inside Backup & Restore. Connections calls it 'Open Backup & cloud'. None of the Assistant's messages is a link. A first-time user who types a question gets 'Kurenai couldn't finish that' with directions to a page they cannot find, while the status pill still says 'Kurenai is with you' with a green dot.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-send-signedout-mac.png; shots/assistant-archive/memory-mac.png; js/core/ai.js:152,154; js/modules/assistant.js:2299; js/core/cloud.js:142-143; js/core/gameapi.js:23,25; js/core/vndb.js:112,281; js/core/ui.js:430-438 (Archive strip names)
- **Root cause:** Navigation text duplicated in many string literals instead of one link helper; page renamed in review A
- **Proposed fix:** Name it as the UI does ('Archive › Backup & Restore › Account & Cloud Sync') and render an inline 'Sign in' action on the Assistant error card and the Memory/History empty states that opens #/data with the email field focused. Signed out with no Ollama model, show a one-line notice above the composer before the first send instead of failing after it.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-07 · P2 · bug — Activity (signed out) shows every action as 'Proposed … Started' forever, with no time

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Signed out, Activity reads the local audit queue but keeps only the 'insert' rows and drops every 'update' (executed/declined/failed), so an action that ran reads 'Proposed', 'todo_add_task · proposed' and '● Started' in one row. The queued rows have no created_at, so the 'when' column is an empty 100px gap at the row's left. The filters Ran / Declined / Failed never match anything signed out.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/activity-after-mac.png (after a todo_add_task that the chat reported complete). js/modules/assistant.js:2587-2590 (filter r.kind === 'insert'); js/core/aiorchestrator.js:232-250 (status changes are separate 'update' rows; the insert payload has no created_at)
- **Root cause:** Queue reader ignores update rows; insert payload lacks a timestamp
- **Proposed fix:** When reading the queue, fold each 'update' patch into its insert by id and use the queue entry's ts as created_at.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-08 · P3 · ui — Assistant's side status card prints the full error as an uppercase letter-spaced eyebrow, cut off mid-word

- **Devices:** Mac, iPad landscape
- **Detail / repro:** After a failed request the character panel's state label becomes 'FAILED: SIGN IN TO CLOUD SYNC FIRST (ARCHIVE → ACCOUNT & CLOUD SYNC) — THE ONLINE ASSIST…' in the 11px tracked uppercase eyebrow style across four lines and ellipsed. An eyebrow is for two-word labels; this is a sentence and is the same text the chat already shows in its error card.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-send-signedout-mac.png (bottom-right card); js/modules/assistant.js:724 setVisual('error', 'Failed: ' + clampText(p.message, 80))
- **Root cause:** State label fed the provider error text
- **Proposed fix:** Set the label to a short state ('Couldn't finish') and leave the message in the thread.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-09 · P3 · content — Verified-action card shows internals: raw tool id, 'done', and 'Target · #1'

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** After a tool runs, the card reads 'VERIFIED ACTION COMPLETE / Add task / done / Target · #1 / todo_add_task'. 'done' is the raw status, '#1' an internal record id, and 'todo_add_task' the function name. The useful fact (the reminder's title 'Revise BFS and DFS', where it went) is missing, and there is no link to open the reminder.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-tool-add-mac.png
- **Root cause:** Card renders the tool result envelope directly
- **Proposed fix:** Show the human result ("Added 'Revise BFS and DFS' to Reminders") with an Open link to the record and Undo; keep the tool id for Activity only.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AA-10 · P3 · ui — No space between consecutive blocks in an Assistant reply (table runs straight into the code block)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** In a reply with a table followed by a fenced code block, the table's bottom edge and the code block's top edge touch (both margins 0px), so the two read as one box.
- **Evidence:** shots/assistant-archive/chat-tool-add-mac.png; eval: table bottom 560.7 = codeblock top 560.7, margin-bottom/top 0px
- **Root cause:** Rich blocks (.k-asst-table, .k-asst-codeblock) carry no block spacing
- **Proposed fix:** Give the message body a flow gap (e.g. .k-asst-msg-body > * + * { margin-block-start: var(--sp-10) }) instead of per-element margins.
- **Flags:** effort S

## DI — Reliability and data integrity (store, merge, sync, IndexedDB, integrations logic) (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** Local-first persistence is careful in its stated paths (quota failures toast, boot is idempotent across reloads, the three-way merge and the CAS loop are sound), but four real data-loss or duplication paths remain. The first cloud sign-in discards local data in any domain that the hand-kept stateMeaningful() allowlist omits, such as reminders, assignments, exams and routines. Two tabs overwrite each other's whole-state saves. The Todoist sync duplicates reminders in two reproducible ways: another device syncing before the state document arrives, and a later command chunk failing after an earlier one created tasks. The root causes repeat across the codebase. Allowlists are maintained by hand and new domains were never added (stateMeaningful, cloudmerge RECORDS refs, the backup and its 'covers' copy, the Storage page). Remote link state is replicated asynchronously while the provider cursor is per device. Multi-request pushes keep all-or-nothing bookkeeping. Absence or a 404 is read as deletion. Every replicated store uses a strict now()-timestamp cursor. The single 373 KB (seeded) localStorage document has no fallback before the ~5 MB ceiling.

**Coverage.** Code review: js/core/ext.js, exttasks.js (all of Todoist and Google Tasks), extgcal.js (delete queue, 404 path), reminders.js (CRUD, complete, recurrence), store.js (load, save, flush, replaceState, snapshotFull/importFull), cloudsync.js (meaningful test, assessLink/adopt, applyRemoteState, pushState CAS), cloudmerge.js (RECORDS, re-keying, mergeValue), money.js syncCloud, the cursor pattern in every replicated store, and the touch trigger migration. Experiments in jsdom: exp/td.js (smoke112's fake Todoist, made more realistic: duplicates across two devices, chunk failure, full-sync sub-tasks, recurring completion, a mid-cycle replaceState, which did NOT lose the delete queue and was dropped) and exp/cs.js (smoke17's fake Supabase, the first-link discard). In the browser on slot 17: state-size measurement on the seeded and empty accounts, a localStorage quota-fill experiment, a two-tab clobber experiment (iframe), boot idempotency over two reloads, and screenshots of Connections, Storage and Backup & Restore (mac, ipad, iphone). Not examined: Google Calendar's recurrence mapping in depth (smoke112 D covers it), ink per-stroke merge, the mind/reports sync bodies beyond their cursor, real Todoist/Google servers (no live accounts; findings that depend on server behaviour are marked as such), and a full visual pass of the data pages in Dawn or the shop themes (left to the UI areas).

### DI-01 · P1 · data — Todoist: a reminder made on one device is duplicated when another device syncs before the state document arrives

- **Devices:** all (multi-device)
- **Detail / repro:** The lease serialises Todoist network work between devices, but the LINK (reminder.ext.id) lives in the synced state document, which reaches the other device later. Device A pushes a new reminder (item_add) and links it locally; device B takes the lease next (A releases it at once) while its state still holds the reminder unlinked and its own per-device sync token predates the add. B's incremental pull returns the new Todoist task, finds no local link (byLink) and reconcile() imports it as a SECOND reminder linked to the same Todoist id; the cloud merge then keeps both (different ids/uids). Reproduced in jsdom with the smoke112 fake: after the B-side cycle, two local reminders 'Made on A' are both linked to T3 while Todoist has one task. With a Mac, iPad and iPhone all open this is the ordinary case, matching the user's 'many problems between Reminders and Todoist'.
- **Evidence:** exp/td.js twodev → local 'Made on A': [[3,'T3'],[4,'T3']], remote: ['T3']; js/core/exttasks.js:180-187 (import with no matching attempt), js/core/ext.js:306-316 (syncProvider takes the lease without pulling the cloud state first), js/core/exttasks.js:229-237
- **Root cause:** Remote link state is replicated asynchronously while the remote cursor (sync token) is per device; import has no identity check beyond byLink.
- **Proposed fix:** Make the cycle state-consistent: before taking the lease run a cloud pull (KOS.cloudsync cycle) and push the state right after the cycle; and in reconcile's import path, before adding, look for an existing reminder already linked to that remote id (dedupe byLink after any merge) or an unlinked one in the same list whose uuidOf('add|'+uid) is pending (record pending adds {uid→temp} in state.ext so any device can recognise its own item_add). Also add a boot/merge pass that folds two reminders linked to the same ext.id.
- **Flags:** effort M

### DI-02 · P1 · data — Todoist: a failed second command batch leaves the first batch's tasks unlinked, so the next sync imports them as duplicates

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** tdPush sends commands in chunks of 90 and runs every 'ok' callback (which writes the link and base) only after ALL chunks succeed. If chunk 2 fails (HTTP 429/5xx, offline, tab suspended), chunk 1's item_add commands have already created tasks in Todoist but nothing local is linked, and the sync token is not saved either. The next cycle pulls those tasks with the old token, finds no local link and imports each as a new reminder; the originals are then re-sent as item_add. Linking a list with ~45+ reminders that have a sub-task each exceeds 90 commands on the first cycle. Reproduced: 60 reminders with one sub each, chunk 2 answered 503 → cycle 2 leaves 105 local reminders (45 duplicates).
- **Evidence:** exp/td.js chunk → 'cycle1 false Todoist answered 503 / remote items 45 local linked 0 / cycle2: local total 105 dup local 45'; js/core/exttasks.js:318-335 (ok callbacks only after the reduce over chunks resolves), js/core/exttasks.js:241-243 (token saved only after push)
- **Root cause:** Non-atomic multi-request push with all-or-nothing local bookkeeping.
- **Proposed fix:** Apply each chunk's results as soon as it returns (run the after[] entries whose commands are all in that chunk's sync_status), keep an item's parent and sub-task adds in one chunk, and persist the link/temp-id map before the next request; on the pull side, treat an incoming task whose id appears in a stored temp_id_mapping as already linked.
- **Flags:** effort M

### DI-03 · P1 · data — First cloud sign-in silently discards a device's reminders, assignments, exams, routines and other data that 'stateMeaningful' does not count

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** assessLink classifies each side with stateMeaningful(), which only looks at progress, sessions, custom cards, wishlist, tracker entries, goals, non-sample calendar events and Governor xp/gold/owned. A device whose data is reminders, assignments, Exams-hub assessments, routines, Pacing ticks, Money settings, curriculum edits, Code/Japanese/Mind/Creatives evidence or SRS state (without a logged session) counts as EMPTY, so linking it to an account that has data takes the 'remoteOnly' path: adoptRemote → applyRemoteState with no lastPushedHash → dirty = stateMeaningful(local) = false → KOS.store.replaceState(incoming). The local records are replaced without a merge, a toast or a backup. Reproduced in jsdom with smoke17's fake Supabase: a fresh device adds one reminder and one assignment, signs in to an account with history; after syncNow both are gone and the cloud copy never received them. A Home Screen install on the iPad has its own storage separate from Safari, so 'used it a bit before signing in' is a realistic path. The mirror case ('localOnly' when the remote only holds such records) overwrites the remote the same way.
- **Evidence:** exp/cs.js → {before:{rem:1,asg:1}, after:{rem:[],asg:[]}, cloudHasLocal:false}; js/core/cloudsync.js:234-251 (stateMeaningful), 975-990 (assessLink), 758-773 (not dirty → replaceState)
- **Root cause:** A hand-maintained allowlist of 'real data' that was not extended as new domains were added; replaceState used as the adopt path.
- **Proposed fix:** Define 'meaningful' as 'differs from a freshly booted DEFAULTS document' (hash syncableState against a boot-time pristine snapshot, ignoring known seeders such as SAMPLE events and pacing seed), or enumerate every record array in cloudmerge RECORDS plus the keyed maps; and on first link always take the no-base three-way merge (union) instead of replaceState when the local side has ANY record. Snapshot the local state into the media kv before any replaceState so it can be restored.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-04 · P1 · data — Two open tabs or windows overwrite each other's saves (last writer wins on the whole state)

- **Devices:** mac (two windows/tabs), iPad Safari with two tabs
- **Detail / repro:** Each tab keeps its own in-memory KOS.store.state and save() writes the WHOLE document to localStorage 120 ms later; nothing listens for the 'storage' event, and there is no BroadcastChannel or Web Lock. A change made in one tab is erased by the next save in the other. Reproduced on the slot with an iframe of index.html (same origin, same localStorage): tab B added 'TAB-B reminder' and saved; tab A then added its own reminder; the stored document holds A's and not B's, and tab A never learns of B's. Both tabs also mint the same nextId. With cloud sync the shared IndexedDB meta (seq, lastPushedHash, base) can let the stale tab's CAS succeed and upload a document without the other tab's record. On the MacBook a second window or a PWA window beside a tab is ordinary.
- **Evidence:** kos.mjs eval 17 (iframe experiment) → {afterBsaved:true, finalHasA:true, finalHasB:false, inMemA_hasB:false}; js/core/store.js:395-410 (whole-document save), no 'storage' listener anywhere in js/ (grep)
- **Root cause:** localStorage used as a single-writer store without cross-tab coordination.
- **Proposed fix:** Listen for 'storage' on the state key and, when another tab wrote, three-way merge the incoming document into memory with KOS.cloudmerge (base = last document this tab wrote/read) before the next save; or elect one writer tab with navigator.locks / BroadcastChannel and make other tabs read-only with a banner. At minimum detect a foreign write (compare a per-save nonce) and reload instead of overwriting.
- **Flags:** effort M

### DI-05 · P2 · data — Todoist: a full sync drops completed sub-tasks from the reminder (and can re-add them to Todoist as duplicates)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** When the linked-project signature changes (linking or unlinking any list) the cycle requests sync_token '*' and resets the mirror. Todoist's full sync returns only active items, so completed sub-tasks are absent from the mirror; the parent's remote subs then differ from base and the pull overwrites the local subs, deleting every ticked sub-task. If the local subs were also edited, local wins, and each local sub whose x is no longer in the mirror is sent again as item_add, duplicating it in Todoist. Reproduced with a fake that, like Todoist, omits checked items from a full sync: 'Plan' (ticked) vanished locally after linking a second list. Depends on Todoist excluding completed sub-items from a full sync (documented behaviour for completed tasks).
- **Evidence:** exp/td.js subs → 'after remote sub tick: [[Plan,true],[Draft,false]]' then 'after full sync: [[Draft,false]]'; js/core/exttasks.js:217-220 (token '*' on any link change, mirror reset), 108-113 (childrenOf reads only the mirror), 296-307 (missing x → item_add)
- **Root cause:** Absence in a full sync is interpreted as deletion.
- **Proposed fix:** Do not reset the mirror on a full sync triggered by a link change; only drop entries of projects no longer linked. Treat a sub missing from a full sync as 'unknown', not deleted (keep subs whose remote child is not returned unless is_deleted arrives), and never re-add a sub that has an x.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-06 · P2 · integration — Todoist: completing a repeating reminder here sends a new due string instead of closing the occurrence

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** KOS.reminders.complete() rolls a repeating reminder forward locally (new due, done=false) so the push sees only a 'when' change and sends item_update with due {date: next, string: 'every week', is_recurring}. Todoist never records the completion (no history/karma, completed view empty), and since Todoist parses due.string, the explicit date may be ignored and re-anchored to today, which the next pull brings back. For a Todoist-only rule (ext.rs, e.g. 'every mon, fri') the reminder instead closes locally (recur is null here) and sends item_close, so it shows as completed until the next pull un-completes it with Todoist's new date: two different behaviours for the same action.
- **Evidence:** exp/td.js recur → cmds [[{type:item_update,args:{id:T1,due:{date:'2026-11-05',string:'every week',is_recurring:true}}}]]; gym: local done=true, cmds [[item_close]]; js/core/reminders.js:229-236, js/core/exttasks.js:99-107, 282-290
- **Root cause:** Local recurrence semantics duplicated instead of delegated to the provider that owns the rule.
- **Proposed fix:** For a linked repeating reminder, push item_close (Todoist rolls the date itself) and let the pull bring the new date back; treat ext.rs rules as repeating in complete() (roll forward with Todoist as the source) so both paths behave alike.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-07 · P2 · data — An assignment re-keyed by the cloud merge leaves its goals, Exams links, Focus sessions and review picks pointing at another assignment

- **Devices:** multi-device
- **Detail / repro:** Ids are per-device counters; when two devices mint the same assignment id between syncs, rekeyCollisions re-keys the local one and rewrites only RECORDS[].refs. For assignments.items that list is calendar.events.assignmentId alone, but assignment ids are also stored in goals (links.assignmentId, js/core/goals.js:130), Exams assessments (links.assignmentId, js/core/exams.js:178, 536, 726), session entries and their segments (metrics.assignmentId, js/core/sessions.js:93-98; Focus banks minutes by it), and Daily Review picks (js/core/dayreview.js:489). After a re-key those references silently follow the OTHER device's assignment: minutes, goal progress and an exam's linked coursework attach to the wrong record. Reminders have the same gap (reminder ids referenced by the review picks).
- **Evidence:** js/core/cloudmerge.js:57-58 (assignments refs: calendar.events only), 293-346 (rekeyCollisions rewrites only spec.refs); references listed above
- **Root cause:** Counter ids plus a hand-maintained reference list that new features did not extend.
- **Proposed fix:** Add every reference to RECORDS[].refs (goals.items links.assignmentId as a nested field, exams.assessments links.assignmentId, sessions metrics.assignmentId and metrics.segments[].assignmentId, dayReview picks), or move assignments/reminders/events to random string ids as Money, Routines and Exams already do (the long-term fix).
- **Flags:** effort M

### DI-08 · P2 · data — A 404 on an update deletes the local event or reminder (e.g. after reconnecting a different Google account)

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** When a PATCH to Google Calendar or Google Tasks answers 404/410, the cycle deletes the LOCAL record inside inbound(): extgcal.js:421 KOS.calendar.deleteEvent(ev.id), exttasks.js:417 KOS.reminders.remove(it.id). disconnect() (ext.js:241-250) clears only the kv (sync tokens, mirrors) and leaves every record's ext link in place, so connecting a different Google account (or the event being moved to another calendar) turns the next local edit of any linked event or reminder into a silent local deletion, with no undo and no notice. This contradicts the 'never inferred from absence' intent of invariant 158 and is the opposite of Todoist's behaviour (an update to a missing item there is just retried forever, exttasks.js:330-333).
- **Evidence:** js/core/extgcal.js:419-421, js/core/exttasks.js:416-417, js/core/ext.js:241-250
- **Root cause:** A failed write is treated as proof of a remote deletion.
- **Proposed fix:** On 404 for an update, unlink the record (ext = null, keep it local, count it as 'unlinked' in the status) instead of deleting it; delete locally only from a pull that reports the item cancelled/deleted. Clear (or archive) ext links on disconnect and on connecting a different account (compare the account id).
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-09 · P2 · data — The full backup leaves out saved Reports and snapshots, and builds one giant string that can fail on the iPad

- **Devices:** ipad, iphone
- **Detail / repro:** store.snapshotFull collects state, media, attachments (kurenai-os-files, every binary as base64), money, reviews, mind, ink and creatives, but not kurenai-os-reports (saved weeks/months/terms/years, their Kurenai words and the boundary snapshots that status moves need — 'never recomputed', invariant 101/102). Signed out, a restore on a new device loses every saved report and snapshot for good. The backup is serialised with JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) into a single Blob: with a few hundred MB of files (the app asks for persistent storage at 200 MB) the base64 plus indentation is a string of several hundred MB, which risks an out-of-memory reload on iPad Safari, and that failure is silent (no error reaches done()). A failing sub-export (e.g. creatives) aborts the whole backup instead of reporting the missing part.
- **Evidence:** js/core/store.js:513-583 (no reportStore step; JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) at 588), js/core/reportstore.js (no exportAll), js/modules/attachments.js:454-480 (base64 for every blob)
- **Root cause:** Backup coverage list maintained by hand; whole-document serialisation.
- **Proposed fix:** Add KOS.reportStore.exportAll/importAll to the snapshot; stream the backup (build the Blob from parts: a JSON head plus one part per file, no indentation) or offer 'backup without files' when files exceed ~100 MB; report per-section failures like importFull does instead of failing the whole export.
- **Flags:** effort M

### DI-10 · P2 · data — The whole state lives in one localStorage key with no fallback: when it outgrows the quota every later change is lost on reload

- **Devices:** all, iPhone/iPad Safari first
- **Detail / repro:** Measured on the seeded account: the state document is 372,924 characters in one key (kurenai-os-v1); srs is 110 KB for 675 rated cards (164 B a card), sessions 84 KB for 600 (140 B each, capped at 2,000), pacing 85 KB. The curriculum alone has 5,436 flashcards, so a fully reviewed deck is ~0.9 MB before Code, Japanese facets and Mind cards, and every new domain adds to the same document. Chrome refused the write at ~5.2 M characters total; WebKit's limit is of the same order. When setItem throws, save() shows one toast ('Save failed — storage may be full. Export your data.') and keeps running on the in-memory copy; every later change, and the pagehide flush (which only logs, store.js:417-425), is lost on the next reload or when iOS evicts the page. Reproduced by filling localStorage on the slot: the save failed with QuotaExceededError and the change was not stored. There is no size readout in Archive › Storage for the state document, and no migration of the big parts (srs, sessions, facets) to IndexedDB.
- **Evidence:** kos.mjs eval 17 → state 372,924 chars; srs 110,391; sessions 84,052; pacing 84,696; 5,436 curriculum cards; quota experiment → errs ['Kurenai OS: save failed QuotaExceededError…'], stored:false, toast 'Save failed — storage may be full. Export your data.'; js/core/store.js:395-425
- **Root cause:** Single-key localStorage persistence for an unbounded document.
- **Proposed fix:** Move the large, append-heavy parts (srs/cards, the session ledger, Japanese facets, Mind cards) into IndexedDB records like the review log, or store the document in IndexedDB with localStorage as a small boot cache; show the state size in Archive › Storage with a warning at 60% of quota; on a failed save keep a persistent banner (not a toast) and retry into IndexedDB.
- **Flags:** effort L

### DI-11 · P2 · data — Every replicated store pulls with a strict updated_at > cursor on now() stamps, so a row committed late is skipped forever

- **Devices:** multi-device
- **Detail / repro:** Media, files, money, reviews, mind, ink, reports and push all pull 'updated_at > cursor' and then advance the cursor to the largest updated_at seen. The touch trigger stamps updated_at := now(), which in Postgres is the TRANSACTION START time. If device A's upsert (a 500-row media chunk, a money batch) starts at t1 and commits after device B's quicker write stamped t2 > t1, a third device that pulls between the two commits sets its cursor to t2 and never fetches A's rows (t1 < t2). Nothing ever re-reads them: the pulling device silently lacks those transactions/reviews/ink strokes until a restore. With three devices syncing on focus this is a real, if rare, divergence with no symptom.
- **Evidence:** supabase/migrations/20260716000001_kos_sync_init.sql:26-35 (now()); q.gt('updated_at', …) at js/core/money.js:1126, reviewlog.js:133, mindjournal.js:191, inksync.js:97, reportstore.js:324, cloudsync.js:358/376; cursor = max seen (money.js:1189, 1207)
- **Root cause:** Timestamp cursor over transaction-start timestamps.
- **Proposed fix:** Stamp with clock_timestamp() in the trigger and pull with an overlap window (gt cursor − 2 minutes) — every store already skips its own echo by stamp or uid, so re-reading a few rows is harmless; or use a monotonic bigserial 'version' column per table as the cursor.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-12 · P3 · integration — Todoist: a command the server rejects is resent on every cycle forever, with no status shown

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** tdPush applies a record's bookkeeping only when every command in its group returns 'ok'. A rejected command (item_update or item_delete for a task deleted or moved out of reach in Todoist, an invalid label, a due string Todoist cannot parse) leaves base and the delete queue unchanged, so the same command is sent every cycle (every few minutes and on every focus) and the Connections status still says the cycle succeeded, because sync_status errors are never turned into counts or an error. A partly-applied group (item_update ok, item_close rejected) also re-sends the parts that did apply.
- **Evidence:** js/core/exttasks.js:329-334 (only the all-ok case is handled; errors in r.sync_status are dropped), js/core/exttasks.js:256 (delete queue entry kept on error)
- **Root cause:** Error results of batched commands are ignored.
- **Proposed fix:** Read sync_status errors: for 'not found' (error_code 22) drop the queue entry or unlink the record; for other errors record them in counts and setStatus so Connections shows 'n changes refused' with the reason; apply per-command bookkeeping rather than per group.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-13 · P3 · data — An unreadable stored state is replaced by a fresh one and overwritten on the next save

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** At boot, if JSON.parse or deepMerge of kurenai-os-v1 throws, the app logs a warning and starts from DEFAULTS; the next save() (any render that creates a progress record, the boot seeders) writes the empty state over the unreadable original, so whatever was recoverable from it is gone and the user is not told. With cloud sync the empty-local guard then adopts the cloud copy, but a signed-out user loses everything silently.
- **Evidence:** js/core/store.js:382-390 (catch → DEFAULTS, console.warn only), 393 and 395-410 (save overwrites KEY)
- **Root cause:** No quarantine of the original before the first overwrite.
- **Proposed fix:** On a parse failure copy the raw string to a side key (kurenai-os-v1.corrupt-<ts>) or into IndexedDB before anything saves, and show a blocking notice offering Restore from backup / cloud.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-14 · P3 · content — Backup & Restore's 'What a backup covers' list is out of date: half of what the file holds, and everything it leaves out, is unlisted

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** The list names study, productivity, Governor, the media vault, the purchase planner and attachments. The file also carries Money transactions, the review log, handwriting, Mind's Journal and Creatives work, none of which is listed; and it does NOT carry saved Reports and snapshots, the Paper library's own PDFs (unless kept in the cloud), Japanese saved lines, or the Google/Todoist sign-ins (records stay linked but need reconnecting) — the reader is not told. The closing line points only to 'Sync & Import' for reconnecting, not Connections.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/data-integrity/data-mac.png; list text read via eval; js/core/store.js:513-583 (what snapshotFull includes)
- **Root cause:** Hand-written copy that did not follow the backup's growth.
- **Proposed fix:** Generate the rows from the same table snapshotFull uses (one row per store with its live count), and add a short 'Not in the file' line naming Reports, the Paper library and connections.
- **Flags:** effort S

### DI-15 · P3 · ui — Archive › Storage accounts for none of the app's real data and prints rows of 0 KB

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** On the seeded account (1,880 media entries, 373 KB state document, money, reviews) 'On this device' says 48.4 MB used while 'By kind of data' lists only Files, Past papers, Your ink and Text index, each '0 KB' — supporting zeros that invariant 77 says to suppress — and never shows the state document (the one store with a hard ~5 MB ceiling), the media vault, Money, the review log, Mind or Creatives. The numbers cannot explain the total. On the Mac the last card ('Not linked anywhere') sits alone in a row with two empty columns beside it.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/data-integrity/storage-mac.png, audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/shots/data-integrity/storage-iphone.png
- **Root cause:** Storage page written for Files R4 only.
- **Proposed fix:** List every store (state document with its share of the localStorage quota, media vault, money, reviews, ink, mind, creatives, reports, files, papers, text index) with sizes from navigator.storage.estimate().usageDetails or a count × average, hide zero rows, and let the last card span the row.
- **Flags:** effort S

**QoL ideas from this auditor:**
- Show the state document's size and its share of the ~5 MB localStorage quota in Archive › Storage.
- Connections: show the last cycle's refused commands and duplicate links, with a 'Fold duplicates' action.
- After a restore or first link, toast what was merged or replaced, naming the counts per domain.
- Add a 'Download a copy of this device first' button to the first-link dialog.

**Backlog ideas from this auditor:**
- Move the state document (or its big parts: srs, sessions, facets) from localStorage to IndexedDB with per-record writes and cross-tab coordination (BroadcastChannel or Web Locks).
- Replace per-device counter ids for assignments, reminders and events with random string ids, as Money, Routines and Exams already use, retiring rekeyCollisions.
- Make provider sync state-consistent: pull and push the cloud state around each leased cycle, and keep pending provider adds in synced state so any device recognises them.
- Use a monotonic version column (bigserial) as the replication cursor for every kos_* table.


---

## AP — Architecture, maintainability, performance, docs drift (complete)

**Auditor's summary.** The codebase is a coherent script-tag app with strong written contracts (invariants, pure derive-on-read models, 112 passing smoke suites), but it has three structural costs. (1) Boot loads ~16.6 MB of JS in 340 synchronous scripts plus 1.2 MB of CSS, mostly curriculum content, so a cold launch spends seconds compiling and running content builders (15 s to domInteractive on the empty account, shell ready at 21 s on the dense one, on a loaded test machine). (2) The derived reads have no memoisation, so the first render of heavy views takes 0.5-2.8 s (Exams readiness, the Code map's unitState loop, the Calendar's per-cell derivedOn), settling to 20-350 ms. (3) There is no view lifecycle: KOS.show empties #main with no unmount, so 15 lazily removed global listeners keep detached pages alive (about 16,000 nodes after 30 topic pages), and KOS.rerender drops keyboard focus to <body>, breaking invariant 65. Maintainability: hub.js (3,899 lines, a 953-line topic-page function) and aitools.js are grab bags; the four study spaces copy the same lesson reader and evidence/award code and have already drifted; there is dead CSS (the retired Habits page). Docs disagree with each other about the current release and gate.

**Coverage.** Measured in harness slot 18 at Mac size on the empty and seeded accounts: boot (Performance API, long tasks, CDP CPU profiles of reloads: arch/prof-boot-empty.txt, arch/prof-boot-dense.txt), first and warm render times of 25 views, CPU profiles of Home, Code and Calendar, an API-level timing wrapper, store.save per navigation, state size (368 KB) and stringify cost, memory and listener growth with forced GC (arch/mem.txt, arch/mem2.txt), rerender focus retention on 5 views, and idle rerender frequency (none in 65 s). Static: boot payload from index.html, file sizes, giant files, KOS namespace/guards, study-space duplication (diff of readers), unused CSS scan, unused KOS namespaces, duplicated helpers, repo root and tracked files (repo is public), docs drift across README/AGENTS/PROGRESS/HANDOFF/GUIDE/CLAUDE.md/in-app Help, and the whole smoke gate run (112 suites, all pass, 849 s). Not done: iPad/iPhone-specific timing (the harness is desktop Chrome; Safari/WebKit and real-device JIT differ, so absolute numbers are indicative only, and the test machine was shared with other slots at load ~5); Dawn/shop themes and visual screenshots (not relevant to this area; other auditors cover pages); Edge Functions and supabase code; Achievements and the art shop, which are not in this checkout.

> **Build audited:** f4fa493 (sw kos-creatives-1), 23 commits before production: no Achievements, no art/shop release. Structural findings (boot cost, hub.js, duplication, listeners, docs drift) almost certainly still hold; re-check sizes, counts and dead-CSS lists on main before acting.

### AP-01 · P1 · perf — Boot loads ~16.6 MB of JavaScript in 340 synchronous script tags, most of it curriculum content no first view needs

- **Devices:** all; worst on iPhone 17 and iPad (cold launch of the Home Screen app)
- **Detail / repro:** index.html lists 340 <script src> tags; only KaTeX, supabase.js and env.local.js are deferred. Summed on disk: 16.64 MB JS (js/data/content 8.28 MB in 126 files, js/modules 3.33 MB, js/core 2.50 MB, js/data 1.67 MB, js/labs 0.60 MB) plus 1.21 MB CSS in 24 sheets. Every topic's notes for every subject (CS, Maths, IT, Code units 1-12, tracks, Japanese, Mind, Creatives), the 567 KB past-paper index, and the 383 KB Rider project scaffolds (code-projects-files.js, needed only when exporting a Rider zip) are parsed and executed before Home renders. In the harness a cold reload of the EMPTY account took 15.0 s to domInteractive with 61 long tasks totalling 15.4 s (max 2.75 s); the CPU profile shows 11 s of (program) (parse/compile) and the top self-time scripts are content files executing their builders (cs-p2-46b.js 746 ms, code.js lesson() 316 ms, cs-p2-411.js 545 ms, cs-p2-410a.js 497 ms, maths-stats-s4 325 ms...). The machine was shared with other audit slots so absolute times are inflated, but the shape is unambiguous: the content payload dominates boot, and on an iPad/iPhone (slower JS, tighter memory, Safari evicting a backgrounded Home Screen app) every cold launch pays it. Heap after boot on the empty account: 68 MB.
- **Evidence:** audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/arch/prof-boot-empty.txt; index.html lines 48-55 (only defer tags) and the 340-script list; sizes via stat: exam-index.js 566,954 B, code-projects-files.js 382,808 B, it-f204.js 299,469 B, hub.js 232,148 B, aitools.js 208,176 B; Performance API: domInteractive 14,993 ms, longtasks 61 / 15,441 ms; dense account warm reload (SW controlling, V8 code cache): data-shell=ready at 21,064 ms, 35 long tasks totalling 19,166 ms, heap 58 MB (machine shared with other audit slots, load avg ~5)
- **Root cause:** Classic script-tag design (chosen so file:// works) with no lazy-loading layer; every new study space appended its whole content to index.html.
- **Proposed fix:** Keep the script-tag architecture but split content into lazily loaded bundles: KOS.content.get(key) already funnels every read, so make content files register a loader per subject/unit (a tiny manifest listing keys -> file) and inject the script on first open of a topic, a desk or search (search can use a prebuilt title/keyword index). Load exam-index.js only on #/exams, code-projects-files.js only on export, ja/mind/creatives content only when their desk opens. Target: boot payload under ~4 MB. Also add performance.mark('kos-boot-start'/'kos-interactive') in main.js so the cost is tracked by a smoke test budget.
- **Flags:** effort L

### AP-03 · P2 · perf — Cold-path cost after boot: the first render of each heavy view takes 0.5-2.8 s on the dense account (Exams, Code map, Due, Pacing, Calendar), then 20-200 ms

- **Devices:** all; most felt on iPhone 17 and iPad cold launches
- **Detail / repro:** Rendering each view three times in a row (via a cheap view in between) on the seeded account gave: exams 2750/589/179 ms, code map 1622/504/355, due 1623/263/22, pacing 1405/51/120, calendar 1015/168/189, japanese 825/59/311, routines 705/11/16, mind 670/171/126, home 205/175/63 (first ever Home after boot was 2.0-3.3 s). Steady state is acceptable; the first visit after a cold launch is not, and on an iPad/iPhone Home Screen app every relaunch after Safari evicts it is a cold launch. Two causes: (1) V8 lazily compiles functions from the 16.6 MB of eagerly loaded script on first use, and (2) the derived reads have no per-render snapshot, so first calls are expensive and repeated: KOS.exams.readiness over 17 assessments measured 3,247 ms then 696/78/55 ms on later runs; KOS.code.unitState (js/core/code.js:888) rescans every lesson and calls stats() (code.js:856) per call, and the Code map (js/modules/code.js:417-490) calls it per unit, per track need (needMet) and per track section (trackState); evidence() re-splits shipped notes with KOS.content.splitPages on every call (code.js:168, 66 ms/call measured). The Calendar month calls dayItems -> derivedOn (37 ms/call warm, calendar.js:563) for each of 35-42 cells, re-reading every Money rule, goal and exam start-by per cell.
- **Evidence:** eval timings above (slot 18, seeded); audit-evidence/2026-11/qa/arch/prof-boot-dense.txt (readiness js/core/exams.js:635 1314 ms self in the boot profile, exams.js:945 predictPass 893 ms), arch/prof-code.txt, arch/prof-calendar.txt; js/core/code.js:166-190, 856-895; js/modules/code.js:417-490; js/modules/calendar.js:546-570, 1762-1800; js/core/exams.js:635-720, 943-960
- **Root cause:** Pure derive-on-read design (a good contract) without any memoisation layer, so composite pages multiply the cost; plus eager loading of all code.
- **Proposed fix:** Add a cheap per-tick memo: a KOS.store revision counter bumped by save(), and memoise the heavy pure reads (exams.readiness/leaves, code stats/unitState/pageCount, calendar derivedOn for a month range, srs.allCards) on (args, revision, day). Shipped content is immutable at runtime (invariant 86), so pageCount/listingsOf can be cached forever. For the calendar, compute derived items for the visible range once and bucket by date. Pair with lazy content loading (boot finding) to cut compile cost.
- **Flags:** effort M

### AP-04 · P2 · arch — The four study spaces each re-implement the same lesson machinery (paged reader with dwell, evidence, syncChecks, award, planDay), and the copies have already drifted

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** Code, Japanese, Mind and Creatives each have their own core model (js/core/code.js 942 lines, ja.js 409, mind.js 633, creatives.js 483), topic module (codetopic.js 718, jatopic.js 387, mindtopic.js 442, crtopic.js 266), desk module and UI helper (codeui/jaui/mindui), 9,644 lines in all. Every core defines its own evidence(), syncChecks(), award(), planDay() and normalise(); every topic module carries its own copy of the paged notes reader with DWELL = 2500 / LEAVE = 1500, a page select, dots, markRead and paint (codetopic.js:26-27/354-397, jatopic.js:22/215-244, mindtopic.js:19/271-302, crtopic.js:16/163). A diff of the Japanese and Mind readers (48 lines each) shows 42 changed lines for the same behaviour, with drift a user can see: Japanese labels the pager 'Note pages' and hides it for one-page lessons, Mind labels it 'Lesson pages' and always shows it; Mind's markRead skips a 'Your notes' page, Japanese's has no such guard. The read-dwell rule is invariant 107's, so four copies mean four places to keep in step, and the next study space (roadmap) will add a fifth.
- **Evidence:** grep 'var DWELL' in js/modules/{codetopic,jatopic,mindtopic,crtopic}.js; diff of jatopic.js:213-260 vs mindtopic.js:269-316 (42 differing lines); grep 'function syncChecks|function award|function planDay|function evidence' finds one per space in js/core/{code,ja,mind,creatives}.js
- **Root cause:** Each study space was built in its own release branch by copying the previous one (comments say 'the pager as Code's and Japanese's'), with no shared component extracted afterwards.
- **Proposed fix:** Extract a shared KOS.lessonReader({pages, peekRead, markPage, onChanged, label}) into js/core/ (the pager, dots, dwell/leave timing, 'Your notes' guard, Next/Prev) and have the four topic modules mount it; then a small KOS.studySpace helper for the checks-sync pattern (evidence -> want[] -> store.setCheck diff) that each core calls with its own evidence(). Do it before the next study space rather than as a rewrite: start with the reader, which has the visible drift.
- **Flags:** effort M

### AP-05 · P2 · docs — README, AGENTS, PROGRESS and HANDOFF each name a different 'current' release and smoke gate; Mind and Creatives are still listed as planned

- **Devices:** n/a
- **Detail / repro:** In this tree (sw.js kos-creatives-1, 112 smoke suites): README.md:19-21 and PROGRESS.md:22-23 say the current release is kos-files-1 (26 Oct); docs/HANDOFF.md:48 says 'Live: sw kos-japanese-5'; CLAUDE.md:163 says kos-creatives-1; origin/main is kos-artshop-1. README.md:33 and AGENTS.md:145 say the gate is 106 suites, PROGRESS.md:24 '106 / 106', CLAUDE.md:164 '112 / 112' (and the main-branch CLAUDE.md 113). README.md:35-37 lists 'Creatives, Mind, and an art and gold shop audit' as in flight/planned though Mind (kos-mind-1) and Creatives (kos-creatives-1) shipped; docs/GUIDE.md:424-425 puts both under '5. What is still on the way'. Google Calendar, Google Tasks and Todoist (kos-connect-1) are not mentioned in README, AGENTS, PROGRESS or GUIDE at all. CLAUDE.md itself is 2,337 lines, and its 'Current baseline' bullet is a ~150-line chronological release diary that CLAUDE.md's own first paragraph says belongs in Git; AGENTS.md (202 lines) is called 'the concise execution contract' but has none of invariants 137-163. Inside CLAUDE.md, the 'Branches' bullet stops at 26 October ('then to feat/routines') and 'In flight' still names branch feat/japanese and Stage 1 unit F2 as the latest; the ownership table has two separate 'Files' rows (R1-R4 and R0).
- **Evidence:** grep -n 'kos-' README.md PROGRESS.md docs/HANDOFF.md AGENTS.md; grep -n 'suites' README.md AGENTS.md PROGRESS.md CLAUDE.md; README.md:30-38; docs/GUIDE.md:418-426; grep -c 'Todoist|Google Calendar' README.md AGENTS.md docs/GUIDE.md PROGRESS.md = 0
- **Root cause:** Each release updates CLAUDE.md (and sometimes memory) but not the other status docs; the same facts are restated in five files with no check.
- **Proposed fix:** Make one file the single source of 'current release, sw version, gate count' (PROGRESS.md's table) and have README/AGENTS/HANDOFF link to it instead of restating; add a smoke check that sw.js VERSION, the highest smoke number and PROGRESS.md's table agree. Move CLAUDE.md's release diary into PROGRESS.md (or git tags) and keep CLAUDE.md to architecture, ownership and invariants. Update GUIDE §5 and README's feature list for Mind, Creatives and the integrations.
- **Flags:** effort S

### AP-06 · P2 · content — In-app Help & Guide has no entry for Japanese, Mind, Creatives, Money, Goals or the Google/Todoist connections

- **Devices:** all
- **Detail / repro:** js/modules/help.js (335 lines) is the Help & Guide page that invariant 50a points readers to ('rules a reader needs once belong in Help & Guide'), and cloudui.js:101 defers to it. Counting mentions: Japanese 0, Mind 0, Creatives 0, Money 0, Todoist 0, Goals 1, Google 1, Routine 5, Files 6. So the study spaces and Money/Goals, which deliberately keep page sub-lines to one line, have no longer explanation anywhere in the app (docs/GUIDE.md covers Japanese and Code but is not shipped in the app).

- **Root cause:** Help content is hand-written per release and was not extended after the Files release.
- **Proposed fix:** Add Help & Guide sections for each study space (how a lesson counts as done, what pays, the Daily plan), Money (Hide amounts, recurring rules, the planner mirror), Goals (repeating goals, pace) and Connections; reuse docs/GUIDE.md's plain-words text as the source.

### AP-07 · P2 · arch — The public repository tracks personal data and one-off clutter at its root (an AniList/MAL export, local Claude settings, spec PDFs, repair scripts, old plans)

- **Devices:** n/a
- **Detail / repro:** gh reports crimsoncodes7/KurenaiOS as PUBLIC. Tracked in it: 'Phase 3 Context/scrape_anilistanime.xml' (the user's MyAnimeList export with user_name and the full 650-title list) and four app screenshots; .claude/settings.local.json and .claude/launch.json (per-machine Claude Code permissions; no secret found in it, but it is local configuration and shows as modified in every session); Context/ (12 files, 7.7 MB: AQA/Edexcel/OCR specification PDFs, a revision-guide .docx, 'Requirements1-5.png', a .gdoc stub); five root repair scripts from the pre-2.1 snapshot (fix_damage.py, fix_damage_2.py, fix_it_data.py, fix_it_data_2.py, fix_structural_corruption.py, last touched a29a716 2026-06-13, referenced by nothing); and four root planning/audit docs from earlier phases (CATEGORY6_PLAN.md, CATEGORY6_UI_PLAN.md, CATEGORY7_PHASE_G_FINDINGS.md, KURENAIOS_FULL_UI_UX_AUDIT.md). The root has 9 markdown files, which makes the canonical four (README, CLAUDE, AGENTS, PROGRESS) harder to find.
